### [1] [A generalization of the Powers-Størmer-Ogata inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16184)
*Markus B. Fröb*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 论文建立了关于冯诺依曼代数上正规正线性泛函的算子单调函数不等式，推广了Powers-Størmer-Ogata不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Powers-Størmer-Ogata不等式是算子代数中的重要结果，作者希望将其推广到更一般的算子单调函数框架下，以揭示其本质结构与适用范围。

Method: 对任意正算子单调函数f，利用相对模算子Δ_{φ,ψ}及谱测度E^{Δ_{φ,ψ}}，建立关于φ、ψ∈M_{*,+}的积分不等式，并通过选取f(t)=t^s (s∈[0,1]) 作为特例来验证。

Result: 证明了不等式 ∫f(t)d‖E^{Δ_{φ,ψ}}(t)ξ_ψ‖² + f'_∞ φ(1−s(ψ)) ≥ f(1)/2 (φ(1)+ψ(1)−‖φ−ψ‖)；取f(t)=t^s时即恢复Powers-Størmer-Ogata不等式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Powers-Størmer-Ogata不等式的一个统一推广，表明其可视为更一般的算子单调函数不等式之特例。

Abstract: We show that for any positive operator monotone function $f$ the inequality $\int_{[0,\infty)} f(t) \, \mathrm{d} \left\lVert E^{Δ_{\varphi,ψ}}(t) ξ_ψ\right\rVert^2 + f'_\infty \varphi(1-s(ψ)) \geq \frac{f(1)}{2} \Bigl( \varphi(1) + ψ(1) - \lVert \varphi - ψ\rVert \Bigr)$ holds, where $\varphi, ψ\in \mathcal{M}_{*,+}$ are two normal positive linear functionals on a von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$, and $Δ_{\varphi,ψ}$ is the associated relative modular operator. Choosing $f(t) = t^s$ with $s \in [0,1]$, the Powers-Størmer-Ogata inequality is recovered.

</details>


### [2] [Sharp tangent inequalities in noncommutative $L_p$-spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16202)
*Zongben Xu,Teng Zhang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文在Haagerup非交换L_p空间中建立了尖锐的切向不等式：对1<p≤2有‖x+y‖_p²≥‖x‖_p²+2⟨J_p(x),y⟩+(p-1)‖y‖_p²，反向不等式对2≤p<∞成立，常数p-1最优，并推广了Xu、Ricard–Xu及Ball–Carlen–Lieb的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xu的切向不等式此前仅限于经典L_p空间，而Ricard–Xu的凸性不等式及Ball–Carlen–Lieb凸性不等式缺乏其尖锐切向形式。作者希望将切向不等式推广到一般非交换L_p空间（Haagerup空间），并给出相应凸性不等式的切向刻画。

Method: 利用忠实正规半有限权下的Haagerup非交换L_p空间、典范实对偶配对⟨z,y⟩=Re Tr(z*y)以及归一化对偶映射J_p(x)=‖x‖_p^{2-p}u|x|^{p-1}，通过分析范数平方的切向展开并验证常数最优性来证明不等式，并针对Schatten p-类及0<p<1情形给出相应结论。

Result: 证明了尖锐切向不等式‖x+y‖_p²≥‖x‖_p²+2⟨J_p(x),y⟩+(p-1)‖y‖_p²（1<p≤2），反向不等式在2≤p<∞成立，常数p-1最优；该结果推广了Xu的切向不等式，给出Ricard–Xu凸性不等式的尖锐切向形式，并在Schatten p-类上得到Ball–Carlen–Lieb凸性不等式的对应切向形式。

Conclusion: 在适当正性假设下，进一步为Schatten p-类在0<p<1范围内建立了Xu切向不等式的尖锐补充形式，完善了非交换L_p空间中的切向不等式理论。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{M}$ be a von Neumann algebra equipped with a faithful normal semifinite weight, and let $L_p(\mathcal{M})$ denote the associated Haagerup noncommutative $L_p$-space. For $1<p<\infty$, let $q=p/(p-1)$. For $z\in L_q(\mathcal{M})$ and $y\in L_p(\mathcal{M})$, denote by $\langle z,y\rangle=\operatorname{Re}\operatorname{Tr}(z^*y)$ the canonical real duality pairing between $L_q(\mathcal{M})$ and $L_p(\mathcal{M})$. Define the normalized duality map by $J_p(x)=\|x\|_p^{\,2-p}u|x|^{p-1}$ for $x=u|x|\neq0$, and set $J_p(0)=0$. We establish the sharp tangent inequality $$\|x+y\|_p^2\ge\|x\|_p^2+2\langle J_p(x),y\rangle+(p-1)\|y\|_p^2,\qquad x,y\in L_p(\mathcal{M}),\quad 1<p\le2.$$ The reverse inequality holds for $2\le p<\infty$, and the constant $p-1$ is optimal. Our inequality extends Xu's tangent inequality \cite{Xu89} from classical $L_p$-spaces to general noncommutative $L_p$-spaces and provides the sharp tangent formulation of the Ricard--Xu convexity inequality \cite{RX16}. When specialized to Schatten $p$-classes, it also yields the corresponding tangent formulation of the Ball--Carlen--Lieb convexity inequality \cite{BCL94}. Under a suitable positivity assumption, we further establish a sharp complement to Xu's tangent inequality for Schatten $p$-classes in the range $0<p<1$.

</details>


### [3] [On Generators for $W^{*}$-bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16428)
*Ben Hayes*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个连续纤维为II₁因子的W*-丛，证明其不满足生成元问题的连续版本：任意有限个连续截面在某个纤维上都无法生成该纤维；并推广到含超有限等子丛的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成元问题（能否用单个元素生成任意可分预对偶的II₁因子或一般von Neumann代数）是算子代数中的著名问题。本文试图通过构造具体反例来揭示该问题的连续/纤维化形式无法在保持连续性的前提下成立。

Method: 构造紧可度量化空间K上的W*-丛M，使每个纤维M_p为可分预对偶的II₁因子；利用纤维化与连续截面的框架，分析有限族连续截面在各纤维生成的von Neumann子代数，并考察子丛N的纤维满足超有限、含Cartan子代数、由两个可交换扩散子代数生成、具有扩散中心列代数、或由单个序列交换轨道生成等条件。

Result: 给出显式例子：对任意有限族连续截面a_1,…,a_n∈M，存在p∈K使得a_{1,p},…,a_{n,p}不生成M_p。更一般地，若N是M的子丛且各纤维满足上述条件之一，则任意有限族a_1,…,a_n∈M都存在p∈K，使得a_{1,p},…,a_{n,p}与N_p一起不能生成M_p。

Conclusion: 不存在一种“连续”方法，能把可数预对偶tracial von Neumann代数的可数多个生成元统一地化为单个生成元——至少当该程序需同时适用于所有von Neumann代数时。这对生成元问题的连续化版本给出否定性结论。

Abstract: We give an explicit example of a $W^{*}$-bundle $M$ over a compact, metrizable space $K$, which has each fiber $M_{p}$ a $\textrm{II}_{1}$-factor with separable predual, and which satisfies the following negation of the generator problem: given any finite family $a_{1},\cdots,a_{n}\in M$ of continuous sections, there is a $p\in K$ (depending upon that family) so that $a_{1,p},\cdots,a_{n,p}$ do not generate $M_{p}$ as a von Neumann algebra. More generally, if $N$ is a sub-bundle of $M$ with the property that each fiber is hyperfinite, or has a Cartan, or is generated by two commuting diffuse subalgebras, or has diffuse central sequence algebra, or is generated by a single sequential commutation orbit, then given any finite family $a_{1},\cdots,a_{n}\in M$ of continuous sections, there is a $p\in K$ (depending upon that family) so that $a_{1,p},\cdots,a_{n,p}$ together with $N_{p}$ do not generate $M_{p}$. We discuss implications for the generator problem for von Neumann algebras: e.g. there is no ``continuous" way to take countably many generators for a tracial von Neumann algebra with separable predual and produce a single generator, at least if such a procedure works for all von Neumann algebras simultaneously.

</details>


### [4] [On the Orlicz function estimations of the $a$-Davis--Wielandt radius in $C^*$-algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16652)
*Debarati Bhattacharya,Fuad Kittaneh,Arnab Patra*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文通过Orlicz函数方法系统研究了单位C*-代数中元素的a-Davis-Wielandt半径估计，给出新的代数界，并借助极分解与Moore-Penrose逆得到经典代数Davis-Wielandt半径的估计，统一并推广了已有不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Davis-Wielandt半径及其代数变形的不等式估计在算子理论与矩阵分析中具有重要地位，但现有结果较为分散，缺乏统一处理框架，尤其缺少对a-Davis-Wielandt半径的一般性估计方法。

Method: 采用Orlicz函数方法研究单位C*-代数中元素的a-Davis-Wielandt半径估计，推导新的代数a-Davis-Wielandt半径界；并利用极分解和Moore-Penrose逆，进一步获得经典代数Davis-Wielandt半径的估计。

Result: 得到了a-Davis-Wielandt半径的新界，以及经典代数Davis-Wielandt半径的估计，且这些结果将文献中多种已有不等式作为特例予以推广与统一。

Conclusion: Orlicz函数方法为C*-代数中的a-Davis-Wielandt半径估计提供了有效且统一的框架，所得结果扩展、推广并统一了已有不等式。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of $a$-Davis--Wielandt radius estimates for elements in a unital $C^*$-algebra through an Orlicz function approach. We derive novel bounds for the algebraic $a$-Davis--Wielandt radius and further obtain estimates for the classical algebraic Davis--Wielandt radius via polar decomposition and the Moore--Penrose inverse. The results obtained in this work extend, generalize, and unify a variety of established inequalities in the literature as special cases.

</details>


### [5] [A uniform commutator bound in finite von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16932)
*Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明有限冯诺依曼代数中迹为零的算子均可表示为交换子，且因子可受绝对常数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果表明矩阵中迹为零的算子可写成交换子，本文将此结果推广到有限冯诺依曼代数，并关注交换子因子的范数估计。

Method: 利用有限冯诺依曼代数的中心值迹理论，构造满足范数乘积约束的算子B、C。

Result: 证明对任意满足中心值迹为零的A，存在B、C使A=BC−CB，且‖B‖‖C‖≤K‖A‖，K为与代数无关的绝对常数。

Conclusion: 该结论将迹为零算子的交换子表示及绝对常数范数控制推广至任意有限冯诺依曼代数。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{M}$ be a finite von Neumann algebra with normalized center-valued trace $T_{\mathcal{M}}$. We prove that every $A\in\mathcal{M}$ with $T_{\mathcal{M}}(A)=0$ admits a representation $A=BC-CB$, where $B,C\in\mathcal{M}$ satisfy $\|B\|\|C\|\le K\|A\|$ for an absolute constant $K$ independent of $\mathcal{M}$.

</details>


### [6] [Invariant random subgroups of $C^*$-simple groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16974)
*Tattwamasi Amrutam,Mehrdad Kalantar*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 可数离散C*-单群Γ上任意不变随机子群（IRS）μ，几乎每个子群H都是C*-单的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C*-单性在随机子群（IRS）意义下的遗传性质，即C*-单群的“典型”子群是否保持C*-单性。

Method: 设定Γ为可数离散C*-单群，μ为Sub(Γ)上的Γ-不变Borel概率测度（IRS），通过测度论与C*-代数方法分析μ-几乎处处子群的C*-单性。

Result: 证明了μ-几乎每个子群H都是C*-单的。

Conclusion: C*-单性在IRS意义下是几乎处处遗传的，即C*-单群的随机子群几乎必然C*-单。

Abstract: Let $Γ$ be a countable discrete $C^*$-simple group. Let $μ$ be a $Γ$-invariant Borel probability measure on $\text{Sub}(Γ)$, i.e., an IRS. We show that $μ$-almost every subgroup $H$ is $C^*$-simple.

</details>


### [7] [Classifiable C*-algebras have quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16999)
*Kan Kitamura*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明了任何可分类单C*-代数（含Jiang-Su代数）可承载若干离散量子群的外作用，由此构造出新的具TLJ标准不变量的超有限子因子，并给出SU_q(2)在Araki-Woods因子上极小作用的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C*-代数上的量子群外作用及其在算子代数结构理论中的构造性应用，特别是将可分类性理论与量子群对称性、子因子指标理论联系起来。

Method: 通过显式构造离散量子群（有限量子群及某些自由紧量子群的对偶）在可分类单C*-代数上的外作用，并分析其交叉积与固定点结构以导出子因子与因子作用。

Result: 证明有限量子群和某些自由紧量子群的对偶均可外作用于任何可分类单C*-代数；得到具Temperley-Lieb-Jones标准不变量的新超有限子因子族；并证明SU_q(2)可极小作用于某些Araki-Woods因子，且对无穷多个q成立。

Conclusion: 可分类单C*-代数具有丰富的离散量子群外作用，为子因子理论与量子群对称性研究提供了统一构造框架与新的具体例子。

Abstract: We show that every classifiable simple C*-algebra, in particular the Jiang-Su algebra, admits outer actions of several discrete quantum groups, such as finite quantum groups and duals of certain free compact quantum groups. As a consequence of the construction, we provide new examples of hyperfinite subfactors with the Temperley-Lieb-Jones standard invariant and show that $\operatorname{SU}_q(2)$ can act minimally on some Araki-Woods factors for infinitely many $q$.

</details>


### [8] [Sharp logarithmic Sobolev inequalities on free groups by variational comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17303)
*Gan Yao*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文给出自由群冯诺依曼代数上字长泊松半群带常数2的尖锐对数Sobolev不等式的一个变分比较证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Xie和Zhang已用完全不同方法证明该不等式，但缺少一个更具结构性的证明途径；同时需将循环群上的Lagrange方法推广到更一般的有限迹冯诺依曼代数框架。

Method: 采用变分比较方法，将[Yao26]中针对循环群的Lagrange方法推广为适用于有限迹冯诺依曼代数的框架，并应用于自由群冯诺依曼代数上的字长泊松半群。

Result: 成功证明了自由群冯诺依曼代数上字长泊松半群带常数2的尖锐对数Sobolev不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作为Xie和Zhang的结果提供了独立的变分比较证明，并将Lagrange方法从循环群扩展至有限迹冯诺依曼代数的一般框架。

Abstract: We give a variational comparison proof of the sharp logarithmic Sobolev inequality with constant $2$ for the word-length Poisson semigroup on free group von Neumann algebras. Our approach extends the Lagrange method for cyclic groups in [Yao26] to a framework in finite tracial von Neumann algebras. The inequality was recently established by Xie and Zhang in [XZ26] using a completely different approach.

</details>


### [9] [Generalized amenability in quantum groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17451)
*Fouad Naderi,Yong Zhang*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 在自然协变假设与可迹条件下，若局部紧量子群的约化C*-代数或预对偶代数具有近似顺从性，则该量子群存在不变量子平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部紧量子群的广义顺从性，探究约化C*-代数或预对偶代数的近似顺从性是否能推出不变量子平均的存在性。

Method: 设局部紧量子群 $\mathbb{G}=(\scr M,\Delta,\varphi,\psi)$，以 $C_r^*(\mathbb{G})$ 和 $VN(\mathbb{G})$ 分别表示量子左正规表示生成的C*-代数与von Neumann代数；在自然协变假设及 $VN(\mathbb{G})$ 的可迹性条件下，分别对 $C_r^*(\mathbb{G})$ 的近似顺从性，以及 $\scr M_*$ 具有有界近似单位元且近似顺从的情形进行论证。

Result: 若 $C_r^*(\mathbb{G})$ 近似顺从，则 $\mathbb{G}$ 具有不变量子平均；进一步，若预对偶代数 $\scr M_*$ 具有有界近似单位元且近似顺从，同样可推出 $\mathbb{G}$ 具有不变量子平均。

Conclusion: 约化C*-代数或预对偶代数的近似顺从性（配合相应假设）均可导出局部紧量子群的不变量子平均，从而建立了广义顺从性的等价刻画。

Abstract: We study generalized amenability of locally compact quantum groups $\mathbb{G}=(\scr M, Δ, φ, ψ)$. Let $C_r^*(\mathbb{G})$ and $VN(\mathbb{G})$ denote, respectively, the C*-algebra and von Neumann algebra generated by the quantum left regular representation of $\mathbb{G}$. With natural covariance assumptions and a traceability condition on $VN(\mathbb{G})$, we show that if $C_r^*(\mathbb{G})$ is approximately amenable, then $\mathbb{G}$ admits an invariant quantum mean. We show further that the same conclusion holds if the predual algebra $\scr M_*$ has a bounded approximate identity and is approximately amenable.

</details>

### [10] [An endpoint exception to a conjectured equality characterization for Laplacian eigenvalue products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16049)
*Scott Gibson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了八个顶点的反例，否定了Chen、Guo、Li与Wang（Electron. J. Combin. 32(4) (2025), P4.37）中猜想20的等号刻画，但并未反驳该猜想中的数值不等式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原猜想20不仅给出一个数值不等式，还给出了等号成立时的图结构刻画。作者希望检验该等号刻画是否严格正确，从而寻找反例以指出刻画部分的漏洞。

Method: 通过构造性方法给出八个顶点的图作为反例，取到包含端点 k=3n/4，并将该构造推广为无限族；其中还包含一个图与其补图均连通的无限族。

Result: 得到了八顶点反例，并推广为无限族（含图与补图均连通的族）。这些反例不满足猜想20的等号刻画，但满足其中的数值不等式。

Conclusion: 猜想20的等号刻画有误，需予以修正；而其数值不等式仍未被这些反例推翻。

Abstract: We give eight-vertex counterexamples to the equality characterization in Conjecture 20 of Chen, Guo, Li and Wang, Electron. J. Combin. 32(4) (2025), P4.37. The examples occur at the included endpoint $k=3n/4$ and extend to infinite families, including a family for which both the graph and its complement are connected. These examples do not contradict the numerical inequality in that conjecture.

</details>


### [11] [Logarithmic Circumference In Tough Grahphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16083)
*Levi Segal,Jacques Verstraete*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了2-连通t-韧图中存在长至少为ℓ的圈的充分条件n ≤ ℓ(1+1/t)^{⌊ℓ/2⌋-1}，从而解决了Broersma等人提出的对数界猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于图中长圈的存在性，韧性与圈长之间存在联系。Broersma、van den Heuvel、Jung和Veldman曾提出一个对数形式的猜想，本文旨在解决这一猜想。

Method: 通过图论分析技术，针对2-连通t-韧图，建立顶点数n与圈长ℓ之间的定量关系证明。

Result: 对任意实数t>0，若2-连通t-韧图满足n ≤ ℓ(1+1/t)^{⌊ℓ/2⌋-1}，则该图含有长度至少为ℓ的圈。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Broersma等人所猜想的对数界。

Abstract: For every real $t>0$, we prove that every $2$-connected $t$-tough graph contains a cycle of length at least $\ell$ whenever $n \leq \ell(1 + 1/t)^{\lfloor \ell/2\rfloor - 1}$. This establishes the logarithmic bound conjectured by Broersma, van den Heuvel, Jung, and Veldman.

</details>


### [12] [A simpler proof of the Matrix Spencer Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16100)
*Nikhil Bansal,Yunbum Kook*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对 Akbas 与 Sra [AS26] 提出的矩阵 Spencer 定理给出一个简洁的阐述性说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵 Spencer 定理是该领域的近期重要结果，但原始证明或表述可能较为复杂，读者难以快速把握其核心思想，因此有必要提供一个简明、易懂的阐释。

Method: 采用阐述性（expository）的讲解方式，对 Akbas 与 Sra [AS26] 的矩阵 Spencer 定理的证明思路与关键步骤进行重新梳理和简化说明。

Abstract: We study combinatorial aspects of hyperplane sections of polytopes, focusing on how much the combinatorics of the sections determines the combinatorics of the original polytope. In contrast, for sufficiently generic simple polytopes, the function recording the number of vertices of each central section determines the full combinatorial type. We organize different combinatorial slicing properties into hierarchies, separately for affine and central sections, according to the level of combinatorial structure they determine on the polytope. Abstract: We determine exactly how harmonic sums, taken over integers with a given number of occurrences of a fixed block of digits, depend on that number of occurrences. Language factorizations, combined with stochastic radix expansions, relate these harmonic sums to the iteration of an operator whose eigenvectors are the Lebesgue measure and distributional derivatives of singular measures. The dual picture is given by a Riesz basis of eigenpolynomials in a suitable Hardy space. Abstract: Hypergraphs provide a natural framework for modeling higher-order relationships, but the development of spectral techniques with provable guarantees for general non-uniform hypergraphs remains challenging. Building on Banerjee's normalized adjacency matrix and Spiro's averaging-based diffusion framework, we develop a spectral framework for non-uniform hypergraphs and establish Cheeger's inequality for their conductance. A fundamental result in the spectral theory of hypergraphs asserts that, for every non-covering hypergraph, the second-smallest eigenvalue of its normalized Laplacian is at most one. This spectral characterization yields an improved Cheeger's inequality for non-covering hypergraphs, and we show that the resulting inequality is tight on both sides using cycle and cube hypergraphs. Our framework further yields higher-order Cheeger inequalities and provides theoretical guarantees for Fiedler's spectral partitioning algorithm, all in the setting of hypergraphs. Conclusion: 中心图的独立控制数可由原图的参数完全刻画，所得界是紧的，公式为闭式表达。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph with vertex set $V(G)$. A set $I\subseteq V(G)$ is an independent dominating set of $G$ if no two vertices in $I$ are adjacent and every vertex in $V(G)\setminus I$ is adjacent to at least one vertex in $I$. The independent domination number of $G$ is the minimum cardinality among all independent dominating sets of $G$. The aim of this article is to obtain tight bounds and closed formulas for the independent domination number of central graphs. The results are expressed in terms of parameters of the original graph from which the central graph is constructed.

</details>


### [17] [Polynomial Length Cycles In Tough Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16398)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图论中，toughness（坚韧度）与 Hamilto 性、长圈的存在性关系密切。经典猜想认为足够 tough 的图应为 Hamiltonian（即含长度为 n 的圈）。该工作旨在长圈方向给出一个可证明的定量下界，即在给定 toughness 常数条件下保证多大长度的圈。

Method: 属于极值图论/结构图论中的存在性证明，采用 tough 图的结构分析（利用 toughness 约束下的割集与分量结构），结合长圈构造技术推出圈长下界。核心思路是把 toughness≥15 转化为对图的结构控制，进而保证一条相当长的圈存在。

Result: 主要结论：任意 n 个顶点、toughness 至少为 15 的图，必含一个长度至少为 Ω(n^{1/20}) 的圈。该界以 n 的多项式（但指数很小）形式给出，属于次线性甚至极弱多项式量级的长圈下界。

Conclusion: 该结果表明高 toughness 能保证长圈存在，但所给出的长度界远低于 Hamiltonicity 猜想所期望的线性长度；因此可作为长圈问题的阶段性定量结果，提示 tough 图长圈的研究仍有显著改进空间。

Abstract: We prove that every $n$-vertex $15$-tough graph contains a cycle of length $Ω(n^{1/20})$.

</details>


### [18] [Billiard Orbits in Young Diagrams: Medial Links, Bicycle Spaces, and Domino Tilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16533)
*David J. Hemmer*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究整数分拆的Young图中斜率为±1的对角台球轨道，将其与胞腔邻接图$G_\lambda$的中介链环分量等同，得到轨道数公式$\sigma(\lambda)=1+\dim\mathcal{B}(G_\lambda)=\mathrm{nullity}\,L(G_\lambda)$，并给出色失衡与秩亏分解、上界与精确值等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Gerdes研究的Chokwe sona沙画镜像曲线模型从矩形网格推广到任意Young图，统一研究对角台球闭合轨道计数问题。

Method: 把台球轨道与邻接图$G_\lambda$中介链环的分量对应；用二元自行车空间$\mathcal{B}$和模2 Laplacian $L$刻画轨道数；通过删除首行首列得$\lambda^\square$，用邻接矩阵零化度表示；再借助棋盘二部划分分解$\sigma(\lambda)-1$。

Result: $\sigma(\lambda)=1+\dim\mathcal{B}(G_\lambda)=\mathrm{nullity}\,L(G_\lambda)=1+\mathrm{nullity}_{\mathbb{F}_2}A(G_{\lambda^\square})$；矩形情形恢复Gerdes公式$\sigma(n^m)=\gcd(m,n)$；一般地$\sigma(\lambda)=1$当且仅当$\lambda^\square$的多米诺铺砌数为奇数；$\sigma(\lambda)-1$分解为色失衡项（联系BG-rank）与偶秩亏项；$\sigma(\lambda)$不超过Durfee长度；楼梯分拆$\sigma(n,n-1,\ldots,1)=\lceil n/2\rceil$；邻接零化度公式与基域无关。

Conclusion: 该工作以图论代数方法统一刻画了Young图对角台球闭合轨道数，推广了沙画模型与Gerdes公式，并给出奇偶限制、渐近下界及各类精确公式，表明轨道数与色失衡及秩亏结构密切相关。

Abstract: We study diagonal billiard trajectories inside the Young diagram of an integer partition $λ$. A trajectory has slope $\pm 1$, passes straight through sides shared by adjacent cells, and reflects from the exterior boundary until it closes. Let $σ(λ)$ be the number of closed orbits. This extends the mirror-curve model of Chokwe sona sand drawings studied by Gerdes from rectangular grids to arbitrary Young diagrams.
  Let $G_λ$ be the cell-adjacency graph of $λ$, with its natural planar embedding. We identify the billiard orbits with the components of the medial link of $G_λ$, and deduce that $σ(λ) = 1 + \dim \mathcal{B}(G_λ) = \mathrm{nullity}\, L(G_λ)$ over $\mathbb{F}_2$, where $\mathcal{B}$ is the binary bicycle space and $L$ the mod-2 Laplacian. Writing $λ^\square$ for the diagram obtained by deleting the first row and column of $λ$, we further prove $σ(λ) = 1 + \mathrm{nullity}_{\mathbb{F}_2} A(G_{λ^\square})$. For rectangles this recovers Gerdes' formula $σ(n^m) = \gcd(m,n)$ via identities for Fibonacci polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_2$, and in general it gives the characterization: $σ(λ) = 1$ if and only if $λ^\square$ has an odd number of domino tilings.
  Using the checkerboard bipartition of $λ^\square$, we decompose $σ(λ) - 1$ into a color-imbalance term, related to the BG-rank of Berkovich-Garvan, and an even rank-deficiency term. This yields parity restrictions and lower bounds for the orbit number, and shows that for any fixed $d$, asymptotically all partitions have more than $d$ orbits. We also prove that $σ(λ)$ is at most the Durfee length of $λ$, determine $σ(n, n-1, \ldots, 1) = \lceil n/2 \rceil$ for staircase partitions, and show that the adjacency-nullity formula is independent of the ground field.

</details>


### [19] [Density and separation for augmented Zarankiewicz numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16555)
*Nikita Lebedev*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究增广Zarankiewicz问题：在无全1的2×2子矩阵的二元矩阵上添加不相交的单元格对，证明从最大C4-free矩阵出发反而会降低最终最优值，否定了Qi、Cui和Xu的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Qi、Cui和Xu提出的问题：以最大C4-free矩阵为初始矩阵是否会降低增广Zarankiewicz问题的最终最优值。直觉上初始占用单元格越多应越有利，作者对此进行检验。

Method: 引入两个最优值z_A(m,n)（所有C4-free初始矩阵上的最优）与z_L(m,n)（初始矩阵必须取最大占用数时的最优）。结合密度估计与稳定性估计、组合构造，以及一个精确的多项式证书来完成证明。

Result: 在n→∞且n≤m=o(n²)时，证明z_A(m,n)−z_L(m,n)≥(1/30−o(1))mn，并确定尖锐二阶项z_A(m,n)=mn/3+(1/√6+o(1))n√m；给出m=n=1893的显式分离构造；发现尖锐密度阈值：当m/n²→c>0时，z_L(m,n)/(mn)趋于1/3当且仅当c≥1/12。

Conclusion: 以最大C4-free矩阵作初始矩阵确实会降低最终最优值，否定了原作者的问题；同时刻画了z_A的尖锐渐近式及z_L的密度阈值现象。

Abstract: We study the augmented Zarankiewicz problem, in which disjoint pairs of cells are added to a binary matrix with no all-one $2\times2$ submatrix. The pairs must satisfy compatibility conditions, and the objective counts each original occupied cell and each added pair once. We show that starting with a maximum $C_4$-free matrix can lower the final optimum, answering a question of Qi, Cui, and Xu.
  Let ${z_A}(m,n)$ be the optimum over all $C_4$-free initial matrices, and ${z_L}(m,n)$ the optimum when the initial matrix must have the maximum number of occupied cells. As $n\to\infty$ with $n\le m=o(n^2)$, we prove \[
  {z_A}(m,n)-{z_L}(m,n)\ge\left(\frac1{30}-o(1)\right)mn \] and determine the sharp second-order term: \[
  {z_A}(m,n)=\frac{mn}{3}+\left(\frac1{\sqrt6}+o(1)\right)n\sqrt m. \] An explicit construction gives a separation at $m=n=1893$. We also find a sharp density threshold: when $n\to\infty$ and $m/n^2\to c>0$, the limited density ${z_L}(m,n)/(mn)$ tends to $1/3$ if and only if $c\ge1/12$. The proofs combine density and stability estimates, combinatorial constructions, and an exact polynomial certificate.

</details>


### [20] [On the number of palindromic factors of low complexity words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16596)
*Dong Han Kim,Sanghoon Kwon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究回文因子复杂度与因子复杂度的关系，证明当因子复杂度不超过长度1.5倍加1时，相邻两长度回文因子总数取到上界等号，并构造达到最优的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果给出递归非周期且语言对反转封闭的无限词中，相邻两个长度的回文因子总数至多为因子复杂度一步增长加二的上界；本文旨在刻画该上界何时取等号，并给出最优性证据与局部判定准则。

Method: 通过因子复杂度与回文复杂度的组合分析，证明当给定长度的因子复杂度不超过该长度1.5倍加1时，上述上界必然取等；结合构造性方法给出取等号的具体例子，并推导自适应的局部判据。

Result: 证明了在因子复杂度不超过长度1.5倍加1的条件下相邻两长度回文因子总数等于上界；构造例子表明该界最优；得到自适应局部判据，并将其应用于反转封闭的准Sturmian词与回文亏格。

Conclusion: 刻画了回文复杂度上界取等号的充分条件，给出了最优性构造与局部判别准则，为反转封闭的准Sturmian词及回文亏格研究提供了工具。

Abstract: Factor complexity counts the distinct factors of each length in an infinite word, while palindromic complexity counts those invariant under reversal. For recurrent aperiodic words with reversal-closed language, the total number of palindromic factors in two consecutive lengths is at most the one-step growth of factor complexity plus two. We prove that equality is forced whenever the factor complexity at a given length does not exceed three halves of that length plus one. We construct examples showing that the bound is optimal. We also obtain an adaptive local criterion and consequences for reversal-closed quasi-Sturmian words and palindromic defect.

</details>


### [21] [Linear matroid products: a synthetic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16747)
*Mykhaylo Tyomkyn*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入星基范式（star-basis normal form），用于比较同一域上抽象（双）刚度拟阵的表示，从而细化底层拟阵上的弱序关系，并据此在线性可表示拟阵类中解决若干（双）刚度拟阵的"最自由"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀拟阵的张量/对称/外积与抽象（双）刚度拟阵之间存在对偶关系，由此联系起两个原本被认为无关的领域的问题。Mason (1981) 问任意两个均匀拟阵是否容许最自由乘积（freest product）；经对偶，Cruickshank、Jackson、Jordán、Tanigawa 将其改写为：一般双刚度拟阵是否为其参数下最自由的抽象双刚度拟阵。Jackson 与 Tanigawa 的相关猜想则问一般 2-超连通拟阵是否为最自由的 {K_4,K_{3,3}}-拟阵。本文旨在有效处理并在可线性表示拟阵类中完全解决这些问题。

Method: 引入星基范式（star-basis normal forms），使得可以在同一域上比较抽象（双）刚度拟阵的表示，并由此得到底层拟阵上弱序关系的一个细化。利用该工具，作者对线性可表示情形下的相关"最自由"问题进行分析与证明。

Result: （1）证明一般双刚度拟阵是最自由的可线性表示抽象 (a,b)-双刚度拟阵，从而在线性拟阵情形下证实 Mason 猜想。（2）证明每个可表示抽象 2-刚度拟阵都容许一个刚度矩阵表示，加强了一般 2-刚度拟阵的极大性性质。（3）证明每个可表示抽象 2-刚度拟阵，若其中每个 K_{3,3} 的副本都是回路，则容许一个超连通矩阵表示。由此推出一般刚度族 R_2 与超连通族 H_2 是仅有的可线性表示 2-刚度族。

Abstract: The recently established duality between tensor, symmetric and exterior products of uniform matroids on the one hand, and abstract (bi-)rigidity matroids on the other hand, connects theorems and open questions from both areas, previously thought unrelated. We also prove that every representable abstract $2$-rigidity matroid in which every copy of $K_{3,3}$ is a circuit admits a hyperconnectivity matrix representation. It follows that the generic rigidity and hyperconnectivity families $\mathcal{R}_2$ and $\mathcal{H}_2$ are the only linearly representable $2$-rigidity families.

Abstract: We classify the labelled final layer orders of ordinary orthogonal 2 x 2k maps whose mountain-valley assignments are periodic with period two in each crease family. The remaining eight words reduce, under labelled symmetries, to two constant-row families described by row pivots. For the canonical word MMMMVV, an ordered pivot pair (a,b) is realizable exactly when a = 1, a = m, or a = b, where m = 2k - 1. For MVMMMM, realizability is equivalent to BR(a) = BR(b). Abstract: Hall algebras can be associated with a broad class of combinatorial structures through the theory of 2-Segal sets. Abstract: Burson and Eichhorn proposed infinite and finite coefficientwise non-negativity conjectures for products arising from copartitions. We obtain several further results. For the infinite conjecture, we prove non-negativity under the broader condition $a\equiv b\pmod m$, which includes the known case $a=b$. Abstract: Let $\mathcal S(T)$ be the adjacency spectral center of a tree $T$, and let $\mathcal C(T)$ be its characteristic set. Writing $Δ_n:=\max_{|V(T)|=n}d(T)$, we prove $Δ_n=0\quad(3\le n\le11),\quad Δ_{12}=1$, and $Δ_n=\left\lfloor\frac{n-11}{2}\right\rfloor \quad(n\ge13)$. Result: 主要结果:(1) 建立普适二次下界 σ(n) ≥ ⌈n(n-2)/6⌉,并证明其为均匀一阶矩论证的最强可达结论;(2) 证明尖锐的最小重排不等式;(3) 构造显式循环 Hollow-Shell 实例,达到 ⌊(n-1)²/4⌋ 个阻塞对;(4) 给出双团可实现性的 Hall 型充分条件;(5) 证明均匀平均与双参数线性分配目标存在结构性局限;(6) 对所有无条件结果提供完整证明,并清楚区分计算机辅助验证与数学证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过盾数刻画了稳定婚姻问题中稳定性的普适量化边界,给出的下界在均匀一阶矩框架下是最优的,并借助最小重排不等式与 Hollow-Shell 构造实现了紧致的实例界。同时揭示了均匀平均及双参数线性分配目标的结构性局限,说明进一步改进需要超越一阶矩的论证技术。所有结论均有完整数学证明,计算验证仅作为辅助且被明确分离。

Abstract: We study the Shield number of the stable marriage problem, defined as the minimum, over all preference profiles of size n, of the maximum number of blocking pairs attainable by a complete matching. We establish the universal quadratic lower bound $σ(n) \ge \lceil n(n-2)/6 \rceil$ using a rearrangement inequality and show that this bound is the strongest consequence obtainable from uniform first-moment arguments. We further prove a sharp min-rearrangement inequality and construct an explicit cyclic Hollow-Shell instance attaining $\lfloor (n-1)^2/4 \rfloor$ blocking pairs. We also establish sufficient Hall-type conditions for biclique realizability, prove structural limitations of uniform averaging and two-parameter linear assignment objectives, and provide complete proofs for all unconditional results. Computer-assisted verification is used only for explicitly identified computational statements and is clearly distinguished from mathematical proofs.

</details>

Abstract: Stanley's conjecture asserts that the $h$-vector of a matroid is a pure $O$-sequence. Corry, Dochtermann, McClain, Perkinson and Yi introduced cycle systems, which give a bijective proof for the matroids that admit one, through coparking functions, and proposed generalized cycle systems. Every matroid admitting a cycle system is regular, and the Wagner and Petersen graphs admit no generalized cycle system consisting of circuits. We propose a relaxation. Where the coparking recursion breaks down, at the strata whose unique union is independent, the coparking functions are replaced by a fibre: a pure multicomplex with the $h$-vector of the dead node, the minor of the matroid attached to the stratum. We prove, for any matroid with a fixed basis, that whenever the required fibres exist the relaxed coparking functions form a pure multicomplex whose degree sequence is the $h$-vector of the matroid, so Stanley's conjecture follows. The proof rests on a version of Dhar's burning algorithm for a matroid with a fixed basis, which gives the purity, and on a deletion-contraction identity that computes the gap between the $h$-vector and the coparking functions as a sum of local $h$-vectors, one per dead node. Coned, biconed and triconed graphs carry a fibre system with their canonical spanning trees, as do the Wagner and Petersen graphs. Beyond graphs, every basis of a matroid of rank at most three, of a matroid of corank two or of a uniform matroid carries a fibre system, so Stanley's conjecture follows for those classes. We also give a graph of radius two on twelve vertices which, with its breadth-first spanning tree, carries no fibre system.

Abstract: Balogh, Bollobás and Narayanan conjectured that among all finite simple $r$-uniform $d$-regular hypergraphs, the number of weak independent sets is maximized by a natural quasi-bipartite construction $H_{r,d}$. We give three types of evidence for this conjecture. For every fixed $r$, we prove the conjectured asymptotic exponential rate whenever the twin quotient has maximum pair codegree $o(d)$. The proof uses the hypergraph container method. For hypergraphs with no cross-edges, the occupancy method gives the sharper error bound $O_r(\log d/d)$. We show that a stronger version of the conjecture in terms of the so-called occupancy fraction is not true, by providing a counterexample for every $r\ge 3$. We also prove exact cases of the conjecture when the hypergraph is $2$-regular. Using an entropy decomposition in the dual edge-cover problem, we settle every odd $r$ and the case $(r,d)=(4,2)$.

Abstract: Kemeny's constant quantifies the expected time for a random walk to reach a randomly chosen vertex, capturing global properties of a Markov chain. We develop a matrix-analytic framework for bounding Kemeny's constant of a finite connected weighted graph using degree-weighted compressions of the normalized adjacency matrix, pinching inequalities, and eigenvalue interlacing. Our main partition theorem gives lower bounds in terms of the compressed spectrum, with complete equality characterizations, and converts structural graph information into spectrally computable estimates. For instance, when applied to proper color partitions, the proposed method yields a sharp lower bound on Kemeny's constant in terms of the chromatic number \[
  K(G)\ge n-2+\frac{1}{χ(G)}, \] which extends a bipartite bound of Ciardo, Dahl, and Kirkland (2022) to arbitrary chromatic number, and which is incomparable with the normalized Hoffman bound by Chung (1997). For unweighted graphs, we also characterize all equality cases of this bound. We further illustrate the power of the proposed matrix framework by deriving spectral bounds for NP-hard graph problems involving normalized cuts and conductance. Our results include an asymptotically sharp conductance bound, two-sided interlacing bounds from principal submatrices and quotient matrices, and estimates for the change in Kemeny's constant under connectivity-preserving deletion of multiple edges.

</details>

Method: 引入循环目标对(ω,β)，用固定点自由的循环型与相对β的定向构造固定内容字；以颜色标识循环、秩表示定向。定义字可实化条件（ρ=ωβ为N-循环），利用颜色置换、秩原点平移、字旋转、位置反射与秩反转构成扭二面体作用，其轨道即"扭手镯"。建立可实化轨道与循环目标对的扩展环路等价类之间的双射，从而精确计数并生成每个类的代表元。

Result: 给出可实化扩展环路等价类的精确轨道计数与代表性生成方法；结合固定点收缩与结构约化，对剩余扭手镯穷举验证，证明TD(16)=9。

Conclusion: TD(16)=9成立，n≤17的所有换位直径问题全部解决，闭合了一个持续25年的公开问题。

Abstract: Sorting By Transpositions (SBT) seeks the minimum number of transpositions required to sort a permutation $π$ on $n$ symbols into the identity $ι$. Let $N=n+1$. A cyclic-target pair $(ω,β)$ consists of an even permutation $ω$ and an $N$-cycle $β$ such that $ρ=ωβ$ is also an $N$-cycle. An SBT instance is the special case $(\barι {\barπ}^{-1},\barπ)$, where $\barπ$ and $\barι$ are $N$-cycles corresponding to $π$ and $ι$, respectively, and $\barι {\barπ}^{-1}\barπ=\barι$, so its target is $\barι$. For each prescribed fixed-point-free cycle type with specified cycle orientations relative to $β$, fixed-content words encode the corresponding permutations $ω$. Colors identify cycles and ranks record their orientations. A word is realizable when the associated product $ρ=ωβ$ is an $N$-cycle. Permuting equal-part colors and shifting rank origins give auxiliary symmetries. Together with word rotation and position reflection combined with rank inversion, they define a twisted dihedral action. Its orbits are twisted bracelets, and the realizable orbits are in bijection with extended-toric equivalence classes of cyclic-target pairs. Here extended-toric equivalence means Eriksson et al.'s toric equivalence with reflection adjoined. This correspondence yields exact orbit counts and direct generation of one representative per realizable extended-toric class. The transposition diameter $TD(n)$ is the largest transposition distance in $S_n$. Previously, $9\leq TD(16)\leq10$ was known, and $16$ was the only unresolved value for $n\leq17$. Combining fixed-point contraction and structural reductions with exhaustive verification of the remaining twisted bracelets, we prove $TD(16)=9$, closing a twenty-five-year gap.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文完整补全了Meijer–Barnes K-函数在N<0情形下的理论，统一了围道结构与变换性质，提供了残数递推与插入演算工具，并通过高精度数值实验验证了相关恒等式与围道公式。

Abstract: Karp and Kuznetsov introduced the Meijer--Barnes $K$-function by replacing the Euler gamma factors in the Mellin--Barnes kernel of the Meijer $G$-function with Barnes double gamma factors, but excluded the regime $N=2(m+n)-p-q<0$. We complete this missing case. The kernel decays super-exponentially in two horizontal sectors, and the defining integral converges absolutely for every $z$ on the Riemann surface of the logarithm and every $α\in\mathbb{C}$. There are exactly four admissible contour classes, denoted $K^\rightarrow,K^\leftarrow,K^\uparrow,K^\downarrow$, satisfying the universal relation $K^\uparrow+K^\downarrow=K^\rightarrow+K^\leftarrow$ and hence spanning a space of dimension at most three. Under an explicit closing-arc growth hypothesis, we derive residue expansions and strengthened vanishing theorems; the three basic transformation laws hold unconditionally. We also prove that no vertical contour is admissible for $N<0$, replace the standard vertical-contour Mellin-transform method by a compatible two-index recurrence for residue coefficients, and develop a kernel-level insertion calculus together with analytic transform identities where the required interchange is justified, including integer and Riemann--Liouville fractional derivatives. Finally, we construct a numerical evaluator for the Barnes double gamma function at general $τ>0$ and validate representative identities and contour formulas to high precision.

Abstract: For every $n\in\mathbb N$, we construct a finite metric subspace $M_n$ of $\ell_\infty^n$ such that the Lipschitz-free space $\mathcal F(M_n)$ contains a linear isometric copy of $\ell_\infty^n$. This answers a question posed by Khan, Mim, and Ostrovskii, who obtained examples in dimensions three and four. Method: 对非极不连通的紧F-空间K，构造闭单位球上的无不动点非扩张自映射。使用含两个递增profile的超限收缩传播系统；后继-极限延迟保持尾约束且在profile域中无子解；分析记录边界亏量；利用序数C_0-空间上的正算子和由两条尾确定的公共中心，得到满足序支配不等式的非扩张综合。拓扑族在零维情形由极大Boole链中的间隙获得，一般情形由符号的超限延拓获得。

Abstract: We prove that, for a compact Hausdorff space $K$, the real Banach space $C(K)$ has the ball fixed point property if and only if $K$ is extremally disconnected. The new implication is obtained by constructing a fixed-point-free nonexpansive self-map of the whole closed unit ball whenever $K$ is a compact $F$-space which is not extremally disconnected. The construction uses a transfinite contractive propagation system with two increasing profiles. A successor--limit delay preserves their tail constraint and has no subsolution in the profile domain. Analysis records boundary deficits; positive operators on ordinal $C_0$-spaces and a common center determined by the two tails yield a nonexpansive synthesis satisfying an order domination inequality. The required topological families are obtained from a gap in a maximal Boolean chain in the zero-dimensional case, and from a transfinite extension of signs otherwise. The argument works in ZFC and resolves the question posed by Avilés, Japón, Lennard, Martínez-Cervantes, and Stawski.

</details>

*Daniel Núñez-Alarcón,Daniel M. Pellegrino,Eduardo V. Teixeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文对完全有界Bohnenblust--Hille不等式的等号情形进行了完整分类，并建立了尖锐的结构稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全有界Bohnenblust--Hille不等式（Arunachalam、Dutt、Escudero Gutiérrez和Palazuelos）以最优常数一控制系数可和性，且与所在空间维数无关。理解其等号情形与近等号结构，有助于刻画极值张量并为稳定性分析提供基础。

Method: 在实数域与复数域上对所有非零等号情形进行分类，将其识别为支撑在等边笛卡尔盒上的Hadamard链的标量倍数；对固定次数d≥2，运用临界系数ℓ_{2d/(d+1)}范数、完全有界范数与亏量ε，构造平坦幺模路径与规范化酉链逼近，并给出归一化Frobenius边估计。

Result: 等号情形恰为支撑于等边笛卡尔盒上的Hadamard链的标量倍数，从而等号同时决定系数张量的支撑与分解。对任意固定d≥2，完全有界范数为1、亏量为ε的形式在临界ℓ_{2d/(d+1)}范数下与同一盒上的平坦幺模路径及规范化酉链的距离均为C_d√ε；相应的幺模与缩放酉边满足匹配的归一化Frobenius估计。零亏量时两种逼近均与原Hadamard链重合。

Conclusion: 所有常数均与空间维数无关，且在两种域上逼近的指数1/2均为最优，表明该稳定性估计在维数自由意义下是尖锐的。

Abstract: The completely bounded Bohnenblust--Hille inequality of Arunachalam, Dutt, Escudero Gutiérrez and Palazuelos controls coefficient summability with optimal constant one, independently of the ambient dimension. We classify all nonzero equality cases over both the real and complex fields: they are precisely scalar multiples of Hadamard chains supported on equal-sided Cartesian boxes. Thus equality determines both the support and the factorization of the coefficient tensor. For each fixed degree $d\ge2$, we also establish sharp structural stability. A form with completely bounded norm one and deficit $\varepsilon$ lies within $C_d\sqrt\varepsilon$, in the critical coefficient $\ell_{2d/(d+1)}$ norm, of both a flat unimodular path and a normalized unitary chain on the same box. The corresponding unimodular and scaled unitary edges satisfy a matching normalized Frobenius estimate. At zero deficit both approximants coincide with the original Hadamard chain. All constants are independent of the ambient dimension, and the exponent $1/2$ is optimal for both approximations over either field.

</details>

### [59] [Arazy's conjecture concerning Schur multipliers: revisited and resolved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17144)
*Jinghao Huang,Fedor Sukochev*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了Schur--Hadamard乘子$S_{\Psi_{f,\lambda}}$在Schatten--von Neumann类$\mathcal{S}^q\to\mathcal{S}^p$上（对于所有满足$f\in C^1([-1,1])$、$f(0)=0$、$|f'(t)|\lesssim|t|^\alpha$的函数$f$及$\lambda\in\ell^r$）有界的参数对$(p,q)$，并给出完整判据$\frac1p\leq\frac\alpha r+\min\{1,\frac1q\}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Schur--Hadamard乘子（其符号为函数的差商矩阵）在非交换Schatten类之间的有界性。此前Arazy (PAMS, 1982)与Potapov--Sukochev--Tomskova (Adv. Math., 2015)的结果未完全处理$q=\infty,1$及$0<p<1$等情形，本文旨在补全这些未被解决的参数情况并给出统一判据。

Method: 设$\mathcal{S}^r$为Schatten--von Neumann类，$S_{\Psi_{f,\lambda}}$的符号为$f$沿$\lambda$的差商矩阵。针对$f\in C^1([-1,1])$，$f(0)=0$，$|f'(t)|\lesssim|t|^\alpha$，$\lambda\in\ell^r$实数且$\|\lambda\|_{\ell^\infty}\leq1$，确定$S_{\Psi_{f,\lambda}}:\mathcal{S}^q\to\mathcal{S}^p$对所有此类$f,\lambda$有界的参数对。主要的新正向估计处理$q=\infty,1$与$0<p<1$；方法同时给出所引两篇主要结果的新的证明。

Result: 有界性成立当且仅当$\frac1p\leq\frac\alpha r+\min\{1,\frac1q\}$。特别地，该结论解决了Arazy (PAMS, 1982)与Potapov--Sukochev--Tomskova (Adv. Math., 2015)中未处理的情形；新方法也重新证明了这两篇论文的主要结果。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{S}^ r$ denote the Schatten--von Neumann class and let $S_{Ψ_{f,λ}}$ be the Schur--Hadamard multiplier whose symbol is the divided-difference matrix of $f$ along $λ$. Abstract: We consider the harmonically weighted Dirichlet spaces $D(μ)$ induced by Borel measures which are mutually absolutely continuous with respect to the Lebesgue measure $m$ on the unit circle $\mathbb T$. We then study the multiplier algebras of these spaces with particular emphasis on inner multipliers. Specifically, when $f'$ is bounded, we obtain an explicit description of all singular inner functions in the multiplier algebra of $D(m_f)$ in terms of the radial zero set of $f$. As an application, we prove that if $f',g'$ are bounded and the radial zero sets of $f$ and $g$ are different, then the operators $(M_z, D(m_f))$ and $(M_z, D(m_g))$ are not similar. This generalizes a result of Richter showing that the operators $(M_z, D(m_{z-1}))$ and $(M_z, D)$ are not similar. Then, we classify all invariant subspaces $\mathcal M$ of $(M_z, D(μ))$ for which $M_z|_{\mathcal M}$ is similar to $(M_z, D(μ))$, where $μ$ is any measure mutually absolutely continuous with respect to $m$. Finally, we study a special case of the problem of finding all functions $g\in D$ for which $gD(m_g)$ is a closed invariant subspace of $(M_z, D)$. A key ingredient in many of our results is the Richter-Sundberg formula for the local Dirichlet integral.

</details>

Abstract: Let $Δ_k$ be the Dunkl Laplacian associated with an arbitrary root system and a nonnegative multiplicity function. More precisely, it is proved that the space $H^p_{\mathrm{Dunkl}}$, which is originally defined by a relevant square function, coincides with the space generated by $({\rm CW},p,2)$-atoms, that is, atoms supported on Euclidean balls and satisfying cancellation conditions against all polynomials of degree $ \leq s_p$, where \[
  s_p=\left\lfloor \mathbf N\left(\frac1p-1\right)\right\rfloor \] and $\mathbf N$ is the homogeneous dimension of the underlying Dunkl measure. The corresponding quasi-norms are equivalent. We also show that the same space is obtained when the $L^2$ size condition in the definition of atoms is replaced by the $L^\infty$ size condition. The strategy of the proof is to use an operator-type atomic decomposition associated with the Dunkl Laplacian, and then prove that
  each such operator atom can be written a linear combination of $({\rm CW},p,2)$-atoms.

</details>

Abstract: This paper systematically investigates the structural, analytical, and operator-theoretic properties of the Nevanlinna class $\mathcal{N}_u$ of Dirichlet series, introduced by Brevig and Perfekt [Adv.\ Math., 2021] and further developed by Guo \textit{et al.}\ [Ann.\ Inst.\ Fourier (Grenoble), 2025]. We then prove a Littlewood--Paley type identity for functions in $\mathcal{N}_u$, establish an equivalent characterization via vertical limit functions, and demonstrate that the interchange of limits in this identity is permissible. In addition, we provide an alternative proof of this identity using potential-theoretic approach. Applying these analytical tools, we study composition operators $C_Φ$ acting on $\mathcal{N}_u$ and characterize those symbols $Φ$ that induce bounded composition operators $C_Φ$. Utilizing Carleson measure techniques on half-planes and infinite-dimensional tori, we characterize the symbols $Φ$ that generate bounded and compact composition operators. In particular, we prove the complete equivalence between the (vanishing) Carleson embedding condition and the geometric (vanishing) Carleson condition on Carleson squares. We conclude with two function-theoretic applications to functions in $\mathcal{N}_u$.

TL;DR: 在GRH下研究大整数表示为m个近似相等的素数k次幂之和，证明当m<k(k+1)/2且m,k为足够大的奇数时可取θ>1/2；对大m情形给出m>16k log k+4k+2时的解存在性及解数渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究短区间Waring-Goldbach问题，即把大整数表示为近乎相等的素数幂之和，并确定所需的分布尺度下界θ。

Method: 假设广义黎曼假设（GRH），采用与先前工作密切相关的新工具；对大m情形通过改进minor arcs的处理进行分析。

Result: 在GRH下，对任意ε>0可取θ>1/2，条件为m<k(k+1)/2且m,k为足够大奇数；对足够大m，若m>16k log k+4k+2则方程有解，并给出解数渐近公式，主项含N^{2kθ(1-(1-1/k)^{m_1})+θ(m-1)+1-k}，其中m_1=4k。

Conclusion: 在GRH下且m,k足够大时，短区间Waring-Goldbach问题基本得到解决。

Abstract: Let N be a sufficiently large odd integer. We study the representation of a large integer m N^k as a sum of m almost equal k-th powers of primes, where the common distribution scale is at least N^theta. Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH), we prove that for any epsilon > 0 one may take theta > 1/2, provided that m < k(k+1)/2 and that m, k are odd integers sufficiently large. This is achieved by employing a new tool closely related to earlier work. For sufficiently large m, by refining the discussion of the minor arcs, we further show that under GRH the equation has a solution provided m > 16k log k + 4k + 2. Moreover, we give an asymptotic formula for the number of solutions, with main term involving N^{2k theta (1 - (1 - 1/k)^{m_1}) + theta(m-1) + 1 - k}, where m_1 = 4k. Under GRH and for sufficiently large m, k, the short-interval Waring-Goldbach problem is essentially settled.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bianchi流形上的素数测地定理此前缺乏较好的无条件误差估计，其关键在于对称平方L-函数二阶矩在平均意义下的Lindelöf型上界。

Method: 解析数论方法：建立对称平方L-函数二阶矩的均值估计，证明其平均Lindelöf假设。

Result: 在素数测地定理中得到无条件误差界O(x^{36/23+ε})（36/23≈1.565）。

Conclusion: 对称平方L-函数二阶矩的平均Lindelöf假设成立，并可直接导出Bianchi流形上素数测地定理的改进无条件误差估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the mean Lindelöf hypothesis for the second moment of symmetric square $L$-functions and thereby obtain the (unconditional) error bound $O (x^{36/23+\varepsilon})$ in the prime geodesic theorem on Bianchi manifolds.

</details>

*Zhengheng Bao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出椭圆曲面 $y^2=x^3+At^6+Bt^3+C$ 在 $\mathbb{Q}(t)$ 上的秩的显式公式及生成元（至有限指数），推广了 Kloosterman (2026) 与 Desjardins–Naskrecki (2024) 的 $y^2=x^3+At^6+C$ 情形，并提出一种在几何 Mordell–Weil 群不再分解为秩一子模时仍适用的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Kloosterman 2026、Desjardins–Naskrecki 2024）仅处理较窄的曲面族 $y^2=x^3+At^6+C$，其几何 Mordell–Weil 群作为 $\mathbb{Z}[\omega]$-模可分解为秩一子模。对于更一般的三元系数族 $y^2=x^3+At^6+Bt^3+C$，这种分解不再成立，现有方法失效，因此需要一个更通用的求解秩与生成元的框架。

Method: 作者针对 $y^2=x^3+At^6+Bt^3+C$ 这一更大的椭圆曲面类，利用几何 Mordell–Weil 群的生成元推导 $\mathbb{Q}(t)$ 上秩与生成元（至有限指数）的显式公式。关键创新在于：即使几何 Mordell–Weil 群（作为 $\mathbb{Z}[\omega]$-模）无法按既有方法分解为秩一子模，该新方法依然适用。整个证明仅使用生成元坐标的 Galois 群中的少量元素，且手工计算量很轻。

Result: 得到了所有非平凡椭圆曲面 $y^2=x^3+At^6+Bt^3+C$ 在 $\mathbb{Q}(t)$ 上的秩的显式公式，并给出其生成元（至有限指数），覆盖了比 Kloosterman (2026) 和 Desjardins–Naskrecki (2024) 更广的曲面类。

Conclusion: 本文推广了椭圆曲面秩公式的适用范围，提出了一种不依赖几何 Mordell–Weil 群秩一分解的新证明方法，且计算负担轻，为处理更一般的椭圆曲面提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we give an explicit formula for the rank and generators (up to finite index) over $\mathbb{Q}(t)$ of all non-trivial elliptic curves of the form $y^2=x^3+At^6+Bt^3+C$, which is a larger class of elliptic surfaces than the one in Kloosterman's paper (2026) and Desjardins and Naskrecki's paper (2024), namely $y^2=x^3+At^6+C$. Our proof provides a new method to find this formula using generators of the geometric Mordell-Weil group even in the case where this geometric Mordell-Weil group (which are $\mathbb{Z}[ω]$-modules) no longer decomposes into rank-one submodules by the methods in Kloosterman's paper (2026) and Desjardins and Naskrecki's paper (2024). Moreover, our proof uses only a few elements in the Galois group of the coordinates of the generators and only light computations by hand.

</details>


### [72] [Number fields as curves over $\mathbf{F}_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16360)
*Igor V. Nikolaev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上单变量函数域，证明 \(\mathbf{Q}\) 的 Galois 扩域与作为 Deitmar 概型的 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上曲线同构，给出亏格、尖点数与单位根之间的显式公式，并分类了 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上单尖点椭圆曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 \(\mathbf{F}_1\)（一元域）框架下的函数域理论，建立 \(\mathbf{Q}\) 的 Galois 扩域与 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上曲线（Deitmar 概型）之间的对应关系，并揭示数域算术不变量（单位根、代数曲线亏格与尖点）之间的显式联系；同时分类 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上的椭圆曲线。

Method: 将 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 延拓为 \(\mathbf{F}_{1^m}\)（阶 \(m\ge1\)），在 Deitmar 概型意义下研究曲线；借助 Drinfeld 模在 Hilbert 空间上有界线性算子上的表示，以及 Cuntz-Krieger 代数的交叉积结构完成证明。

Result: 证明 \(\mathbf{Q}\) 的 Galois 扩域与 Deitmar 意义下 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上曲线同构；得到 \(\mathbf{F}_{1^m}\) 上代数曲线的亏格与尖点数同相应数域的 \(m\) 次单位根之间的显式公式；推出 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 上单尖点椭圆曲线只能是分圆域或二次数域的极大阿贝尔非分歧扩域。

Conclusion: 该工作将 \(\mathbf{F}_1\) 几何与数域 Galois 理论相统一，通过算子代数与 Drinfeld 模工具实现数值不变量间的精确对应，并对单尖点椭圆曲线给出完整分类。

Abstract: We study function fields in one variable over the field with one element $\mathbf{F}_1$. It is proved that the Galois extensions of $\mathbf{Q}$ are isomorphic to the curves over $\mathbf{F}_1$ being understood as the Deitmar schemes. Specifically, one gets explicit formulas linking the genus and the number of cusps of an algebraic curve over the extension $\mathbf{F}_{1^m}$ of order $m\ge 1$ of the field $\mathbf{F}_1$ and the $m$-th roots of unity of the corresponding number field. It follows that elliptic curves with one cusp over $\mathbf{F}_1$ are either the cyclotomic fields or the maximal abelian unramified extensions of the quadratic number fields. Our proof depends on representaion of the Drinfeld modules by the bounded linear operators on a Hilbert space and the crossed product structure of the Cuntz-Krieger algebras.

</details>

Method: 通过显式构造的方法，设计（"construct"）一个可计算无理数，使其连分数展开的熵和对所有整数b≥2的b进制展开的熵同时保持较小。

Result: 成功构造出同时具有小连分数熵和所有整数进制下小b进制熵的可计算无理数，从而正面回答了Bugeaud的问题。

Abstract: We construct a computable irrational number that simultaneously has small continued fraction entropy and base $b$ entropy for every integer base $b\geq 2.$ This addresses a question raised by Bugeaud.

</details>


### [74] [Elementary proofs of congruences modulo 5 for overpartitions with restricted odd differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16376)
*Bishnu Paudel,James A. Sellers,Haiyang Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了 $\overline{t}(80n+40)\equiv \overline{t}(80n+60)\equiv 0 \pmod{5}$ 这组同余式的纯初等证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanson 和 Smith 在 2023 年借助模形式理论证明了关于 $\overline{t}(n)$ 的两个模 5 同余式，作者希望摆脱模形式等高等工具，提供一个真正初等的证明。

Method: 采用初等方法（结合 overpartition 的组合性质与生成函数/级数操作等初等技巧）来证明该组同余式，避免使用模形式理论。

Result: 成功给出了 $\overline{t}(80n+40)\equiv \overline{t}(80n+60)\equiv 0 \pmod{5}$ 的初等证明。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Hanson 与 Smith 的模 5 同余结果提供了不依赖模形式的初等证明，深化了对 $\overline{t}(n)$ 算术性质的理解。

Abstract: In 2015, Bringmann, Dousse, Lovejoy, and Mahlburg defined the function $\overline{t}(n)$ to be the number of overpartitions of weight $n$ where (i) the difference between two successive parts may be odd only if the larger part is overlined and (ii) if the smallest part is odd then it is overlined. In their work, they proved that $\overline{t}(n)$ satisfies an elegant congruence modulo 3. Since then, a number of authors have studied arithmetic properties satisfied by $\overline{t}(n)$. In particular, in 2023, Hanson and Smith utilized the theory of modular forms to prove the following two congruences modulo 5: For all $n\geq 0$, \begin{align*} \overline{t}(80n+40)\equiv \overline{t}(80n+60)\equiv 0 \pmod{5}. \end{align*} Our goal in this work is to provide a truly elementary proof of this pair of congruences.

</details>

*Faustin Adiceam,Volodymyr Pavlenkov,Evgeniy Zorin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了此前关于有理点分布的“振荡原理”猜想的一个弱化形式，对一大类确定性分形曲线成立，首次给出了其有界分母有理点数的精确计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前研究布朗运动附近的有理点分布时，猜想存在一个“振荡原理”支配单调分形曲线图像附近有界分母有理点数的渐近行为，需要验证该猜想并拓展到确定性分形曲线。

Method: 以确定性分形曲线为对象，证明该猜想的一个较弱形式对广泛一类曲线成立，包括经典Takagi与Weierstrass处处不可微函数，以及在给定Hölder连续指数下“大”范畴（普遍）的一类函数。

Result: 对上述确定性分形曲线，建立了所考虑有理点数的精确计数，这是首次在确定性分形曲线情形下得到该精确计数。

Conclusion: 弱化后的振荡原理猜想在广泛的确定性分形曲线类上成立，覆盖Takagi、Weierstrass函数及一大类Hölder连续函数，为分形曲线上的有理点计数提供了首个精确结果。

Abstract: In their previous work devoted to the distribution of rational points near Brownian motion, the authors conjectured the existence of an \emph{oscillation principle} governing the asymptotic behavior of the number of rational points with bounded denomi\-nators near the graph of a monofractal curve. In this note, a weaker form of this conjecture is shown to hold for a broad class of deterministic fractal curves. These include the classical Takagi and Weierstrass nowhere differentiable functions, and indeed a prevalent (i.e.~"large") class of functions among those which are Hölder continuous with a given exponent of regularity. This constitutes the first instance of deterministic fractal curves for which a precise count of the rational points under consideration is established.

</details>


### [76] [Local Langlands functoriality for Yu's supercuspidals I: Kaletha's parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16381)
*Sean Cotner,Tony Feng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文是关于Fargues–Scholze对应显式计算的系列论文中的第一篇，为Yu构造产生的所有尖点表示构造了半单惰性L-参数，并给出该对应在函子性性质下的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致力于Fargues–Scholze对应的显式计算问题。受Kaletha关于非奇异超尖点表示显式局部Langlands对应的启发，希望将这类显式对应推广到更一般的尖点表示并支持任意系数。

Method: 受Kaletha显式局部Langlands对应启发并加以扩展，对来自Yu构造的所有尖点表示构造半单惰性L-参数（允许任意系数），随后通过若干函子性性质建立该对应的刻画。

Result: 为Yu构造产生的所有尖点表示构造了半单惰性L-参数，并证明了该对应可由一组函子性性质唯一刻画。

Conclusion: 所建立的函子性刻画将在后续论文中用于计算限制到惯性群后的Fargues–Scholze对应。

Abstract: This is the first of two papers dedicated to the explicit computation of the Fargues--Scholze correspondence. In this first part, we construct (inspired by, and extending, Kaletha's explicit Local Langlands Correspondence for non-singular supercuspidal representations) semisimple inertial L-parameters for all cuspidal representations arising from Yu's constructions, with arbitrary coefficients. We then establish a characterization of this correpondence in terms of certain functoriality properties. This characterization will be used in the sequel paper in order to compute the Fargues--Scholze correspondence after restriction to inertia.

</details>


### [77] [Local Langlands functoriality for Yu's supercuspidals II: Fargues--Scholze's parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16387)
*Sean Cotner,Tony Feng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文是显式计算 Fargues–Scholze 对应系列的第二篇，通过计算来自 Yu 构造的尖点表示的 Tate 上同调，结合模函子性与第一篇的部分刻画，比较了 Fargues–Scholze 与 Kaletha 参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式计算 Fargues–Scholze 对应，并厘清其与 Kaletha 参数化之间的关系，从而刻画该对应的像与纤维性质。

Method: 计算 Yu 构造所得尖点表示的 Tate 上同调；结合局部 Langlands 对应中的模函子性，以及第一篇论文建立的部分刻画，比较 Fargues–Scholze 与 Kaletha 参数化。

Result: 在某个预期由后续工作补足的假设下，推出 Fargues–Scholze 对应具有有限纤维，且满射到惯性 L-参数上。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了 Fargues–Scholze 对应的显式理解，并揭示其与 Kaletha 参数化的一致性及像的结构性质。

Abstract: This is the second of two papers dedicated to the explicit computation of the Fargues--Scholze correspondence. We compute the Tate cohomology of cuspidal representations arising from Yu's construction. Combining this with modular functoriality in the Local Langlands Correspondence, and the partial characterization of the Local Langlands Correspondence established in the first paper, we compare the Fargues--Scholze and Kaletha parametrizations. Among the consequences (and under an assumption expected to be supplied in forthcoming work), we deduce that the Fargues--Scholze correspondence has finite fibers and is surjective onto inertial L-parameters.

</details>


### [78] [Twisted Rational Zeros and Local-Global Principles for Linear Recurrence Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16392)
*Piotr Bacik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Skolem猜想（指数局部-全局原理），构造反例否定其"限制模素数幂"的强化形式，并刻画扭有理零点与p进零点的关系；在p进Schanuel猜想下证明两个互素LRS同时零点的局部-全局原理，并无条件回答了Bilu等人的两个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skolem问题（线性递推序列是否存在零项）的可判定性长期悬而未决。Bilu等（2022）证明：在弱p进Schanuel猜想与Skolem猜想下，简单LRS的Skolem问题可判定。因此理解Skolem猜想本身成为关键目标；本文旨在厘清该猜想的结构，特别是其"模素数幂"强化形式是否成立，以及p进零点与扭有理零点之间的相互作用。

Method: 通过显式构造反例，证明将Skolem猜想中的模数m限制为素数幂的自然强化形式不成立；引入并分析p进零点（Bacik等研究）与扭有理零点（Bilu等引入）之间的关系；给出扭有理零点在无穷多素数p上是否构成p进零点的无条件刻画；据此证明两个互素LRS同时零点的局部-全局原理（依赖p进Schanuel猜想）；并给出启发式论证支持新猜想。

Result: 1）构造反例表明"仅限制m为素数幂"的强化Skolem猜想为假；2）该失败可由p进零点与扭有理零点之间的关系解释；3）在p进Schanuel猜想下证明两个互素LRS同时零点的局部-全局原理；4）无条件刻画扭有理零点何时成为无穷多素数下的p进零点；5）回答了Bilu等（2025）的两个公开问题。

Conclusion: 扭有理零点的存在是上述强化Skolem猜想失效的唯一途径，作者据此提出新猜想并给出启发式支持；该工作深化了对Skolem猜想及LRS零点结构的理解。

Abstract: The Skolem Problem asks whether a given linear recurrence sequence (LRS) has a zero term, and is equivalent to proving an effective version of the Skolem-Mahler-Lech theorem, which states that a non-degenerate LRS has finitely many zeros. Decidability of the Skolem Problem however, has remained open for many decades.
  Bilu et al. (2022) showed that the Skolem Problem for simple LRS is decidable subject to the weak $p$-adic Schanuel Conjecture and the Skolem Conjecture (also known as the exponential local-global principle), the latter of which states that an LRS has an integer zero if and only if it has a zero modulo every integer $m$. This paper works towards understanding the Skolem Conjecture. We provide an example showing that a natural strengthening of the Skolem Conjecture, where we restrict $m$ to be a prime power, is false. This failure is accounted for by the relationship between $p$-adic zeros of LRS (which arise naturally in the proof of the Skolem--Mahler--Lech theorem and have been studied algorithmically by Bacik et al. (2026)) and twisted rational zeros (introduced by Bilu et al. (2025)).
  By studying this relationship further, we are able to prove the main result of this paper: a local-global principle for simultaneous zeros of two coprime LRS, subject to the $p$-adic Schanuel Conjecture. A fundamental step in proving this result is characterising when twisted rational zeros are (or are not) $p$-adic zeros for infinitely many primes $p$, which we do unconditionally. This also answers two open questions of Bilu et al. (2025). Finally, we conjecture that the existence of twisted rational zeros is the only way that the aforementioned strengthened Skolem Conjecture may fail, which is supported by a heuristic argument.

</details>


### [79] [On Sarnak--Strömbergsson conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17356)
*Senping Luo,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维体积归一化格上的theta函数与Epstein zeta函数极小化问题，证明theta函数在α>1时由FCC格极小化、α<1时由BCC格极小化、α=1时两者皆可，且Epstein zeta函数在s>3/2时由FCC格极小化，从而证明了Sarnak–Strömbergsson猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维格点能量极小化在物理上有重要意义，Sarnak与Strömbergsson曾提出关于theta函数和Epstein zeta函数极小格（FCC/BCC）的不等式猜想，此前尚缺乏完整证明。

Method: 将格L的余体积固定为1，考虑正交变换意义下的等价类；通过分析theta函数Th(α,L)与Epstein zeta函数E(L,s)在三维情形下的极值性质，分别确定其极小化格随参数α、s变化的分段结果。

Result: 证明了argmin_{|L|=1}Th(α,L)：当α>1时为FCC格，α<1时为BCC格，α=1时为FCC或BCC格；并证明argmin_{|L|=1}E(L,s)在s>3/2时为FCC格。

Conclusion: 该结果完整证明了Sarnak–Strömbergsson猜想（其文第5节不等式(43)–(44)），刻画了三维归一化格上theta函数与Epstein zeta函数极小化格的具体结构。

Abstract: Let $\Th(α,L)=\sum_{v\in L}e^{-πα|v|^2}$ for
  $α>0$ and
  $E(L,s)=\sum_{v\in L\setminus\{0\}}|v|^{-2s}$ for
  $s>3/2$ be the theta and Epstein zeta functions associated to the lattice $L$, respectively. We are particularly interested in physically relevant dimension three. Fix the covolume of the lattice $L$ to $1$. Up to an orthogonal transformation, we prove that \begin{equation}\nonumber \argmin_{|L|=1}\Th(α,L)= \begin{cases}
  \boldsymbol{\mathrm{FCC}}\;\;\mathrm{lattice},\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\; \;\;\;\;\;\;&\text{if}\;\; α>1,
  \boldsymbol{\mathrm{BCC}}\;\;\mathrm{lattice}, \;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;&\text{if}\;\; α<1,
  \boldsymbol{\mathrm{FCC}}\;\;\mathrm{or}\;\; \boldsymbol{\mathrm{BCC}}\;\;\mathrm{lattice},\;\;&\text{if}\;\;α=1, \end{cases} \end{equation} and \begin{equation}\nonumber \argmin_{|L|=1}E(L,s)=
  \boldsymbol{\mathrm{FCC}}\;\;\mathrm{lattice},\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\; \;\;\;\;\;\;\text{if}\;\; s>3/2. \end{equation} Therefore, we prove the Sarnak--Strömbergsson conjecture \cite[Inequalities (43)--(44), Section 5]{SS}.

Abstract: We determine the Aharonov invariants of order at least two of every elliptic double cover by evaluating its projective kernel. In orders at least three, the formula separates the invariant into a constant Eisenstein term and an Eisenstein--Kronecker function evaluated under multiplication by two. The formula determines the ramification principal parts, torsion specializations, and isogeny traces, including the correction from two-torsion in an isogeny kernel. It also realizes the same intrinsic de Rham tensor through every degree-two projection: in higher orders this is the image of an Eisenstein section, while order two gives the classical Weierstrass complement to the Hodge line. A separate differential calculation shows that the Bernoulli-normalized invariant of order $p-1$ reduces to the quotient of the $p$th and first iterates of the defining derivation. For an elliptic invariant derivation this quotient is the Hasse invariant, independently of the rational function on its separable locus. The universal double cover provides a global specialization, integral away from two-torsion, whose reduction extends regularly across that locus. Classical Eisenstein zero theorems and the supersingular divisor congruence then describe the exactness loci and their reduction.

</details>


### [81] [The Furstenberg-Sárközy theorem for sums of an even number of odd powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16595)
*Alexandros Kalogirou,Andrew Lott,Ákos Magyar,Akash Singha Roy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数集 $A\subset[N]$ 的差集 $A-A$ 不含 $s$ 个正整数的 $k$ 次幂之和（$k>1$ 为奇数，$s>0$ 为偶数）的情形，证明了 $A$ 满足幂次节省上界 $|A|\ll N^{1-\frac1k\min\{s\sigma_k,1/2\}+\varepsilon}$，并通过贪心构造说明该界在形态上是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Furstenberg-Sárközy 定理刻画了差集避开平方数的集合的稀疏性。本文旨在将其推广到差集避开 $s$ 个正整数 $k$ 次幂之和（奇数 $k$、偶数 $s$）的更一般情形，并给出定量化的幂次节省界，以刻画此类集合的最大可能密度。

Method: 采用经典一变量 Weyl 和的估计，引入可容许节省量 $\sigma_k>0$，并在条件 $k>1$ 奇数、$s>0$ 偶数的结构下对 $A$ 的差集回避性质进行分析，推导出密度上界 $|A|\ll N^{1-\frac1k\min\{s\sigma_k,1/2\}+\varepsilon}$；同时给出贪心构造证明下界匹配。

Result: 证明了幂次节省上界 $|A| \ll N^{1-\frac1k\min\{s \sigma_k, 1/2\}+\varepsilon}$（对任意固定 $\varepsilon>0$），其中可取 $\sigma_k=\max\{2^{1-k}, \frac{1}{k(k-1)}\}$；贪心构造给出 $|A|\gg N^{1-s/k}$ 且 $A-A$ 不含 $s$ 个正 $k$ 次幂之和的集合，表明该上界形态正确。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Furstenberg-Sárközy 型定理推广至差集回避 $s$ 个正 $k$ 次幂之和的情形，给出了与构造下界相匹配的幂次节省上界，说明所得界在指数形态上是最优的。

Abstract: We obtain a Furstenberg-Sárközy-type result for sets $A\subset [N]$ whose difference set $A-A$ does not contain the sum of $s$-many $k$-th powers of positive integers, with $k>1$ odd and $s>0$ even. Namely, we prove that such sets must satisfy a power-saving bound $|A| \, \ll \, N^{1-\frac1k\min\{s \, σ_k, \, 1/2\}+ε}$ for any fixed $ε>0$, where $σ_k >0 $ is any admissible saving in a classical one-variable Weyl estimate. In particular, we can take $σ_k=\max\left\{2^{1-k}, \, \frac{1}{k(k-1)}\right\}$ using the classical theory and the best currently available bounds for classical Weyl sums. A greedy construction produces a set $A\subset[N]$ with $|A|\gg N^{1-s/k}$ for which $A-A$ contains no sum of $s$-many positive $k$-th powers, so our power-saving bound is of the correct shape.

</details>

Abstract: We construct an infinite family of imaginary biquadratic fields with large class numbers. The construction of this family is done by composing two distinct families of imaginary quadratic fields: the first one is the family Q(sqrt(k^2-p^l)) introduced by Banerjee and Hoque [1], subject to specific constraints on the integer parameters, l, k and p, and the second one is the parametrized family of the form Q(sqrt(-F_(50s+25))) based on Fibonacci numbers, introduced by Kishi [2]. One of the main tools that is used is Kuroda's class number formula of imaginary biquadratic fields. We choose these two families with the sole aim to get high order elements in the class group of the resultant biquadratic fields.

</details>

Conclusion: 常数项情形可回收已知有限部分表示，本文的核心贡献在于将其推广到更高阶Laurent系数，并给出相应的极限公式、全阶关系与大阶渐近刻画。

Abstract: We study the Laurent coefficients of the Barnes multiple zeta function with complex parameters in a common open half-plane and a fixed holomorphic determination of the logarithm. At the highest pole, we derive explicit limit formulae for every regular Laurent coefficient in terms of finite multiple sums and logarithmic correction terms. At the lower possible poles, we establish all-order relations with the Taylor coefficients at the origin and their parameter derivatives. We also determine the large-order behavior by subtracting all principal parts. The remaining function is entire, so Cauchy's estimate separates explicit residue contributions from a remainder that decays faster than any fixed geometric rate. The neighboring residues yield the limiting even and odd subsequences, with vanishing residues and cancellations accounted for. The constant-term cases recover known finite-part representations; the main focus is their extension to higher Laurent coefficients.

Abstract: We study the equidistribution in arithmetic progressions modulo powers of a fixed odd prime of Hecke eigenvalues of noncuspidal $\mathrm{GL}_3$ automorphic forms, with a focus on the convolution coefficients $λ_{1\boxplus f}(n)=(λ_f\star 1)(n)$,attached to a level $1$ holomorphic primitive cusp form $f$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在所考虑的群类（阿贝尔群、二面体群、半直积群 $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z} \rtimes \mathbb{Z}/(p-1)\mathbb{Z}$）上证明了 Defect Conjecture；并在多种情形下给出了相关 Bowen--Franks 群的 Iwasawa 不变量的具体值。

Conclusion: 结果推广了 Defect Conjecture 的已知适用范围，为相关群的 Bowen--Franks 群算术不变量提供了新的计算实例，有助于进一步理解该猜想与 Iwasawa 理论之间的联系。

Abstract: In this article, we prove the Defect Conjecture for Cayley graphs of abelian groups, dihedral groups, and groups of the form $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z} \rtimes \mathbb{Z}/(p-1)\mathbb{Z}$ where $p\geq 3$ is a prime. We also compute the Iwasawa invariants of the Bowen--Franks groups associated with these graphs in several cases.

</details>

Abstract: Let $p\geq 11$ and let \[ \barρ\cong \left(\begin{smallmatrix}ω&*\0&1\end{smallmatrix}\right) \] be a tr`es ramifi'ee nonsplit representation. For a primitive projective-augmented module of the corresponding Steinberg type, we determine the associated Iwahori and unramified dimensions and construct its ghost series. We prove that, at every point of the relevant component of $p$-adic weight space, the Newton polygon of the characteristic power series of the $U_p$-operator agrees with that of the ghost series. This establishes the local ghost conjecture in the tr`es ramifi'ee case. The main new feature is that the Iwahori power basis is indexed by a single arithmetic progression, so the Iwahori dimension may be odd. We establish the corresponding forms of ghost duality, the characterization of vertices by near-Steinberg ranges, and the finite-minor estimates required in the proof, treating both integral and half-integral centres and the fixed central index of the Atkin--Lehner involution. The global applications in the tr`es ramifi'ee case and the analogous local and global results in the peu ramifi'ee case are the subject of ongoing work.

Abstract: We study prime-power congruences for the prime-to-\(p\) Hecke eigensystems of normalized cuspidal level-one eigenforms as the weight varies, and we address the problem of classifying such systems for small prime powers. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次Hecke L-函数在导子方向（conductor aspect）的大值分布，量化特征值在半直线 1/2 附近可达到多大，以及有多少个特征能达到接近极大值的水平；并将结果推广到函数域情形，同时给出固定带内与 s=1 处的界。

Method: 在固定数域 K 上假设有限阶Hecke L-函数的GRH；在固定射线类分量、导子范数约为 X 的条件下，借助共振方法（resonance estimate）构造检验函数以逼近大值。组合输入为基数 N 的稀疏无平方 Gál 集，保持已知首项常数 2，具有平方乘法能量 N^{2+o(1)}，该能量界反映相关布尔多项式次数较低。将能量界与共振估计结合得到特征的丰度下界。对特征域情形，用素数导子远离一个固定位点、奇特征的全局函数域上的二次特征作无条件类似论证；并另给固定带与 s=1 处的界，含Dedekind zeta函数留数与局部因子依赖。

Result: 对每个固定 A≥0，在 1/2 + A/log₂X 处有 max_χ L(...) ≥ exp{(e^{-A}+o(1))√(log X log₃X / log₂X)}；每个固定较小常数至少被 X^{1-o(1)} 个特征取到；在缓慢移动并趋近该常数的阈值处同样成立。函数域情形（奇特征、素数导子远离一个固定位点）得到无条件类似结果。另在固定带内和 s=1 处给出界，并明确其对Dedekind zeta函数留数与指定局部因子的依赖。

Conclusion: 二次Hecke L-函数在导子方向上的大值由 e^{-A} 型指数常数控制，且大值特征具有 X^{1-o(1)} 的丰度；稀疏无平方Gál集的低次布尔多项式结构产生的能量界是获得该丰度的关键；上述现象在奇特征全局函数域上亦无条件成立，并附带固定带与 s=1 的定量界。

Abstract: We study large values of quadratic Hecke $L$-functions in the conductor aspect. Let $K$ be a fixed number field, and assume GRH for its finite-order Hecke $L$-functions. In a fixed ray class component with conductor norm comparable to $X$, we prove that \[
  \max_χL\left(\frac12+\frac A{\log_2X},χ\right)
  \geq\exp\left\{(e^{-A}+o(1))
  \sqrt{\frac{\log X\log_3X}{\log_2X}}\right\} \] for every fixed $A\geq0$. Every fixed smaller constant is attained by at least $X^{1-o(1)}$ characters. The same count holds at a suitably slowly moving threshold approaching the displayed constant. The combinatorial input is a sparse squarefree Gál set of cardinality $N$, retaining the known leading constant $2$ and having square multiplicative energy $N^{2+o(1)}$. The energy bound reflects the low degree of the associated Boolean polynomial. Together with the resonance estimate, it yields the abundance bound. We also prove unconditional analogues for quadratic characters with prime conductor away from one fixed place over any global function field of odd characteristic. Finally, we give bounds in the fixed strip and at $s=1$, including the dependence on the residue of the Dedekind zeta function and the prescribed local factors.

</details>


### [90] [On $t$-sumfree sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16843)
*Daan van Berkel,Wieb Bosma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究 t-sumfree 序列（主要 t=2,3），从 t 个递增正整数出发，每次取最小的、不能表示为前面 t 个不同项之和的正整数，构造无限序列。核心问题是其最终周期性的猜想。作者给出验证给定预周期与周期正确的充分条件，对 2-sumfree 与 3-sumfree 的可观子类证明周期性，并报告大量 3-sumfree 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-sumfree 序列的最终周期性是一个公开猜想，缺乏一般性证明工具；需要给出可验证判定条件并拓展已证周期性的范围。

Method: 以“每次选最小不可由前 t 个不同项之和表示的正整数”定义序列；提出证明某给定预周期与周期确实成立的充分条件；据此对 2-sumfree 与 3-sumfree 的重要子类给出周期性证明；并对 3-sumfree 序列进行大规模计算验证。

Result: 建立了验证预周期/周期正确性的充分条件；证明了 2-sumfree 与 3-sumfree 中相当大子类的最终周期性；获得 3-sumfree 序列的广泛计算数据。

Conclusion: 为 t-sumfree 序列最终周期性猜想提供了可操作的验证框架与部分理论结果，并通过计算支持一般猜想。

Abstract: In this paper we explore the world of $t$-sumfree sequences, primarily for $t=2$ and $t=3$. Starting with $t$ increasing positive integers, build an infinite sequence by choosing as the next entry the least positive integer that is not the sum of $t$ distinct previous values. The main open problem concerns the question of the conjectured ultimate periodicity of these sequences. We formulate a sufficient condition to prove that a conjectured preperiod and period are correct. We also prove periodicity for a considerable subclass of 2-sumfree and of 3-sumfree sequences. Finally, we present the results of extensive computations on 3-sumfree sequences.

</details>


### [91] [Mixed moment of $GL(2)$ and $GL(3)$ $L$-functions in the level aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16893)
*Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文在水平方向建立了自守L函数混合矩的渐近公式，并推导出同时非消没结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自守L函数中心值的非消没是解析数论的重要课题。以往工作多关注单一L函数，而同时研究L(1/2,f)与L(1/2,sym²f)的混合矩及其同时非消没较少涉及，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者针对权和为k、素数水平q、实原特征nebentypus χ（模q）的尖形式f，在水平方向研究L(1/2,f)与L(1/2,sym²f)的混合矩，利用解析数论方法（如近似函数方程、Kuznetsov/Petersson公式等）建立带幂次节省误差项的渐近公式。

Result: 建立了L(1/2,f)与L(1/2,sym²f)混合矩的渐近公式，误差项具有幂次节省；作为应用，证明了当素数水平q充分大时L(1/2,f)与L(1/2,sym²f)同时非消没。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了自守L函数非消没的研究，为混合矩方法应用于同时非消没问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an asymptotic formula of mixed moments of $L(1/2,f)$ and $L(1/2,\mathrm{sym}^2 f)$ for cusp forms $f$ of weight $k$, prime level $q$ and real primitive nebentypus $χ$ modulo $q$ in the level aspect with a power-saving error term. As an application, we deduce the simultaneous non-vanishing of $L(1/2, f)$ and $L(1/2,\mathrm{sym}^2 f)$ for sufficiently large prime levels.

</details>


### [92] [An additive problem in connection with some quadratic real fields with class number one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16960)
*Alexandru Gica*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 寻找所有满足对每个奇整数 x（x²<p）均有 p−x² 至多含两个不同素因子的素数 p。完全解决 p≡1,5,7 (mod 8) 的情形，p≡3 (mod 8) 的情形仅余一个可能例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画素数 p 与其二次剩余结构之间的素因子约束，属数论中素数分布与二次剩余交叉问题，考察 p−x² 的素因子个数这一受限条件。

Method: 以素数 p 及其小于 p 的平方差 p−x² 为对象，对奇整数 x（x²<p）施加"至多两个不同素因子"条件，按 p 模 8 的剩余类分情形逐一讨论。

Result: 对 p≡1,5,7 (mod 8) 三类情形给出完全解答；对 p≡3 (mod 8) 给出解答，仅遗留一个可能的例外情形。

Conclusion: 按模 8 分类基本完成了全部素数 p 的确定，仅 p≡3 (mod 8) 存在一个未定例外，有待进一步排除或确认。

Abstract: Our aim is to find all the prime numbers $p$ such that $p-x^2$ has at most two different prime factors, for all the odd integers $x$ such that $x^2<p$. We solve entirely the cases $p\equiv 1,5,7 \pmod 8$. The case $p\equiv 3 \pmod 8$ is solved with one possible exception.

</details>


### [93] [Powers of the Thue--Morse Series: 2-Adic Valuations and Automatic Odd Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16966)
*Zhao Shen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Thue–Morse生成函数幂次系数的2-adic赋值及奇数部分的自动性，证明了在m=2^r (r≥1)与m=3·2^r (r≥2)时，odd(t_m(n)) mod 2^s 序列是2-自动的，并给出m=6时的精确赋值公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thue–Morse生成函数的幂次系数t_m(n)的2-adic性质是组合数论与自动序列理论的交叉问题。已有工作给出了m=2^r时ν_2(t_m(n))的赋值恒等式，但奇数部分模2^s的自动性尚不明确，且对更一般的m缺乏系统结果，因此需要推广到m=3·2^r及m=6等情形。

Method: 利用2-adic赋值ν_2与odd函数的分解，将t_m(n)的赋值与二项式系数ν_2(C(n+m-1,m-1))相联系；通过生成函数的代数性质与2-自动序列的有限自动机刻画，对m=2^r、m=3·2^r分别构造并验证odd(t_m(n)) mod 2^s的2-自动性；对m=6引入指示函数1_{n≡3 mod 4}修正赋值公式。

Result: 对m=2^r (r≥1)和m=3·2^r (r≥2)，证明序列(odd(t_m(n)) mod 2^s)_{n≥0}是2-自动的，且ν_2(t_m(n))=ν_2(C(n+m-1,m-1))；对m=6，证明ν_2(t_6(n))=ν_2(C(n+5,5))+1_{n≡3 mod 4}，并证明其奇数部分模任意2^s是2-自动的。m=2^r的赋值恒等式为已有结果，本文给出新证明并同时建立自动性。

Conclusion: Thue–Morse生成函数幂次系数的奇数部分模2^s在m=2^r、m=3·2^r及m=6等情形下具有2-自动性，赋值行为与二项式系数密切相关，部分情形需附加模4指示项修正，为更一般m的2-adic自动性研究提供了方法框架。

Abstract: Let $T(x)=\prod_{j\ge 0}(1-x^{2^j})=\sum_{n\ge 0}t(n)x^n$ be the Thue--Morse generating function, and write $T(x)^m=\sum_{n\ge 0}t_m(n)x^n$ for a positive integer $m$. For a nonzero integer $a$, let $ν_2(a)$ be the exponent of $2$ in $a$ and put $\operatorname{odd}(a)=a/2^{ν_2(a)}$. For every $s\ge 1$, we prove that the sequence $(\operatorname{odd}(t_m(n))\bmod 2^s)_{n\ge 0}$ is $2$-automatic when $m=2^r$, $r\ge 1$, or $m=3\cdot 2^r$, $r\ge 2$. In both families, $ν_2(t_m(n))=ν_2\binom{n+m-1}{m-1}$. The valuation identity for $m=2^r$ is known; our proof recovers it and also establishes the automaticity assertion. For $m=6$ we prove $ν_2(t_6(n))=ν_2\binom{n+5}{5}+\mathbf{1}_{n\equiv 3\pmod 4}$ and show that its odd parts modulo every $2^s$ are $2$-automatic.

</details>


### [94] [Refined conjectures on Fitting ideals of BDP Selmer groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17003)
*Chan-Ho Kim,Aprameyo Pal,Jishnu Ray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在温和假设下证明了椭圆曲线BDP Selmer群的主猜想（Kurihara精化Iwasawa理论意义下），并给出反cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-扩张有限子扩张上BDP Selmer群初始Fitting理想的结构，其由相应$p$-进模形式在CM点的取值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDP Selmer群的主猜想是Iwasawa理论中连接算术对象（Selmer群）与解析对象（p-进L函数/模形式）的核心问题。Kurihara的精化Iwasawa理论要求给出更精确的Fitting理想结构而非仅特征理想，此前结果多限于特征理想层面或特殊情形，因此需要建立精化意义下的强主猜想。

Method: 针对具有好归约的椭圆曲线，在温和假设下研究其BDP Selmer群；采用Kurihara精化Iwasawa理论框架，分析反cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-扩张的各有限子扩张上BDP Selmer群的初始Fitting理想，并将其与$p$-进模形式在CM点的取值相联系。

Result: 证明了BDP Selmer群的强主猜想（Kurihara精化意义下）：反cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-扩张有限子扩张上BDP Selmer群的初始Fitting理想的结构，完全由相应$p$-进模形式在CM点的取值所决定。

Conclusion: 该工作将BDP主猜想从特征理想层面推进到精化Fitting理想层面，建立了Selmer群初始Fitting理想与$p$-进模形式CM点取值之间的精确对应，为精化Iwasawa理论提供了新的证据与工具。

Abstract: Under mild assumptions, we prove the strong main conjecture for BDP Selmer groups of elliptic curves with good reduction in the sense of Kurihara's refined Iwasawa theory. In particular, the structure of the initial Fitting ideal of BDP Selmer groups over finite subextensions in the anticyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension is determined by the values of the corresponding $p$-adic modular form at CM points.

</details>


### [95] [Modular functoriality for finite groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17060)
*Sean Cotner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Deligne–Lusztig理论推广到一类可能为无限型的非连通约化群（源于Bruhat–Tits building中点稳定子的特殊纤维，称为paraductive群），计算了其表示Tate上同调的下界，并应用于Fargues–Scholze L-参数的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点稳定子的特殊纤维上的群往往是无限型的非连通约化群，经典Deligne–Lusztig理论无法直接处理；同时需要刻画非正规深度0尖点表示的Fargues–Scholze L-参数。

Method: 将Deligne–Lusztig理论推广到所谓paraductive群，计算其表示Tate上同调的显式下界，并借助Shintani下降、Lusztig限制与Glauberman对应建立联系；应用Feng的模函子性与Scholze的ℓ独立性。

Result: 给出了paraductive群表示Tate上同调的显式下界，并将其与Shintani下降、Lusztig限制及Glauberman对应相联系；进而计算出非阿基米德局部域上（可能野分歧）约化群的非正规深度0尖点表示的Fargues–Scholze L-参数。

Conclusion: 该推广为无限型非连通约化群提供了Deligne–Lusztig型工具，并实现了对非正规深度0尖点表示Fargues–Scholze L-参数的计算。

Abstract: We develop an extension of Deligne--Lusztig theory to certain (possibly infinite type) disconnected reductive groups arising from the special fibers of point stabilizers in the Bruhat--Tits building, which we call \emph{paraductive}. We then compute explicit lower bounds for the Tate cohomology of representations of paraductive groups, relating these to Shintani descent, Lusztig restriction, and the Glauberman correspondence. As an application, using Feng's modular functoriality and Scholze's independence of $\ell$, we compute the Fargues--Scholze L-parameters of non-singular depth $0$ cuspidal representations of a (possibly wildly ramified) reductive group over a nonarchimedean local field.

</details>
