Method: 摘要未给出具体技术路线。通常此类结果需结合高度理论、Arakelov几何、Vojta方法或p进方法，处理半阿贝尔簇（阿贝尔簇的扩张，可含环面部分）上的退化与平移行为。

Result: 建立了半阿贝尔簇上一致Mordell-Lang猜想的成立性，即将一致Mordell-Lang从阿贝尔簇推广到半阿贝尔簇。

Conclusion: 该结果为Mordell-Lang型一致性问题提供了新的普遍性证据，拓展了其在半阿贝尔簇上的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove the uniform Mordell-Lang conjecture for semiabelian varieites.

</details>


### [97] [A class formula for effective Anderson motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17321)
*Xavier Caruso,Quentin Gazda,Alexis Lucas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为任意有效Anderson模建立含变量的类公式，推广Taelman及Anglès–Ngo Dac–Tavares Ribeiro的工作，并猜想、证明弱形式及超收敛版本的类公式，导出L级数的强收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Taelman开创性工作及Anglès–Ngo Dac–Tavares Ribeiro的后续成果受限于可容许性假设与特定Anderson模，适用范围较窄，需推广至更一般情形。

Method: 对任意有效Anderson motive建立含变量类公式，去除可容许性假设；提出取行列式前的非行列式类公式猜想并证明其弱形式；证明超收敛类公式。

Result: 获得任意有效Anderson motive的含变量类公式；证明非行列式类公式的弱形式；证明超收敛类公式，并由此导出相关L级数的强收敛性质。

Conclusion: 类公式可推广至一般Anderson模，非行列式版本在弱意义下成立，超收敛类公式为L级数强收敛性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We establish a class formula with variable for arbitrary effective Anderson motives. This pursues the pioneering work of Taelman and extends the scope of the work of Angl{è}s-Ngo Dac-Tavares Ribeiro, removing the admissibility assumption and allowing for more general Anderson modules. In the same setting, we further conjecture a ''nondeterminantal'' version of the class formula which holds before taking determinants and prove a weak version of it. Finally, we prove an overconvergent version of the class formula and derive from it strong convergence properties for the associated L-series.

</details>


### [98] [Improved bounds for Szpiro's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17390)
*Hector Pasten*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在椭圆曲线Szpiro猜想方向，作者将最佳上界从 log Δ ≪ N log N 改进为 log Δ ≪ N log log N，并在半稳定情形证明 log Δ ≪ N，方法基于作者先前通过Shimura曲线研究abc猜想的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Szpiro猜想是数论中关于椭圆曲线判别式与导子关系的核心问题，并与abc猜想密切相关。此前所有椭圆曲线的最佳无条件上界 log Δ ≪ N log N 自2013年以来未有突破，而更强的 log Δ ≪ N log log N 仅在GRH下已知，因此需要给出无条件改进。

Method: 方法建立在作者此前为通过Shimura曲线逼近abc猜想而发展的理论之上。

Result: 对所有Q上椭圆曲线无条件证明 log Δ ≪ N log log N；对半稳定椭圆曲线证明更强的 log Δ ≪ N。

Conclusion: 该工作无条件改进了Szpiro猜想方向上的长期最佳界，并在半稳定情形取得更强估计，推进了通过Shimura曲线方法研究abc猜想的纲领。

Abstract: In the direction of Szpiro's conjecture for all elliptic curves $E$ over $\mathbb{Q}$, the current best bound is $\log Δ\ll N \log N$ where $Δ$ is the absolute value of the minimal discriminant of $E$ and $N$ is the conductor of $E$ (the implicit constant is absolute). This bound dates back to 2013. We obtain the stronger bound $\log Δ\ll N\log \log N$ which was previously known only under GRH. In addition, for semistable elliptic curves we prove $\log Δ\ll N$. Our methods build on the theory developed by the author for approaching the $abc$ conjecture via Shimura curves.

</details>


### [99] [Even-degree Hermitian Ikeda lifts via Fourier-Jacobi descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17396)
*Nobuki Takeda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 从Yamana的奇次数Hermitian Ikeda提升出发，通过其第一Fourier-Jacobi系数的theta分量构造偶次数Hermitian Ikeda提升，确定其整体Arthur参数与局部成分，并用整体重数公式给出无重数分解，证明同构类与Fourier-Jacobi指标无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶次数Hermitian Ikeda提升的整体Arthur参数与局部成分此前尚不明确，尤其是如何借助奇次数构造逐层递推，以及其表示分解是否依赖于Fourier-Jacobi指标。

Method: 以E/F为CM扩张、m≥2为偶数。取Yamana构造的m+1次奇次数Hermitian Ikeda提升，求其第一Fourier-Jacobi系数，从该系数的theta分量构造m次Hermitian Ikeda提升；确定整体Arthur参数及每个有限素位的局部成分；运用酉群的整体重数公式导出分解。

Result: 确定所构造提升的整体Arthur参数与各有限素位局部成分；得到U_{m,m}(A_{F,h})表示的一个显式无重数分解；证明其同构类不依赖于Fourier-Jacobi指标；当F=Q时，通过比较Fourier系数将该构造与Ikeda的偶次数Hermitian提升等同。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了偶次数Hermitian Ikeda提升的构造性描述与自守表示论刻画：其Arthur参数、局部成分与U_{m,m}上的无重数分解均被显式给出，同构类与Fourier-Jacobi指标无关，且在F=Q时与Ikeda经典构造一致。

Abstract: Let $E/F$ be a CM extension and let $m\geq2$ be an even integer. Starting from the odd-degree Hermitian Ikeda lift of degree $m+1$ constructed by Yamana, we construct a Hermitian Ikeda lift of degree $m$ from the theta components of its first Fourier-Jacobi coefficient. We determine its global Arthur parameter and its local constituents at every finite place. Using the global multiplicity formula for unitary groups, we obtain an explicit multiplicity-free decomposition of the resulting representation of $\mathrm{U}_{m.m}(\mathbb{A}_{F,\mathbf{h}})$ and show that its isomorphism class is independent of the Fourier-Jacobi index. When $F=\mathbb{Q}$, we compare Fourier coefficients and identify the construction with Ikeda's even-degree Hermitian lift.

</details>


### [100] [Hypothetical connection of the theta functions of Dirichlet characters with the real cyclotomic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17478)
*Yuri Matiyasevich*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文探讨了通过theta函数特殊形式的函数方程来研究Dirichlet L函数Lindelöf假设的一种可能途径，并基于数值数据提出了关于逆矩阵结构的若干猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Dirichlet L函数的Lindelöf假设寻找新的研究途径，探索利用theta函数特殊函数方程来估计临界线上的L值。

Method: 基于相应theta函数的一种特殊形式的函数方程；为估计L_χ(0.5+it)，求解特定线性方程组，其矩阵元素由theta函数及其导数级数的求和项构成；通过数值分析研究逆矩阵的结构。

Result: 数值数据揭示逆矩阵具有深层结构。对于模q的特征，当矩阵尺寸沿公差为2q的等差数列变化时，逆矩阵的某些元素趋于有限极限；且这些极限（在乘以√q缩放因子后）属于实分圆域ℚ(cos(π/q))。

Conclusion: 该数值证据支持提出一系列关于逆矩阵极限结构和代数特性的猜想，为Lindelöf假设的研究提供了潜在的代数结构线索。

Abstract: We consider a possible approach to the Lindelöf hypothesis for Dirichlet $L$-functions. It is based on a special form of the functional equation for the corresponding theta functions. To estimate $L_χ(0.5+it)$ we need to solve certain systems of linear equations. The entries to the corresponding matrices are formed by the summands to the series for theta functions and their derivatives. Numerical data suggest that the inverse matrices have a deep structure and allow us to state a number of conjectures. In particular, it seems that for a character modulo $q$ certain entries to the inverse matrices tend to finite limits when the sizes of the matrices run over arithmetical progressions with step $2q$. Moreover, these limits belong to the real cyclotomic field $\mathbb{Q}(\cos(π/q))$ (up to a scaling factor of $\sqrt{q}$).

</details>

### [101] [An inverse problem on eigenfunction triple products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16399)
*Carl Schildkraut,Romain Speciel*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文引入"N-乘积特征基"概念，通过限制特征函数两两乘积的线性组合项数来细化特征函数三重积与几何结构的对应关系，证明流形具有2-乘积特征基当且仅当它是平坦环面，并在有界度图上得到类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明，拉普拉斯特征函数的三重积代数结构可唯一确定流形的几何。本文旨在细化这一对应关系，通过引入参数N刻画乘积展开的复杂度，寻找能刻画特定几何（如平坦环面）的代数判据。

Method: 引入N-乘积特征基的定义：由特征函数构成，其两两乘积可表示为至多N个基元素的线性组合。围绕该定义建立流形几何与特征基代数结构之间的联系，并结合有界度图上拉普拉斯特征向量的类似框架进行分析。

Result: 证明流形具有2-乘积特征基当且仅当该流形是平坦环面；并在有界度图的拉普拉斯特征向量上得到相应结论。

Conclusion: 特征函数乘积展开的稀疏程度（以N度量）可作为区分几何结构的代数不变量，N=2恰好刻画平坦环面及其图论对应物。

Abstract: On a connected closed smooth Riemannian manifold, the algebraic structure of the Laplace eigenfunctions, as described by eigenfunction triple products, uniquely determines the geometry. We refine this correspondence by introducing the notion of an $N$-product eigenbasis, which consists of eigenfunctions whose pairwise products may be written as linear combinations of at most $N$ basis elements. We prove that a manifold admits a $2$-product eigenbasis if and only if it is a flat torus. We also prove an analogous result for Laplace eigenvectors of bounded-degree graphs.

</details>


### [102] [Weighted heat traces of the Dirichlet Laplacian on Lipschitz domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16746)
*Lucas Kersten*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了有界Lipschitz区域上Dirichlet Laplacian加权热迹的渐近展开，权重为到边界距离的幂乘以边界附近连续的有界函数，并给出加权Riesz平均的单项渐近与特征函数局域化测度的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加权热迹的渐近行为，以刻画边界附近的几何信息，并为加权Riesz平均与Laplace特征函数局域化提供分析工具。

Method: 对有界Lipschitz区域上的Dirichlet Laplacian，取权重为到边界距离的幂乘以边界附近连续的有界函数，推导其加权热迹的渐近展开；依据权重指数得到单项或两项渐近。

Result: 得到加权热迹的渐近展开；权重指数决定渐近为单项或两项；应用于任意阶加权Riesz平均的单项渐近，以及一类描述Laplace特征函数局域化的测度族的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该加权热迹渐近展开为加权Riesz平均渐近和特征函数局域化测度收敛提供了统一框架，拓展了Lipschitz区域上的谱渐近理论。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic expansion of a weighted heat trace of the Dirichlet Laplacian on a bounded Lipschitz domain. The weight is given by a power of the distance to the boundary times a bounded function that is continuous near the boundary. Depending on the exponent of the weight, we obtain one-term or two-term asymptotics. We present two applications, namely a version of one-term asymptotics of weighted Riesz means for arbitrary orders, as well as convergence results for a family of measures that describes localization of Laplace eigenfunctions.

</details>


### [103] [Irreducibility of the Bloch variety for periodic Schrödinger operators in arbitrary dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16763)
*Wencai Liu*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明周期薛定谔算子 $-Δ+V$（$V\in C(\mathbb{T}^d;\mathbb{C})$）的 Bloch 簇在模周期性意义下不可约，通过谱带函数全纯延拓与准动量整数平移实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch 簇的不可约性是周期薛定谔算子谱理论中的基本结构问题，关系到谱带能否被单一全纯分支统一描述；此前对该问题在复值连续势下的完整刻画尚不明确。

Method: 结合谱带函数沿虚方向的全纯延拓与准动量的整数平移；运用解析几何方法，并沿虚射线做扰动估计，证明经适当整数平移后所有不可约分支共享同一全纯谱分支。

Result: 对任意 $d\ge1$ 及 $V\in C(\mathbb{T}^d;\mathbb{C})$，Bloch 簇模周期性不可约；即所有不可约分支经整数平移后包含相同的全纯谱分支。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了周期薛定谔算子 Bloch 簇的模周期性不可约性，为谱带函数的整体全纯描述提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Let $d\ge1$ and $V\in C(\mathbb{T}^d;\mathbb{C})$. We prove that the Bloch variety of $-Δ+V$ is irreducible modulo periodicity. The proof combines holomorphic continuation of spectral band functions with integer translations of quasimomentum. Using analytic geometry and perturbation estimates along imaginary rays, we show that, after suitable integer translations, all irreducible components contain the same holomorphic spectral branch.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Endpoint Differentiability Moduli for Fully Nonlinear Elliptic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16326)
*Aelson Sobral,Eduardo V. Teixeira*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究一致椭圆方程 $F(D^2u)=f$ 在 $f\in L^p$ 下 Caffarelli 梯度 Hölder 正则性理论的临界端点缺口：经典理论仅给出 $\gamma<\min\{\alpha_H,\sigma_p\}$，当 $\alpha_H\le\sigma_p$ 时端点 $\gamma=\alpha_H$ 的可微性未被量化。作者证明在 $\alpha_H<\sigma_p$ 时解具有带对数缺陷的 Taylor 展开，并在临界情形 $\alpha_H=\sigma_p$ 得到带指数型缺陷的估计，从而在齐次可微尺度上触及端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caffarelli 全非线性正则性理论的经典推论是：当 $f\in L^p$、$p>n$ 时，$F(D^2u)=f$ 的粘性解局部属于 $C^{1,\gamma}$，对任意 $\gamma<\min\{\alpha_H,\sigma_p\}$。这里 $\alpha_H$ 是齐次方程 $F(D^2h)=0$ 梯度正则性的通用 Hölder 指数，$\sigma_p=1-n/p$ 是源项的尺度指数。在源受限情形 $\sigma_p<\alpha_H$ 下，$f$ 的奇性是决定性障碍且端点可达；但在齐次受限情形 $\alpha_H\le\sigma_p$ 下，经典理论只给出 $\gamma<\alpha_H$，端点可微性估计未被量化，形成端点缺口。该文旨在填补这一缺口，刻画解在极限齐次可微尺度 $\alpha_H$ 处的定量可微性。

Method: 作者为端点 Campanato 型递推引入一种新的尺度选择机制（scale-selection mechanism）。在 $\alpha_H<\sigma_p$ 时通过迭代控制得到带对数修正的点态 Taylor 展开；在临界阈值 $\alpha_H=\sigma_p$ 时，由于有限次对数幂无法闭合迭代，转而采用更缓慢的尺度选择，得到带指数型修正的可微性估计。

Result: 当 $\alpha_H<\sigma_p$ 时，解满足 $|u(x)-u(x_0)-Du(x_0)\cdot(x-x_0)|\lesssim |x-x_0|^{1+\alpha_H}\left(1+\log\frac{1}{|x-x_0|}\right)^m$，即在显式对数缺陷意义下达到齐次可微尺度。在临界阈值 $\alpha_H=\sigma_p$ 时，有限对数幂无法闭合迭代，但更缓慢的尺度选择给出 $|u(x)-u(x_0)-Du(x_0)\cdot(x-x_0)|=O\left(|x-x_0|^{1+\alpha_H}\exp\left(A\sqrt{1+\log\frac{1}{|x-x_0|}}\right)\right)$。两种估计都改进了经典亚端点 $C^{1,\gamma}$（$\gamma<\alpha_H$）的整族界，在极限齐次指数处量化了可微性。

Conclusion: 该文填补了齐次受限情形下 Caffarelli 正则性理论的端点缺口，证明解在极限齐次指数 $\alpha_H$ 处具有带对数型（或指数型）缺陷的定量可微性，优于所有经典亚端点结果。所提出的端点 Campanato 型递推尺度选择机制为凡由齐次理论主导极限光滑性的问题提供了一种通用工具，并讨论了所得对数缺陷的作用及可能的最优性。

Abstract: A classical consequence of Caffarelli's fully nonlinear regularity theory [L. A. Caffarelli, Ann. of Math. (2) 130 (1989), no. 1, 189-213] is that viscosity solutions of uniformly elliptic equations $F(D^2u)=f$, with $f\in L^p$, $p > n$, are locally $C^{1,γ}$ for every $γ< \min\{α_H,σ_p\}$. Here $α_H=α_H(n,λ,Λ)\in(0,1)$ denotes the universal Hölder exponent for gradient regularity of $F(D^2h)=0$, while $σ_p=1-n/p$ is the scaling exponent of the source term. In the source-limited regime $σ_p < α_H$, the singularity of $f$ is the decisive obstruction and the endpoint $γ=σ_p$ is attainable. In the homogeneous-limited regime $α_H\leσ_p$, however, classical theory only yields $γ< α_H$, leaving the limiting differentiability estimate unquantified. This is the endpoint gap addressed here. When $α_H < σ_p$, we prove that solutions admit pointwise Taylor expansions satisfying $|u(x)-u(x_0)-Du(x_0)\cdot(x-x_0)|\lesssim |x-x_0|^{1+α_H}\left(1+\log\frac{1}{|x-x_0|}\right)^m$. Thus the homogeneous differentiability scale is reached up to an explicit logarithmic defect. At the critical threshold $α_H=σ_p$, finite logarithmic powers no longer close the iteration; nevertheless, a slower selection of scales yields $|u(x)-u(x_0)-Du(x_0)\cdot(x-x_0)|=O\left(|x-x_0|^{1+α_H}\exp\left(A\sqrt{1+\log\frac{1}{|x-x_0|}}\right)\right)$. Both estimates improve the full family of classical sub-endpoint $C^{1,γ}$ bounds, $γ< α_H$, by quantifying differentiability at the limiting homogeneous exponent. The proof introduces a new scale-selection mechanism for endpoint Campanato-type recurrences, suggesting a flexible tool whenever the limiting smoothness is dictated by the homogeneous theory itself. We also discuss the role and possible optimality of the resulting logarithmic defects.

</details>


### [105] [Effective Dissipation and Asymptotic Stability in a Finite-Level Quantum System with Wave-Induced Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16327)
*Thierry Goudon*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究有限能级量子系统与波动方程耦合的Liouville-Von Neumann动力学，在整体能量守恒框架下揭示环境效应导致的量子相干衰减机制，并区分纯态与混合态情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境对量子系统的退相干作用通常以耗散形式出现，但直接引入耗散会破坏整体能量守恒。作者希望构建一个整体守恒的模型，将阻尼效应自然归结为系统与环境的能量交换，从而在无外部耗散的条件下解释量子相干性的衰减。

Method: 采用Liouville-Von Neumann方程描述有限能级量子系统，并将其与一个波动方程耦合以刻画环境；在整体能量守恒的约束下，分析该耦合系统的结构性质，识别使量子相干性产生有效阻尼的关键机制，并对纯态与混合态分别讨论。

Result: 给出了此类阻尼得以出现的若干关键机制与结构性条件，并明确区分了纯态与混合态两种情形下相干衰减的差异。

Conclusion: 在整体能量守恒的量子-波动耦合模型中，量子相干性的衰减可通过系统与环境间的能量交换实现，其发生依赖于特定的机制与结构条件，且纯态与混合态的表现存在本质区别。

Abstract: We study quantum systems modeled by Liouville-Von Neumann equations, with a finite number of energy levels. The system is coupled to a wave equation, intended to reproduce environmental effects on the quantum system. The whole dynamic is energy conservative, but damping effects are expected to be embodied into the energy exchanges with the environment. We analyse how these effects can effectively damp quantum coherences. We exhibit key mechanisms and structural properties for such a damping to arise, with a neat distinction between pure and mixed cases

</details>


### [106] [From qualitative smoothness to uniform estimates for fully nonlinear elliptic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16334)
*Aelson Sobral,Eduardo V. Teixeira*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文建立了一个将定性光滑性转化为一致定量正则性估计的通用机制：对于被方程自然缩放保持的紧类，定性正则性与定量正则性具有相同的临界阈值；在二阶情形，类上的几乎处处二阶可微即可推出统一 C^{2,α} 理论，并给出单算子的部分正则性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则性理论通常需要先验的定量控制（如定量模估计、逐点定量平坦度）才能得到一致估计；而许多问题中光滑性在定性层面可见（如可微性），但相应估计难以获得。作者希望弥合这一“定性可见、定量不可及”的鸿沟，建立定性光滑性与定量正则性之间的等价阈值机制。

Method: 核心技术路线是“紧性 + 尺度自适应平坦性改进”。证明结合了完全重整化类（full renormalized class）的紧性论证与尺度自适应的改进平坦度（scale-adaptive improvement of flatness），将各 profile 上分别可得的信息统一提升为整类一致估计；论证不使用对假设光滑性的任何定量控制。对于单算子情形，进一步采用单侧切向（one-sided tangent）版本。

Result: （1）一般机制：对被自然缩放保持的紧类，定性正则性与定量正则性临界阈值相同。（2）二阶情形：在整个对应中心凸包（centered hull）上仅仅二阶可微（mere twice differentiability）即可强制存在某 α>0 的统一 C^{2,α} 理论。（3）单算子部分正则性判据：每个使解二阶可微的点都是正则点，而奇异集具有通用的正余维数。

Conclusion: 定性光滑性本身足以在缩放不变的紧类中产生一致的定量正则性，二者阈值一致；该结论不依赖对光滑性假设的定量控制，为那些“光滑性可见但估计不可得”的问题开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We establish a general mechanism that turns qualitative smoothness into uniform, quantitative regularity estimates for fully nonlinear elliptic equations. The central conclusion is that, for compact classes preserved by the natural rescalings of the equation, qualitative and quantitative regularity have the same critical threshold. At second order, mere twice differentiability throughout the corresponding centered hull already forces a uniform $C^{2,α}$-theory for some $α>0$. A one-sided tangent version gives a new partial regularity criterion for a single operator: every point at which a solution is twice differentiable is regular, whereas the singular set has universal positive codimension. The proof combines compactness of the full renormalized class with a scale-adaptive improvement of flatness, converting information available separately at each profile into estimates uniform across the entire class. The argument requires no quantitative control of the assumed smoothness and opens a path in problems where smoothness is visible, but estimates remain out of reach.

</details>


### [107] [Well-posedness and Blow-up in a semilinear heat equation with variable-order Scarpi memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16411)
*Pu Yuan,P. A. Zegeling*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了Scarpi阶数从α₁指数变化到α₂的半线性热方程，建立局部与最大温和适定性，用高斯Kaplan矩量法证明有限时间爆破，并刻画以A^{-(p-1)/α₁}为标度的极大生命跨度及其跨区数值特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scarpi阶数随时间指数变化的分数阶扩散具有非自相似核与时间依赖记忆，现有Caputo端点模型难以刻画其过渡区间动力学；需建立适定性并量化过渡阶段对爆破时间与生命跨度的控制。

Method: 对抽象Scarpi–Volterra方程建立局部与最大温和适定性；对全空间热问题利用正预解族得到非负解、比较原理、质量恒等式与L∞爆破二择一；采用高斯型Kaplan加权矩量法将PDE归约为标量非线性Volterra不等式，无需核的点式下界；数值上用连续Laplace反演、非线性时空重标度比较Scarpi与Caputo端点。

Result: 当1<p<1+2/n时每个非平凡解有限时间爆破；任意p>1时足够大初值爆破。对u₀=Aφ，A→∞时极大生命跨度T_A≍A^{-(p-1)/α₁}，亚临界小振幅上界由长时阶α₂控制；积分记忆的斜率非单调变化，大振幅增长阈值趋近Caputo α₁模型，阈值时间比相对Caputo α₂非单调，过渡速率对阈值时间的提前/延迟依赖振幅大小。

Conclusion: Scarpi变阶模型在短时由初值阶α₁主导、长时由末端阶α₂主导，过渡区间寿命与阈值呈非单调行为，两端分别回到Caputo α₁与α₂模型，为变阶异常扩散的爆破分析提供了适定性与定量框架。

Abstract: We study the semilinear heat equation ${}^{S}D_0^{α(t)}u=Δu+u^p$ on $\RR^n$, where $p>1$ and the Scarpi order changes exponentially from $α_1$ to $α_2$, with $0<α_1,α_2<1$. We establish local and maximal mild well-posedness for abstract Scarpi--Volterra equations. For the whole-space heat problem, positive resolvent families yield nonnegative solutions, comparison, a mass identity, and an $L^\infty$ blow-up alternative. To study finite-time growth, we use a Gaussian version of Kaplan's weighted-moment method. It reduces the PDE to a scalar nonlinear Volterra inequality and requires no pointwise lower estimate for the non-self-similar Scarpi heat kernel. Consequently, every nontrivial solution blows up in finite time when $1<p<1+2/n$, and sufficiently large data blow up for every $p>1$. For $u_0=A\varphi$ with fixed nonzero $0\le\varphi\in L^1\cap L^\infty$, the maximal lifespan satisfies \[
  T_A\asymp A^{-(p-1)/α_1}
  \qquad(A\to\infty), \] while a subcritical small-amplitude upper bound is governed by the long-time order $α_2$. The lifespan bounds are expressed through the inverse of the integrated memory. In the numerical section, we complement these estimates by comparing the Scarpi dynamics with both Caputo endpoint models. Continuous Laplace inversion shows that the logarithmic slope of the integrated memory varies nonmonotonically across the transition. At large amplitudes, the growth thresholds approach those of the Caputo $α_1$ model. A nonlinear space--time rescaling probes the long-time regime and reveals nonmonotone threshold-time ratios relative to Caputo $α_2$. For fixed-width initial profiles, increasing the transition rate delays the prescribed growth threshold at larger tested amplitudes but advances it at smaller ones.

</details>


### [108] [A Differential Algebraic Framework for Boundary Layer $α$-Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16442)
*C. Valencia-Negrete,J. Flores,M. Perez,M. Romero de Terreros,M. Favela*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 通过刻画Prandtl与Leray-α边界层饱和幺半群的差异，并代数地区分Helmholtz算子作用，本文说明当矢量场修正被阻止在由微分滤波器确定的最小尺度时，可避免有限时间内达到无穷速度的解构造悖论，并获得施加Helmholtz算子的边界层壁面附近的Taylor展开系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于描述Prandtl边界层与Leray-α边界层各自饱和幺半群之间的差异，并从代数角度区分Helmholtz算子的作用，以处理边界层模型中有限时间内速度发散的悖论问题。

Method: 方法上，通过比较两类边界层的饱和幺半群、对Helmholtz算子作用进行代数区分，并将矢量场的修正阻止在由微分滤波器确定的最小尺度。

Result: 结果表明，当矢量场修正被阻止在微分滤波器确定的最小尺度时，可避免构造出有限时间内达到无穷速度的解这一悖论；同时能够获得施加了Helmholtz算子的边界层在壁面附近的Taylor展开系数。

Conclusion: 结论是，对饱和幺半群差异的刻画与Helmholtz算子作用的代数区分，为Leray-α边界层提供了一个正则化机制：在最小尺度处停止矢量场修正即可规避有限时间奇异性悖论，并支持壁面附近Taylor展开系数的求解。

Abstract: By addressing the problem of describing the differences between the saturation monoids of the Prandtl and Leray-$α$ boundary layers, and algebraically distinguishing the action of the Helmholtz operator, we can show that, when the modification of the vector field is halted at a minimum scale determined by the differential filter, the paradox of constructing a solution that reaches infinite velocity in a finite time is avoided. Furthermore, it allows us to obtain the coefficients of the Taylor expansion near the wall for boundary layers where the Helmholtz operator has been applied.

</details>


### [109] [On the Stability of Type II Blowup for the Keller-Segel System in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16467)
*Thomas Y. Hou,Xiang Qin,Peicong Song,Zirui Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究高维（d≥11）抛物-椭圆Keller-Segel系统在质量超临界情形下的有限时刻爆破。作者对每个整数l≥2构造了光滑径向对称解，其在爆破时刻附近以量化尺度λ(t)=c(T-t)^{l/γ(d)}集中归一化稳态Q，得到type II爆破，并给出稳定性与不稳定性方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller--Segel系统在质量超临界维数下的有限时刻爆破是核心问题，但其爆破速率与剖面结构（尤其是type II、稳态集中现象）缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在刻画高维下可实现的量化爆破速率族及其稳定性，从而给出稳态浓度速率的层级结构。

Method: 证明结合了由Q的代数尾部驱动的广义核展开、调制分析、强制性加权高阶能量估计，以及有限维拓扑论证；通过构造光滑径向对称解实现每个l≥2的爆破regime，并保持种群密度非负。

Result: 对于每个整数l≥2，构造出光滑径向对称解，其径向质量变量在量化尺度上集中稳态Q，爆破附近满足u(t,r)=λ^{-2}(t)[Q(r/λ(t))+ε(t,r/λ(t))]，λ(t)=c(T-t)^{l/γ(d)}(1+o(1))，其中γ(d)=½(d-2-√((d-2)(d-10)))。余项ε在局部L∞及高阶齐次Sobolev范数下趋于零，且因2l>γ(d)使得浓度尺度严格小于抛物尺度√(T-t)，故为type II爆破。

Conclusion: 第l个regime恰有l-1个不稳定的径向调制方向，并在余维l-1的合适正则径向初值类中稳定，从而得到高维Keller--Segel流的量化稳态浓度速率层级。该结果为超临界维数下的type II爆破提供了完整的速率与稳定性图景。

Abstract: We study finite-time blowup for the parabolic--elliptic Keller--Segel system on $\mathbb{R}^d$ in dimensions $d\geq11$, where the problem is mass supercritical. For every integer $l\geq2$, we construct smooth radially symmetric solutions whose radial mass variable concentrates the normalized stationary state $Q$ at a quantized scale. Each blowup regime can be realized by solutions with nonnegative population density throughout their classical lifespan. More precisely, near the blowup time $T$, \[
  u(t,r)=\frac{1}{λ^2(t)}\left[Q\left(\frac{r}{λ(t)}\right)
  +ε\left(t,\frac{r}{λ(t)}\right)\right],
  \qquad
  λ(t)=c(T-t)^{\frac{l}{γ(d)}}(1+o(1)), \] where $c>0$ and $γ(d)=\frac12\bigl(d-2-\sqrt{(d-2)(d-10)}\bigr)$. The remainder $ε$ converges to zero in local $L^\infty$ norms and in a range of high-order homogeneous Sobolev norms. Since $2l>γ(d)$, the concentration scale is strictly smaller than the parabolic scale $\sqrt{T-t}$, and the resulting blowup is of type II. The $l$-th regime has exactly $l-1$ unstable radial modulation directions and is stable within a codimension-$(l-1)$ class of suitably regular radial initial data. The proof combines a generalized-kernel expansion driven by the algebraic tail of $Q$, modulation analysis, coercive weighted high-order energy estimates, and a finite-dimensional topological argument. This yields a quantized hierarchy of stationary-state concentration rates for the high-dimensional Keller--Segel flow.

</details>


### [110] [Extending the Córdoba-Martínez-Zoroa IPM Blow-Up to Uniformly Space-Time Smooth Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16470)
*Levent Alpöge,Tristan Buckmaster,Matei P. Coiculescu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在二维环面上研究带外力项的IPM方程，借助Córdoba-Martínez-Zoroa的空光滑外力爆破技术，证明了在一致时空光滑外力情形下有限时间爆破的存在性：存在光滑奇初值密度、光滑奇外力以及经典解，使得密度梯度与速度梯度在L∞意义下于有限时间发散，但密度本身在C^η中收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有IPM方程爆破结果多针对空间光滑外力，本文旨在将相应技术推广到一致时空光滑外力情形，以刻画外力光滑性对爆破现象的影响，并揭示梯度发散与密度自身收敛并存的奇性结构。

Method: 采用Córdoba-Martínez-Zoroa的IPM爆破构造技术并进行适配，通过选取光滑奇的初始密度与光滑奇外力F∈C^∞([0,1]×T²)，构造[0,1)上的经典解并分析其梯度在L∞中的渐近行为。

Result: 证明存在光滑奇初值密度、一致时空光滑外力F∈C^∞([0,1]×T²)以及[0,1)上的经典解ρ，使得当t↑1时，ρ的密度梯度与空间速度梯度在L∞中发散；同时ρ(t)在每个C^η(0≤η<1)中收敛。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，即使外力在时空上一致光滑，IPM方程在T²上仍可发生有限时间爆破，且爆破表现为梯度发散而密度本身在低阶Hölder范数下仍保持收敛，说明奇性集中在导数层级而非密度函数本身。

Abstract: By adapting the techniques used in the IPM blowup result of Córdoba-Martínez-Zoroa with spatially smooth force, we prove finite-time blow-up for the IPM equation on $\mathbb T^2$ with a uniformly spacetime smooth force. In particular, we show there exist a smooth odd initial density, a smooth odd force $F\in C^\infty([0,1]\times\mathbb T^2)$, and a classical solution $ρ$ on $[0,1)$ whose density gradient and spatial velocity gradient diverge in $L^\infty$ as $t\uparrow 1$. Nevertheless, $ρ(t)$ converges in $C^η$ for every $0\leqη<1$.

</details>


### [111] [Finite inverse nodal problems for singular weighted Sturm--Liouville equations: variational selection and spectral matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16481)
*Zhibo Cheng,Yuchao He,Sunan Wang,Yonghui Xia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文建立了球内 Schrödinger 算子径向谱分支的有限径向逆结点理论：以径向本征函数的球面结点超曲面半径为结点数据，经精确径向约化得到权为 w(r)=r^{n-1} 的奇异加权 Sturm–Liouville 问题，并在匹配有限个给定球面结点半径的径向势集合上证明谱正则性、结点可微性、弱闭性、精确实现及有限余维 C^1 流形结构；同时指出缺少 q_0 变分选择时有限结点半径不能唯一确定势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作 \cite{HeWuXiaZhang2025} 仅针对正则一维 Sturm-Liouville 算子建立了有限数据逆结点框架，而多维 Schrödinger 算子的逆结点理论仍是公开难题。本文旨在填补这一空白，把逆结点理论从区间情形推广到球内径向谱分支。

Method: 将球内 Schrödinger 算子的径向谱分支精确约化，保留 PDE 结点几何，转化为权重 w(r)=r^{n-1} 的奇异加权 Sturm--Liouville 问题；以径向本征函数球面结点超曲面的半径为结点数据，在所有匹配有限个指定球面结点半径的径向势中最小化 PDE 距离 \|Q-Q_0\|_{L^p(B_R)}，等价于加权距离 \|q-q_0\|_{L^p_w(0,R)}，从而引入由 q_0 强制施加的变分选择。

Result: 对相应的结点约束集证明了谱正则性、结点可微性、弱闭性、精确实现以及有限余维 C^1 流形结构。关键发现是：若没有 q_0 带来的变分选择，指定的有限结点半径无法唯一确定势——容许势在常数平移下不变，且有限结点约束在正则点处产生局部无限维水平集。

Conclusion: 该文建立了球内 Schrödinger 算子径向谱分支的有限径向逆结点理论，给出了结点约束集的良态几何结构，并揭示有限结点半径的非唯一性；变分选择 q_0 对保证唯一性起关键作用。

Abstract: The recent work \cite{HeWuXiaZhang2025} introduced a finite-data inverse nodal framework for regular one-dimensional Sturm-Liouville operators. Nevertheless, formulating a multidimensional inverse nodal theory for Schrödinger operators remains a challenging open problem. This paper addresses this gap by establishing a finite radial inverse nodal theory for the radial spectral branch of Schrödinger operators in balls. In contrast with the interval case, the nodal data are the radii of spherical nodal hypersurfaces of radial eigenfunctions. The exact radial reduction preserves this PDE nodal geometry and leads naturally to a singular weighted Sturm--Liouville problem with weight $w(r)=r^{n-1}$, rather than to a regular interval model. We minimize the PDE distance $\|Q-Q_0\|_{L^p(B_R)}$ , equivalently the weighted distance $\|q-q_0\|_{L^p_w(0,R)}$, among all radial potentials matching finitely many prescribed spherical nodal radii. For the associated nodal constraint sets, we prove spectral regularity, nodal differentiability, weak closedness, exact realization, and a finite-codimensional $C^1$ manifold structure. Crucially, without the variational selection imposed by \(q_0\), the prescribed finite nodal radii fail to determine the potential uniquely: the admissible potentials are invariant under constant shifts, and the finite nodal constraints yield locally infinite-dimensional level sets at regular points.

</details>


### [112] [Asymptotic Stability of Multi-Solitons for Coupled Nonlinear Schrödinger Equations via the $\bar{\partial}$-Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16480)
*Yubin Huang,Liming Ling,Huajie Su*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究聚焦型耦合非线性薛定谔（CNLS）方程在加权Sobolev空间初值下的Riemann-Hilbert问题，借助Darboux变换消去离散谱，利用$\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法建立解的长时间渐近行为，并将误差估计提升至$\mathcal{O}(t^{-3/4})$量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNLS方程的长时间渐近行为是孤立子理论的重要问题，而既有改进的非线性最速下降方法在误差精度与多孤子稳定性刻画上仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于$3\times3$矩阵谱问题建立聚焦型CNLS方程的Riemann-Hilbert问题；利用Darboux变换消去初始RHP的离散谱；运用$\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法分析加权Sobolev空间初值下解的长时间渐近行为。

Result: 建立了CNLS方程解的长时间渐近行为，将误差估计改进至$\mathcal{O}(t^{-3/4})$量级；给出了多孤子解的渐近稳定性，并从Yang-Baxter映射的视角分析了多孤子碰撞规律。

Conclusion: 相比改进的非线性最速下降方法，本文在误差精度上有所提高，并成功将多孤子渐近稳定性与碰撞规律纳入Yang-Baxter映射的框架之中。

Abstract: The Riemann-Hilbert problem for the focusing coupled nonlinear Schrödinger (CNLS) equation is formulated on the basis of the corresponding $3\times3$ matrix spectral problem. We remove the discrete spectrum of initial RHP with the aid of Darboux transformations. Based on the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method, we establish the long-time asymptotic behavior of solutions to the CNLS equation for initial condition in the weighted Sobolev space. Compared to the improved nonlinear steepest descent method, we improve the error estimate up to order $\mathcal{O}\left(t^{-3/4}\right)$. Furthermore, we obtain the asymptotic stability of multi-soliton solutions for CNLS equation and analyze the law of multi-soliton collision in the view-point of Yang-Baxter map.

</details>


### [113] [Singular solutions to parabolic equations in one space dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17351)
*Pascal Auscher,Moritz Egert,Benjamin W. Kosmala*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一维复系数一致抛物型方程的奇异弱解，证明能量估计给出的时空可积性指标是精确的：对任意 p>6 都存在能量解在紧子集上不可 p 可积，从而说明 De Giorgi–Nash–Moser 理论中的局部有界性在复系数情形下一维即失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 De Giorgi–Nash–Moser 理论证明了实系数一致抛物型方程弱解的局部有界性。对于复系数情形，这一结论是否仍成立、能量估计给出的可积性是否最优，是一个悬而未决的基本问题。本文旨在澄清这一点。

Method: 通过显式构造一维标量线性一致抛物型方程（系数为复值）的奇异弱解，分析其能量解的正则性。利用时空可积性指标 p 的阈值行为，对每个 p>6 构造相应的反例，说明解在紧子集上不属于 L^p。

Result: 证明能量估计提供的时空可积性是精确的：对任意 p>6，都存在满足能量估计的解在紧子集上不可 p 可积；由此推出复系数下局部有界性在一维即失效。

Conclusion: 复系数一致抛物型方程的能量解正则性显著弱于实系数情形，De Giorgi–Nash–Moser 的局部有界性结论不能推广到复系数，且一维即出现反例，说明能量估计的可积性指标是尖锐的。

Abstract: We construct singular weak solutions to scalar, linear, uniformly parabolic equations with complex coefficients in one space dimension. Our examples show that the space-time integrability provided by the energy estimates is sharp: for every $p>6$, there exists such an equation admitting an energy solution that fails to be $p$-integrable on a compact subset of the space-time domain. In particular, the local boundedness of weak solutions from the De Giorgi--Nash--Moser theory fails for complex coefficients already in one space dimension.

</details>


### [114] [A Gauge-Covariant Semiclassical Limit from the magnetic Hartree--Fock equation to the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16511)
*Jacky J. Chong,Zhuohui You*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究三维空间中磁Hartree-Fock方程到磁Vlasov-Poisson方程的半经典极限，建立正则性传播并给出规范协变的收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半经典极限下刻画量子多体（Hartree-Fock）动力学向经典动理学（Vlasov-Poisson）的过渡，且需处理非均匀、纯空间依赖磁场带来的规范（矢势）选取依赖问题。

Method: 首先在指数加权假设下建立磁Vlasov-Poisson方程解的正则性传播；再结合规范协变的磁Weyl量子化框架，在半经典Schatten范数下推导显式收敛界，估计以磁场而非矢势表述。

Result: 得到规范协变的半经典收敛估计，即收敛界用磁场表达而与矢势选择无关；对合适的一类初始数据，在任意固定有限时间区间上收敛成立。

Conclusion: 磁Hartree-Fock到磁Vlasov-Poisson的半经典极限在三维、非均匀纯空间磁场下成立，且结果保持规范协变性与有限时间有效性。

Abstract: We study the semiclassical limit from the magnetic Hartree--Fock equation to the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation in three spatial dimensions in the presence of a nonuniform, purely space-dependent magnetic field. We first establish propagation of regularity for solutions of the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation under exponentially weighted assumptions. Building on this result and a gauge-covariant magnetic Weyl quantization framework, we then derive explicit convergence bounds for the semiclassical limit in semiclassical Schatten norms. In particular, the estimates are formulated in terms of the magnetic field rather than a particular choice of vector potential and are therefore gauge covariant. The resulting semiclassical convergence holds for a suitable class of initial data on any fixed finite time interval.

</details>


### [115] [Symmetry, monotonicity, and asymptotics of singular solutions to semilinear elliptic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16516)
*Xusheng Du,Hui Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带零牛顿容量的奇异闭集上的半线性椭圆方程奇异正解，证明了解的对称性与单调性，并给出对法向的渐近对称性，改进了Chen-Lin、Li和Sciunzi的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有关于奇异椭圆方程解对称性的经典结果（Chen-Lin、Li、Sciunzi），并克服原有证明方法的局限，推广其适用范围。

Method: 采用改进版的移动球面法（moving sphere method），对含奇异闭集Γ（零牛顿容量）的半线性椭圆方程 -Δu=f(u) 的奇异正解进行分析。

Result: 当Ω=R^n且Γ⊂{x_1=0}时，证明解关于超平面{x_1=0}对称且在x_1方向单调；当Γ为维数≤n-2的光滑闭流形时，证明解关于Γ法向的渐近对称性。

Conclusion: 所得结果显著改进了Chen-Lin、Li和Sciunzi的工作，所用改进移动球面法简化了分析并扩展了方法的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study singular positive solutions to the semilinear elliptic equation $$ - Δu = f(u) ~~~~~~ \textmd{in} ~ Ω\setminus Γ, $$ where $Ω\subset \R^n$ is a bounded or unbounded domain, and $Γ\subset Ω$ is a singular closed set with zero Newtonian capacity. When $Ω= \R^n$ and $Γ\subset \{ x_1 = 0 \}$, we establish the symmetry of singular solutions with respect to the hyperplane $\{ x_1 = 0 \}$ and their monotonicity in the $x_1$-direction. For the case where $Ω\subset \R^n$ and $Γ$ is a smooth closed manifold of dimension $\leq n - 2$, we show the asymptotic symmetry of singular solutions with respect to the normal direction of $Γ$. These results significantly improve those of Chen-Lin (Duke Math. J. 1995; Ann. Scuola Norm. Sup. Pisa Cl. Sci. 2001), Li (Invent. Math. 1996) and Sciunzi (J. Math. Pures Appl. 2017). Unlike their proofs, we employ an improved version of the moving sphere method, which also simplifies the analysis and extends its applicability.

</details>


### [116] [On the internal modes of the ground state for 3D cubic--quintic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16613)
*Gong Chen,Zhaojie Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究三维三次-五次Klein-Gordon与Schrödinger方程在内部模式存在下的基态渐近稳定性问题，分析ω→3/16端点时两类谱问题：标量Hessian的离散谱及NLS的Hamiltonian线性化中的内部模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维Klein-Gordon和Schrödinger方程基态在存在内部模式时的渐近稳定性问题尚缺严格的线性理论基础，需研究三次-五次非线性模型相应谱性质。

Method: 针对三维三次-五次椭圆方程的正径向基态Q_ω，令ω趋近基态分支端点3/16：一是确定标量Hessian L_+=-Δ+ω-3Q_ω²+5Q_ω⁴的完整离散谱，证明离散角动量扇区数目趋于无穷并给出位置与重数的精确渐近；二是分析三次-五次NLS的完整Hamiltonian矩阵线性化，证明内部模式存在。

Result: Hessian离散角动量扇区数目随ω→3/16趋于无穷，其位置与重数具有尖锐渐近；NLS内部模式恰存在于长度约为(3/16-ω)^{-1}的角动量扇区区间内。

Conclusion: 结果为三维三次-五次模型未来的非线性稳定性与辐射阻尼分析提供了严格的线性基础。

Abstract: Motivated by the problem of asymptotic stability for ground states of the Klein--Gordon and Schrödinger equations in three spatial dimensions in the presence of internal modes, we study the corresponding models with cubic--quintic nonlinearities. These equations arise in several physical contexts; in particular, the cubic--quintic Klein--Gordon equation appears naturally in the study of spin-$0$ particles in quantum field theory.
  More precisely, let $Q_ω$ be the positive radial ground state of the three-dimensional cubic--quintic elliptic equation \[
  -ΔQ_ω+ωQ_ω-Q_ω^3+Q_ω^5=0. \] We study two related, but logically distinct, spectral problems as $ω$ approaches the endpoint $3/16$ of the ground-state branch. First, we determine the complete discrete spectrum of the scalar Hessian \[
  L_+=-Δ+ω-3Q_ω^2+5Q_ω^4. \] This operator is also the exact linearized spatial operator around the static state $Q_ω$ for the real scalar cubic--quintic Klein--Gordon equation with mass parameter $ω$. We prove that the number of its discrete angular-momentum sectors tends to infinity, and obtain sharp asymptotics for their locations and multiplicities. Second, for the cubic--quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equation, we analyze the full Hamiltonian matrix linearization and prove the existence of internal modes in precisely an interval of angular-momentum sectors of length comparable to $(3/16-ω)^{-1}$. %The rotating complex Klein--Gordon problem leads %to a different gyroscopically coupled pencil; since that pencil is not %analyzed here, no identification of its internal spectrum is asserted. These results provide a rigorous linear foundation for future nonlinear stability and radiation-damping analysis in three-dimensional cubic--quintic models.

</details>


### [117] [Existence and Asymptotic Stability of Traveling Waves of Two-species Lotka-Volterra Competition-Diffusion Systems via Geometric Singular Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16624)
*Chueh-Hsin Chang,Tzi-Sheng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究具有小扩散率和小竞争系数的双物种 Lotka-Volterra 竞争-扩散系统，利用几何奇异摄动理论证明连接共存态与平凡态的行波前解的存在性，并给出单调性与稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察一种物种扩散率和竞争系数均较小的双物种竞争-扩散系统，旨在刻画连接共存态与平凡态的行波前解及其动力学性质。

Method: 采用几何奇异摄动理论（geometric singular perturbation theory）分析系统，构造行波前解并证明其存在性；进而分析解的单调性，并通过估计 Evans 函数研究线性稳定性。

Result: 证明了当行波速度大于最小波速时，连接共存态与平凡态的行波前解存在；并证明该行波前中两个物种的剖面均为单调的；同时给出相关的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 在该奇异摄动极限下，双物种竞争-扩散系统存在单调的行波前解，其稳定性可由 Evans 函数的估计得到刻画。

Abstract: We consider the two-species Lotka-Volterra competition-diffusion system where one species has a small diffusion rate relative to the other species and has a small competition coefficient. By the geometric singular perturbation theory, we prove the existence of the wavefront connecting the coexistence state to the trivial state, with traveling speed greater than the minimal speed. For such wavefronts, we show that the profiles of both species are monotone. Finally, we use the geometric singular perturbation theory to estimate the associated Evans function, and give the related stability results.

</details>


### [118] [Point vortex dynamics in quasi-periodic channels: transporting trajectories and ergodic distribution of equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16704)
*Mohamed Ali,Taoufik Hmidi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究无界平面通道中单点涡在准周期界面下的动力学，通过hull提升为环面周期问题，给出Robin函数的精确准周期表示，构造全局准周期不变图，并在Diophantine条件下将运动直化为常漂移；核心结果是利用紧致环面上Kronecker流的唯一遍历性建立带有限阶切触的零点计数定理，得到临界点渐近密度与相位分布的几何通量公式，并用数值验证周期/准周期模型中的分岔与相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于准周期几何下涡旋动力学缺乏基本空间胞元，经典周期性方法（胞元约化、Floquet理论、周期平均）无法直接应用。需要在无基本周期的情形下刻画涡旋的输运轨道与平衡点（Robin函数临界点）的空间分布，并给出可量化的密度与相位规律。

Method: 方法上引入hull表述，将准周期界面几何提升为有限维环面上的周期问题，从而得到Robin函数的精确准周期表示；在自然横截性条件下构造全局准周期不变图，证明每个边界分量邻域被此类图叶化；在空间频率满足Diophantine条件时沿图将动力学直化为常漂移。对平衡点分布，将Robin函数临界点识别为hull环面上Kronecker流对某超曲面的穿越，利用唯一遍历性证明允许有限阶切触的零点计数定理，导出渐近密度的几何通量公式及极限相位分布。

Result: 主要结果包括：得到Robin函数的精确准周期表示与全局准周期不变图，可实现输运型涡旋轨道的刻画与边界邻域叶化；在Diophantine条件下运动模平移为拟周期，沿图直化为常漂移；建立一般零点计数定理（允许有限阶切触），给出临界点渐近密度的显式几何通量公式与极限相位分布，且结果在临界hull构造后是非微扰的；通过显式周期与准周期模型揭示临界点分布的分岔与相变，数值计算定量验证了解析结论。

Conclusion: 结论：hull环面框架可将准周期问题化为紧致环面上的周期动力系统，使不动点分布与涡旋输运得到统一且非微扰的解析刻画；临界点密度与相位分布由Kronecker流穿越超曲面的几何通量控制。该框架可推广至更一般的准周期流体与哈密顿系统，并提示周期模型中的分岔与相变现象在准周期情形下具有稳健的结构性对应。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a single point vortex in an unbounded planar channel whose interfaces are quasi-periodic in the longitudinal direction. The motion is governed by the Robin function. We introduce a hull formulation that lifts the quasi-periodic geometry to a periodic problem on a finite-dimensional torus and yields an exact quasi-periodic representation of the Robin function. Under a natural transversality condition, we construct global quasi-periodic invariant graphs describing transporting vortex trajectories and show that a full neighborhood of each boundary component is foliated by such graphs. Under a Diophantine condition on the spatial frequencies, the dynamics along each graph can be straightened to a constant drift, so that the vortex motion is quasi-periodic modulo translation. A central contribution concerns the distribution of vortex equilibria. In the quasi-periodic setting, where no fundamental spatial cell exists, we identify critical points of the Robin function with crossings of a hypersurface by a Kronecker flow on the hull torus. Exploiting unique ergodicity, we establish a general zero-counting theorem, allowing finite-order tangencies, which yields an explicit geometric flux formula for the asymptotic density of critical points and their limiting phase distribution. The result is nonperturbative once the critical hull is constructed. Explicit periodic and quasi-periodic models reveal bifurcations and phase transitions in the critical-point distribution, while numerical computations provide quantitative validation of the analytical results.

</details>


### [119] [Local Well-posedness of Free Boundary Problems for the Euler--Monge--Ampère Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16717)
*Sicheng Liu,Tao Luo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究自由边界Euler--Monge--Ampère系统作为理想不可压缩流体运动的守恒松弛，证明了在凸初始域下的局部适定性与正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Euler--Monge--Ampère系统作为理想不可压缩流体运动的守恒松弛来研究，其力由与实际流体域耦合的Monge--Ampère方程及非线性边界条件决定。

Method: 对每个固定松弛参数，针对一致凸的C^{2,α}类初始域、C^{1,α}密度及C^{2,α}任意初始速度，建立局部适定性分析。

Result: 解保持空间正则性；力势在运动边界上属于C^{3,α}；在一致有界数据族内，解在每个较低Hölder指数下连续依赖于初始数据。

Conclusion: 该松弛系统在给定正则性假设下具有局部适定性，且解对初始数据具有连续依赖性。

Abstract: We study the free-boundary Euler--Monge--Ampère system as a conservative relaxation of ideal incompressible fluid motion. The force is determined on the actual fluid domain by a Monge--Ampère equation coupled to a nonlinear boundary condition. For each fixed relaxation parameter, we establish local well-posedness for uniformly convex initial domains of class $C^{2,α}$, densities in $C^{1,α}$, and arbitrary initial velocities in $C^{2,α}$. The solution retains these spatial regularities, and the force potential belongs to $C^{3,α}$ up to the moving boundary. Within uniformly bounded data families, the solution depends continuously on the initial data at every lower Hölder exponent.

</details>


### [120] [Double-logarithmic stability for a parabolic inverse problem on CTA manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16759)
*Yujian Zheng,Zhiwen Duan,Shiqi Jing*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在共形横向各向异性流形上，建立了抛物型方程中时间相关势函数反演的双对数稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究抛物型方程 $(c^{-1}\partial_t-\Delta_g+q)u=0$ 中时间相关势的反演稳定性，并将结果推广至共形横向各向异性流形的一般几何设定。

Method: 利用高斯束准模（具一致 $O(h^{1/2})$ 集中估计）、由边界 Carleman 估计得到几何光学解，以及小衰减下测地射线变换的稳定性；并结合 Hilbert 空间值 Fourier 变换的定量解析延拓与 Sobolev 插值。

Result: 在所给部分输入-输出映射算子范数刻画下，证明了在衰减测地射线变换一致稳定性假设下的稳定性，并获得双对数模量；当横向流形为 simple 及某些非 simple 流形上的正则族时验证了该假设。

Conclusion: 该工作给出时间相关势反演的双对数稳定性估计，推进了共形横向各向异性几何中抛物型反问题的稳定性理论。

Abstract: We establish a double-logarithmic stability estimate for the recovery of a time-dependent potential in the parabolic equation $(c^{-1}\partial_t-Δ_g+q)u=0$ on a conformally transversally anisotropic manifold. The partial input--output map associates an initial state and lateral Dirichlet data supported in a prescribed open set with the terminal state and the Neumann trace on another open set. These sets contain the positive and negative boundary parts determined by a limiting Carleman weight, respectively. The measurement discrepancy is the operator norm of the difference of two such maps, whose range has improved Sobolev regularity. We prove stability under a uniform stability assumption for the attenuated geodesic ray transform on a family of non-tangential geodesics. This assumption is verified when the transversal manifold is simple and for regular families on certain non-simple manifolds. The proof uses Gaussian beam quasimodes with a uniform $O(h^{1/2})$ concentration estimate, geometric optics solutions obtained from boundary Carleman estimates, and stability of the geodesic ray transform for small attenuation. Quantitative analytic continuation for Hilbert-space-valued Fourier transforms and Sobolev interpolation yield the double-logarithmic modulus.

</details>


### [121] [Formalization of Landau damping in the Vlasov--Poisson equations in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16801)
*Jacob Bedrossian*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 Lean 4 中对 Mouhot 与 Villani 关于 $\mathbb{T}^d$ 上非线性 Landau 阻尼的定理进行形式化，覆盖所有 Gevrey 正则性指标 $s>1/3$ 的小背景情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性 Landau 阻尼是动力学方程与 Vlasov 方程研究中的核心结果，但其证明涉及估计极其复杂、技术细节繁重，传统人工验证难以保证完全无误。形式化该定理可提供机器可检验的正确性保证，并推动交互式定理证明在硬分析领域的应用。

Method: 采用 Lean 4 证明助手，对 Mouhot–Villani 定理进行机器形式化：在环面 $\mathbb{T}^d$ 上处理 Vlasov–Poisson 系统的非线性 Landau 阻尼，并覆盖全部 Gevrey 正则性指标 $s>1/3$ 以及小背景扰动的情形，构建相应的形式化定义、引理与估计链。

Result: 在 Lean 4 中完成了 Mouhot–Villani 非线性 Landau 阻尼定理的形式化陈述与证明，范围涵盖 $\mathbb{T}^d$ 上所有 Gevrey 指标 $s>1/3$ 的小背景情形。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，涉及精细解析估计与非线性动力学机制的深刻定理，可以在现代证明助手中被完整形式化，为分析学领域的形式化验证树立了范例。

Abstract: We present a formalization in Lean 4 of Mouhot and Villani's theorem on nonlinear Landau damping in $\mathbb T^d$ in all Gevrey regularity indices $s > 1/3$ for small backgrounds.

</details>


### [122] [Viscosity solution of systems of integral-partial differential equations with interconnected obstacles without Monotonicity Conditions and infinite L{é}vy measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16812)
*Said Hamadène,Mohamed Mnif,Sarra Neffati*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带互联障碍的二阶积分-偏微分方程组（含HJB系统），在去除生成元关于跳跃项的单调性条件下，构造并证明连续粘性解的存在唯一性，工具为带跳跃和互联障碍的反射倒向随机微分方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类方程组（尤其跳跃扩散模型中最优切换问题对应的HJB系统）通常依赖生成元关于跳跃分量的单调性条件来保证解的存在唯一性。本文旨在摆脱这一限制，并处理无穷活动Lévy测度的一般情形。

Method: 通过引入并研究与之对应的带跳跃及互联障碍的反射倒向随机微分方程组（RBSDEs），利用其解的存在唯一性结果，构造原积分-偏微分方程组的连续粘性解并证明唯一性。

Result: 在生成元关于跳跃分量不满足单调性的前提下，成功构造了方程组的连续粘性解，并证明其在连续有界函数类中唯一；同时给出了相应反射倒向随机微分方程组解的存在唯一性结论。Lévy测度λ(·)具有无穷活动性。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了已有结果，表明互联障碍的积分-偏微分方程组（含跳跃扩散最优切换HJB系统）在有界连续粘性解框架下仍具适定性，无需跳跃项单调性假设；反射BSDE-with-jumps方法可有效处理此类问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a system of second-order integral-partial differential equations with interconnected obstacles. A particular case, is the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB for short) system associated with the optimal switching problem in the jump-diffusion model. Getting rid of the monotonicity condition on the generators with respect to the jump component, we construct a continuous viscosity solution of the system, which is unique in the class of continuous bounded functions. The L{é}vy measure $λ$(.) is of infinite activity. The main tool we use is the associated system of reflected backward stochastic differential equations with jumps and interconnected obstacles for which we also study existence and uniqueness of the solution.

</details>


### [123] [An improved bound on the support diameter of nonnegative planar Euler vorticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16869)
*Daomin Cao,Junhong Fan,Guodong Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明非负平面Euler涡量在紧支$L^1$初值下支撑半径的$O(t^{1/4})$界，去除对数因子，并证明支撑半径平方的时间Hölder连续性（指数$1/2$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典涡量隔离估计中带有对数因子，导致支撑半径增长界不够简洁/最优。本文旨在去除这些对数因子，得到更干净的$O(t^{1/4})$界。

Method: 在对称化涡量表述下，对相互作用核进行因式分解，得到高阶矩的二次卷积不等式；保留该卷积结构，对矩的有限和进行初等比较以控制整体支撑。

Result: 得到支撑半径的$O(t^{1/4})$界，且不含对数因子；并证明支撑半径平方关于时间以指数$1/2$Hölder连续。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了经典涡量隔离估计，提供了无对数因子的$t^{1/4}$增长率，并给出支撑半径平方的时间正则性结论。

Abstract: We prove an $O(t^{1/4})$ bound on the support radius of nonnegative planar Euler vorticity with compactly supported $L^1$ initial data, removing the logarithmic factors from the classical confinement estimates. The result holds in the symmetrized vorticity formulation. A factorization of the interaction kernel yields a quadratic convolution inequality for high-order moments. Retaining this convolution allows an elementary comparison for finite sums of moments to control the full support. We also prove that the squared support radius is Hölder continuous in time with exponent $1/2$.

</details>


### [124] [Improved Regularity for Nonlocal Hamilton--Jacobi Equations with Superlinear Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16918)
*Adina Ciomaga,Tri Minh Lê,Olivier Ley,Erwin Topp*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究由x依赖的积分-微分算子驱动、具超线性强制Hamiltonian的非局部Hamilton-Jacobi方程粘性解的正则性：在无椭圆性假设下证明Hölder正则性并给出显式指数，在弱椭圆性假设下证明全局Lipschitz连续性，并给出反例说明连续性假设的尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部Hamilton-Jacobi方程的正则性理论中，无椭圆性条件下能否获得解的正则性，以及超线性Hamiltonian（尤其是1<m<σ<2这一微妙区域）对正则性的影响尚不清楚；同时Lévy测度空间依赖的连续性要求是否必要也缺乏明确刻画。

Method: 针对由x依赖的积分-微分算子（Lévy测度）与强制超线性Hamiltonian构成的非局部方程：在一般结构与连续性假设、无椭圆性条件下，利用强制Hamiltonian提供的Hölder正则性；再在弱椭圆性假设下结合非局部扩散的正则化效应，通过Ishii-Lions方法证明全局Lipschitz连续性；并构造反例检验空间依赖仅Hölder连续时Lipschitz正则性的失效。

Result: 在无椭圆性条件下建立了有界粘性解的Hölder正则性，Hölder指数由算子阶σ∈(0,2)与Hamiltonian增长指数m>1显式给出；方法适用于任意超线性Hamiltonian，包括1<m<σ<2的困难情形，并在σ∈(1,2)时给出改进的正则性指数。在附加弱椭圆性条件下证明解全局Lipschitz连续。反例表明：无椭圆性且Lévy测度的空间依赖仅为Hölder连续时，Lipschitz正则性可能失效。

Conclusion: 强制超线性Hamiltonian与非局部扩散的正则化效应相结合，可在很弱的假设下获得Hölder乃至Lipschitz正则性；所得指数对1<m<σ<2及σ∈(1,2)情形具有改进意义。Lévy测度空间依赖的连续性假设在无椭圆性时是尖锐的，不能削弱为一般Hölder连续性。

Abstract: We investigate the regularity of viscosity solutions to a class of nonlocal Hamilton-Jacobi equations driven by x-dependent integro-differential operators and coercive superlinear Hamiltonians. We first establish H{ö}lder regularity for bounded viscosity solutions under general structural and continuity assumptions on the underlying L{é}vy measures, without imposing any ellipticity condition on the nonlocal operator. The H{ö}lder exponent is given explicitly in terms of the order $σ$ $\in$ (0, 2) of the operator and the growth exponent m > 1 of the Hamiltonian. In particular, our approach applies to arbitrary superlinear Hamiltonians, including the delicate regime 1 < m < $σ$ < 2, and yields an improved regularity exponent when $σ$ $\in$ (1, 2). Assuming in addition a weak ellipticity condition on the nonlocal operator, we prove that viscosity solutions are globally Lipschitz continuous. The proof combines the H{ö}lder regularity supplied by the coercive Hamiltonian with the regularizing effect of the nonlocal diffusion through an Ishii-Lions argument, allowing us to treat Hamiltonians with arbitrary superlinear growth. Finally, we provide a counterexample showing that, in the absence of ellipticity, Lipschitz regularity may fail if the spatial dependence of the L{é}vy measures is merely H{ö}lder continuous, thereby illustrating the sharpness of our continuity assumptions.

</details>


### [125] [Rigidity results via a Poincaré type formula for stable solutions of quasilinear anisotropic elliptic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16959)
*Berardino Sciunzi,Domenico Vuono*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 $\mathbb R^2$ 与 $\mathbb R^3$ 中拟线性各向异性椭圆方程稳定解的一维对称性结果，并推广了已有文献中的恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳定解的一维对称性，是对经典椭圆方程对称性理论（由 [Fhdr, FSV] 等启发）向拟线性各向异性情形的自然延伸。

Method: 沿用 [Fhdr, FSV] 中的思路，通过推广 [SZ1, SZ2, FSV] 中的某个恒等式/公式，并结合稳定解的性质进行推导。

Result: 在 $\mathbb R^2$ 和 $\mathbb R^3$ 中获得了拟线性各向异性椭圆方程稳定解的一维对称性结果，且给出了 [SZ1, SZ2, FSV] 中公式的推广形式。

Conclusion: 所得结果将一维对称性理论扩展至拟线性各向异性椭圆方程，所推广的恒等式是证明的核心工具。

Abstract: In this paper we prove some one-dimensional symmetry results for stable solutions to quasilinear anisotropic elliptic equations in $\mathbb R^2$ and $\mathbb R^3$. In this regard, we give an extension of a formula obtained in \cite{SZ1,SZ2, FSV}. The techniques used are inspired by the works of \cite{Fhdr,FSV}.

</details>


### [126] [Dimension-dependent symmetry breaking in a diffusive nonlocal interaction model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16978)
*Dohyun Kim,Hansol Park,Woojoo Shim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究由随机 n-单形平方体积生成的扩散非局部相互作用方程，利用均值-协方差封闭系统证明全局适定性与平衡态分类，并给出指数收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解由随机 n-单形平方体积诱导的非局部相互作用方程的解结构与长期行为，尤其是漂移项仅依赖均值与协方差时能否实现有限维约化，以及不同维度下平衡态是否唯一。

Method: 推导非线性漂移仅通过解的均值与协方差矩阵依赖，得到封闭的有限维协方差系统；将测度值解显式表示为仿射推前与高斯卷积的复合；据此在 P_2(R^d) 中对任意初值证明全局适定性；对自由能极小化与定常态进行分类；在二次 Wasserstein 距离下证明收敛并计算指数速率。

Result: 任意初始数据在 P_2(R^d) 中全局适定；定常态与自由能极小化子一致；当 1≤n<d 时，平衡态为平移意义下唯一的各向同性高斯；在临界维数 n=d 时，能量仅固定协方差矩阵的行列式，且当 d≥2 时出现各向异性高斯平衡态的连续统；所有解在二次 Wasserstein 距离下收敛到某个平衡态，并得到显式指数收敛率。

Conclusion: 随机 n-单形体积产生的非局部相互作用方程可通过均值-协方差有限维约化完全刻画，平衡态由自由能决定：亚临界维数下唯一各向同性，临界维数下各向异性连续统，并具有指数收敛的长期稳定性。

Abstract: We study a diffusive nonlocal interaction equation generated by the squared volumes of random $n$-simplices. The nonlinear drift depends on the solution only through its mean and covariance matrix, which yields a closed finite-dimensional covariance system and an explicit representation of the measure-valued solution as an affine pushforward followed by Gaussian convolution. We use this reduction to prove global well-posedness for arbitrary initial data in $\mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)$. We then classify the stationary states and show that they coincide with the minimizers of the associated free energy. When $1\leq n<d$, the equilibrium is a unique isotropic Gaussian up to translation. By contrast, at the critical dimension $n=d$, the energy fixes only the determinant of the covariance matrix, and, when $d\geq2$, a continuum of anisotropic Gaussian equilibria appears. Finally, we prove convergence of every solution to one of these equilibria in quadratic Wasserstein distance and obtain explicit exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [127] [Large time nonlinear dynamics close to inhomogeneous stationary states of the Vlasov-HMF model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16998)
*Erwan Faou,Frédéric Rousset,Tooryanand Seetohul*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究Vlasov-HMF模型中与摆型哈密顿流相关的对称非均匀定态的紧支集族及其在有限正则性小扰动下的非线性动力学，证明长时间内解在调制作用-角坐标下保持接近定态，且扰动磁化强度满足由线性动力学驱动的衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vlasov-HMF模型是研究无碰撞等离子体与自引力系统长时间稳定性的重要简化模型。已有工作多集中于均匀态或较规则的定态，而对于具有紧支集、位于分界线上方（separatrix region）的对称非均匀定态及其在有限正则性扰动下的长时间行为尚缺乏系统性刻画，尤其缺少对线性稳定性判据（Penrose判据）与非线性动力学之间联系的定量描述。

Method: 1）在Vlasov-HMF框架下，考察与摆型哈密顿流相关联的对称扰动及其对应的对称非均匀定态；2）构造一类满足以下条件的定态族：支集紧含于分界线区域内、在原点附近取常值、并满足线性稳定性（Penrose）判据；3）引入调制作用-角坐标（modulated action-angle coordinates）描述小扰动的演化；4）对具有有限正则性的紧支集小扰动进行非线性分析，估计其长时间行为与磁化强度的衰减。

Result: 证明了上述紧支集对称非均匀定态族的存在性及其线性稳定性；进一步证明在足够大但有限的时间尺度上，解在调制作用-角坐标下始终与某个调制后的定态保持接近；同时得到扰动磁化强度的衰减估计，该衰减由相应的线性动力学所决定。

Conclusion: 该工作将线性稳定性判据与非线性长时间动力学联系起来，揭示了Vlasov-HMF模型中一类具有紧支集的非均匀定态在有限正则性扰动下仍能长时间保持结构稳定，并给出磁化强度的定量衰减率，为理解平均场类系统的弛豫与稳定性提供了新的分析工具与结果。

Abstract: We consider the Vlasov-HMF (Hamiltonian Mean-Field) model and symmetric perturbations of symmetric inhomogeneous stationary states that are naturally associated with a pendulum Hamiltonian flow. We first show the existence of families of such stationary states having compact support inside the separatrix region, constant value near the origin, and satisfying a linear stability (Penrose) criterion. We then describe the nonlinear dynamics of small compactly supported perturbations with finite regularity of this class of equilibria. More precisely, we prove that for large (but finite) times, in a modulated action-angle coordinate system the solution remains close to a modulated stationary state and that the magnetization of the perturbation satisfes decay estimates driven by the linear dynamics.

</details>


### [128] [Solutions of the Bernoulli one-phase problem with a defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17066)
*William M Feldman,Inwon C Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究球外区域一相Bernoulli自由边界问题解及带紧支集缺陷的整体解的远场行为，证明"真解"（blow-down为半平面解者）在无穷远处存在渐近展开：d≥3时自由边界高度以|x|^{2-d}速率收敛（含容量型系数），d=2时展开含对数项，且展开对所有真解定量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一相Bernoulli自由边界问题在无穷远处的渐近结构，特别是自由边界高度趋于极限的速率与方式，并刻画带局部缺陷（紧支集不均匀性）的整体解行为，是自由边界正则性理论中的核心问题。

Method: 研究球外区域的一相Bernoulli自由边界问题解以及带单个紧支集缺陷的整体解；针对blow-down为半平面解的真解，在无穷远处作渐近展开分析，并建立定量且对所有真解一致的估计。

Result: 在维数d≥3时，自由边界高度以速率|x|^{2-d}收敛到一个极限，并带有容量型（capacity-type）系数；在维数d=2时，展开中出现对数项。

Conclusion: 该渐近展开的重要新颖之处在于其对所有真解是定量且一致的，深化了对Bernoulli自由边界问题远场行为的理解。

Abstract: We study the far-field behavior of solutions of the one-phase Bernoulli free boundary problem in the exterior of a ball, and of entire solutions with a single compactly supported inhomogeneity of the free boundary condition, which we call a defect. For solutions which blow down to a half-plane solution (proper solutions) we establish an asymptotic expansion at infinity: in dimension $d \geq 3$ the free boundary height converges to a limit at rate $|x|^{2-d}$ with a capacity-type coefficient, while in dimension $d=2$ the expansion carries a logarithmic term. A significant novelty is that the expansions are quantitative and uniform over all the proper solutions.

</details>


### [129] [Classification of finite energy positive solutions to Yamabe-type equation on the fifteen dimensional octonionic Heisenberg group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17072)
*Daowen Lin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文分类了15维八元数Heisenberg群上Yamabe型方程的有限能量正解，刻画了Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式极值函数，证实了Garofalo与Vassilev (2001) 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式极值函数的存在性与显式表达是CR几何与次椭圆分析的核心问题；Garofalo与Vassilev在2001年研究了该问题，但15维八元数Heisenberg群情形因其代数非结合性而遗留为公开猜想，本文即针对该猜想展开。

Method: 在15维八元数Heisenberg群（其底层代数非结合）上，对Yamabe型方程进行分类，利用变分与对称性约化方法研究该方程有限能量正解的结构。

Result: 完整分类了该方程的所有有限能量正解，并给出该群上Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式极值函数的显式描述，从而证实了Garofalo–Vassilev猜想。

Conclusion: 非结合代数结构下该猜想同样成立，结果将已知情形推广至八元数Heisenberg群，为CR流形上极值问题提供了新的显式范例。

Abstract: We classify finite-energy positive solutions to the Yamabe-type equation on the 15-dimensional octonionic Heisenberg group, whose algebra is non-associative. Extremals for the Folland-Stein-Sobolev inequality on this group are explicitly described. This confirms the conjecture of Garofalo and Vassilev [Duke Math. J. 2001] on the $15$ dimensional octonionic Heisenberg group.

</details>


### [130] [Asymptotic Behavior of Radial Solutions to Singular $p$-Laplacian Equations as $p\to1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17511)
*Juan Pablo Alcon Apaza*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究p-Laplace方程在奇异极限$p \downarrow 1$下的正径向整解收敛性，证明解序列在局部一致拓扑下收敛到1-Laplace方程的粘性解，并刻画极限方程右端项$\mathtt{K}(|x|)$的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1-Laplace方程（$p=1$）是退化椭圆方程，缺乏标准弱解理论；通过$p \downarrow 1$的奇异极限可以从p-Laplace方程的已知理论逼近1-Laplace方程的解，同时探究非线性源项中奇异指数$\beta_p$与$p-1$同阶时解的渐近行为。

Method: 对正径向整解$u_p$施加一致先验估计，利用$C_{\text{loc}}$紧性和$L_{\text{loc}}^\infty$弱*收敛提取子列；通过分析$u_p^{p-1}$的极限$w$以及$F_p$的局部一致收敛和$\beta_p/(p-1) \to \mathbf{c}$，在集合$\{\mathtt{G}(|x|)<1\}$上识别极限算子，并分仿射与非仿射$F_1$情形讨论梯度项贡献。

Result: 在$n\geq 2$、$1<p<\min\{2,\sqrt{n}\}$、$0\leq\beta_p\leq p-1$条件下，$u_{p_j}\to u$于$C_{\text{loc}}$且$\nabla u_{p_j}\overset{*}{\rightharpoonup}\nabla u$于$L_{\text{loc}}^\infty$，$u\in W^{1,\infty}$径向且满足$-\Delta_1 u=\mathtt{K}(|x|)$。在$\{\mathtt{G}(|x|)<1\}$上，$\mathtt{K}=w^{-\mathbf{c}}F_1(|x|,u,0)$且$\nabla u=0$ a.e.；若$F_1$关于$\rho$仿射，则$\mathtt{K}=w^{-\mathbf{c}}F_1(|x|,u,|\nabla u|)$ a.e.。$n=1$情形得到类似结论。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了带奇异权$u_p^{-\beta_p}$的p-Laplace方程向1-Laplace方程的极限理论，揭示了奇异指数临界标度$\beta_p\sim(p-1)$对极限算子结构的影响，并区分了仿射与一般非线性下梯度项的贡献差异。

Abstract: We study the singular limit $p \downarrow 1$ for positive radial entire solutions $u_p$ of $$ -Δ_p u_p=F_p\left(|x|, u_p,\left|\nabla u_p\right|\right) u_p^{-β_p} \quad \text { in } \mathbb{R}^n, $$ where $n \geq 2,1<p<\min \{2, \sqrt{n}\}$, and $0 \leq β_p \leq p-1$. Assuming that $$ F_p \rightarrow F_1 \quad \text { locally uniformly }, \quad \frac{β_p}{p-1} \rightarrow \mathbf{c} \in[0,1] \quad \text { as } p \downarrow 1, $$ we prove that, after passing to a subsequence, $u_{p_j} \rightarrow u$ in $C_{\text {loc }}\left(\mathbb{R}^n\right)$ and $\nabla u_{p_j} \stackrel{*}{\rightharpoonup} \nabla u$ in $L_{\text {loc }}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^n ; \mathbb{R}^n\right)$, where $u \in W^{1, \infty}\left(\mathbb{R}^n\right)$ is radial, and solves $$ -Δ_1 u=\mathtt{K}(|x|) \quad \text { in } \mathbb{R}^n . $$ On $\left\{x \in \mathbb{R}^n \mid \mathtt{G}(|x|)<1\right\}$, we prove that $\mathtt{K}(|x|)=w^{-\mathbf{c}} F_1(|x|, u(x), 0)$ and that $\nabla u=0$ a.e., where $u_{p_i}^{p_i-1} \rightarrow w$. On the other hand, we show that if $ρ\mapsto F_1(r, t, ρ)$ is affine for every $r$ and $t$, then $\mathtt{K}(|x|)=$ $w^{-\mathbf{c}} F_1(|x|, u(x),|\nabla u(x)|)$ a.e. in $\mathbb{R}^n$.
  For $n=1$ and $1<p<2$, we study $$ \frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{~d} x}\left(\left|u_p^{\prime}\right|^{p-2} u_p^{\prime}\right)=\mathtt{F}_p\left(|x|, u_p,\left|u_p^{\prime}\right|\right) u_p^{-β_p} \quad \text { in } \mathbb{R} $$ and obtain analogous results as $p \downarrow 1$.

</details>


### [131] [The vanishing latent heat limit of a stochastic Stefan problem : An error estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17079)
*Ioana Ciotir,Perla El Kettani,Dan Goreac,Danielle Hilhorst*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Hilhorst-Mimura-Schätzle关于生物学两相Stefan问题潜热系数趋于零极限的结果推广到含时空白噪声（空间有色）的随机情形，证明弱解存在唯一性并研究潜热系数趋于零的极限，方法基于正/零潜热解之间的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文献[18]研究了生物学中两相Stefan问题在潜热系数趋于零时的极限，但未涉及随机扰动；本文旨在引入较一般的噪声项，将确定性极限分析推广至随机偏微分方程框架。

Method: 引入时间白噪声、空间有色噪声的加性噪声；先证明对应随机Stefan问题弱解的存在唯一性；再通过建立正潜热解与零潜热解之间的误差估计来研究潜热系数趋于零的极限。

Result: 证明了该随机Stefan问题弱解的存在唯一性；给出了潜热系数趋于零时解的极限；误差估计方法即使在不含噪声的确定性情形下也属新颖。

Conclusion: 成功将潜热趋于零的Stefan问题极限理论推广到随机情形，并提出一种基于误差估计的证明方法，该方法在确定性与随机情形下均具有创新性。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to extend an article by Hilhorst, Mimura and Sch{ä}tzle [18] about the limit as the latent heat coefficient tends to zero of a two-phase Stefan problem arising in biology. We introduce a rather general additive noise white in time and colored in space, and search for the limit of the solution of the corresponding stochastic Stefan problem as the latent heat coefficient vanishes. We first prove the existence and uniqueness of the weak solution of this problem, and then study the limit of the solution as the latent heat coefficient tends to zero. Unlike in [18], our method of proof is based upon an error estimate between the solution of the Stefan problem with positive latent heat and that of the Stefan problem with zero latent heat, which seems to be novel even in the deterministic case when no noise is added.

</details>


### [132] [On some functionals for which the ball is a saddle shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17083)
*Alba Lia Masiello,Gloria Paoli,Francesco Salerno*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究在quermassintegral约束下$k$-扭转刚度的最大化问题，发现球并非极值形状而是鞍点，并证明相关曲率泛函在体积约束下也具有类似鞍点行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Laplace 与 Monge-Ampère 情形下球通常为极值形状，而中间 Hessian 区域（$k$-Hessian 算子）的变分问题中球的作用尚不清楚，作者希望揭示该区域与经典情形的结构性差异。

Method: 对相应变分问题进行二阶分析，首次将二阶形状导数方法应用于 $k$-Hessian 算子的变分问题；并在此基础上对相关曲率泛函在体积约束下做类似分析。

Result: 在适当的 quermassintegral 约束下，$k$-扭转刚度在球附近存在既递增又递减的扰动，表明球为鞍点；相关曲率泛函在体积约束下也呈现鞍点行为。作者称这一现象此前未被观察到。

Conclusion: 球在 $k$-扭转刚度最大化问题中不是极值形状而是鞍点，揭示了中间 Hessian 区域与经典 Laplace、Monge-Ampère 情形之间的结构性区别。

Abstract: In the present paper, we study the maximization problem of the $k$-Torsional rigidity under quermassintegral constraint, with particular emphasis on the role of the ball. Our main result shows a phenomenon which, as far as we know, has not previously been observed: the ball, rather than being an extremal shape, is a saddle point. More precisely, for suitable quermassintegral constraints, we show that the $k$-torsional rigidity admits both increasing and decreasing perturbations around the ball. The proof is based on a second-order analysis of the corresponding variational problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first use of a second-order shape derivative approach in the study of variational problems for $k$-Hessian operators.
  We also establish an analogous saddle-point behavior for a related curvature functional under a volume constraint, highlighting a structural distinction between the intermediate Hessian regime and the classical Laplace and Monge-Ampère cases.

</details>


### [133] [Mixed local-nonlocal eigenvalue problems in the Heisenberg group: spectral theory and singular multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17125)
*Prashanta Garain,Vicentiu Radulescu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在海森堡群上研究由局部扩散与分数阶非局部扩散耦合算子的非线性特征值问题，并应用于含变奇异指数的扰动奇异问题，建立了多重性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有特征值理论与奇异问题研究多集中于欧氏空间或单一扩散机制，缺乏在海森堡群这一非欧几何背景下对混合局部-非局部算子及变奇异指数的系统分析，本文填补该空白。

Method: 在运动受水平方向约束的海森堡群上，构造结合经典局部扩散（短程作用）与分数阶非局部扩散（长程效应）的算子族，建立其谱理论；对纯非局部与混合情形分别研究扰动奇异问题，混合情形借助梯度收敛定理导出多重性结果，并处理域内逐点变化的变奇异指数。

Result: 获得了混合局部-非局部算子族的谱刻画，并在纯非局部与混合两种设定下证明了扰动奇异问题的多重解存在性；混合情形中梯度收敛定理起到关键作用，变奇异指数得到统一处理。

Conclusion: 这是海森堡群上混合局部-非局部算子非线性特征值问题与变奇异指数奇异问题的首个系统性研究，为次椭圆算子谱理论开辟新方向，并为反常扩散、非局部相变、图像分析及非完整系统控制等应用提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates nonlinear eigenvalue problems governed by a family of operators that combine two distinct diffusion mechanisms: a classical local diffusion, which accounts for short-range interactions, and a fractional nonlocal diffusion, which captures long-range effects. The analysis is carried out in the Heisenberg group, a non-Euclidean geometric setting in which motion is constrained to a distinguished set of horizontal directions. As an application of the obtained spectral theory, we establish multiplicity results for perturbed singular problems in both the purely nonlocal and mixed local--nonlocal settings. For the mixed case, a gradient convergence theorem plays a crucial role in deriving these multiplicity results. A key feature of our analysis is the treatment of variable singular exponents, allowing the singularity to vary throughout the domain. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of nonlinear eigenvalue problems and singular problems with variable singularity exponents for mixed local-nonlocal operators in the Heisenberg group. The results therefore open a new direction in the spectral theory of subelliptic operators and provide a rigorous mathematical foundation for applications ranging from anomalous diffusion and nonlocal phase transitions to image analysis and control theory on nonholonomic systems.

</details>


### [134] [Liouville-type theorems and universal estimates for semilinear elliptic equations involving the product of the function and its gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17149)
*Wenguo Liang,Zhengce Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究方程 $-Δu=u^p|\nabla u|^q$ 正解的局部与全局性质，引入线性算子构造微分不等式以获得梯度估计并建立Liouville型定理，进而给出局部解的通用估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程的梯度非线性项使经典方法难以直接应用，且已有结果对参数 $p+q$ 的限制较窄，需在更广参数范围内获得梯度估计与Liouville型定理。

Method: 引入线性算子，构造相应的微分不等式，由此推导梯度估计，并建立Liouville型定理，最后应用于局部解的通用估计。

Result: 得到该方程正解的梯度估计与Liouville型定理，并获得局部解的通用估计；将He、Hu和Wang（Math. Z. 313 (2026), No. 6）所考虑的条件 $p+q<(N+3)/(N-1)$ 推广到更宽参数范围，部分结果为新的。

Conclusion: 线性算子方法有效处理了带梯度非线性项的半线性方程，所得结果推广了已有参数条件，部分结论具有新颖性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study local and global properties of positive solutions to the equation $-Δu=u^p|\nabla u|^q$ in a domain $Ω$ of $\mathbb R^N$, where $p$ and $q$ are parameters. We introduce a linear operator to construct the differential inequality to obtain gradient estimates, and further establish Liouville-type theorems. As an application, we derive universal estimates for local solutions. Some of our results are new, as we extend the condition $p+q<(N+3)/(N-1)$ considered by He, Hu and Wang [Math. Z. 313 (2026), No. 6] to a wider range of parameters.

</details>


### [135] [Magnetic norm inflation at the $\ell^1$ Besov endpoint for viscous non-resistive incompressible MHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17192)
*Yingzhe Ban,Boling Guo,Maotuo Guo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了粘性、非电阻不可压缩磁流体力学方程在端点空间 $\dot B^{-1}_{\infty,1}\times\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}$ 中于原点处的磁场范数膨胀现象，并由此推出解映射在该磁性端点拓扑下不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究MHD方程在临界/端点Besov空间中的适定性与解映射连续性。当二分求和指数大于1时可用的低-高仿积机制在 $\ell^1$ 端点失效，因此需要新的机制来解释端点处的范数膨胀与不连续性。

Method: 构造光滑无散初始数据：对任意小 $\varepsilon>0$，令 $b_0$ 紧支且 $\|u_0\|_{\dot B^{-1}_{\infty,1}}+\|b_0\|_{\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}}<\varepsilon$。结合共振四波速度相互作用与磁场拉伸，在递增的二分壳层族上产生均匀尺度的贡献；使用频率局部化测试泛函提取端点下界，并借助加权Fourier $L^1$ 空间中的一致Lagrangian估计与空间局部化论证控制余项、去除辅助常磁场。

Result: 对每个足够小的 $\varepsilon>0$，存在经典解满足 $\|b(t_\varepsilon)\|_{\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}}>\varepsilon^{-1}$，其中 $0<t_\varepsilon<\varepsilon$。因此任何与经典解一致的解映射在原点处（磁性端点拓扑下）都不可能连续。

Conclusion: 粘性、非电阻MHD方程在端点空间 $\dot B^{-1}_{\infty,1}\times\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}$ 中于原点处发生磁场范数膨胀，解映射不连续，表明该端点拓扑不适合作为适定性框架。

Abstract: We prove magnetic-field norm inflation at the origin for the viscous, non-resistive incompressible magnetohydrodynamic equations on $\R^d$, $d\ge2$, in the endpoint space \[
  \dot B^{-1}_{\infty,1}(\R^d)\times \dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}(\R^d). \] For every sufficiently small $\varepsilon>0$, we construct smooth divergence-free initial data $(u_0,b_0)$, with $b_0$ compactly supported and \[
  \norm[\dot B^{-1}_{\infty,1}]{u_0}
  +\norm[\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}]{b_0}<\varepsilon, \] such that the corresponding classical solution satisfies \[
  \norm[\dot B^{0}_{\infty,1}]{b(t_\varepsilon)}>\varepsilon^{-1} \] for some $0<t_\varepsilon<\varepsilon$. Consequently, no solution map agreeing with classical solutions can be continuous at the origin in the magnetic endpoint topology. The low--high paraproduct mechanism available when the dyadic summability exponent is greater than one gives no gain at the $\ell^1$ endpoint. We instead combine a resonant four-wave velocity interaction with magnetic stretching to produce uniformly sized contributions on a growing family of dyadic shells. Frequency-localized test functionals extract the endpoint lower bound, while uniform Lagrangian estimates in weighted Fourier $L^1$ spaces and a spatial localization argument control the remainders and remove the auxiliary constant magnetic field.

</details>


### [136] [Geometric sets of elliptic equations with Dini coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17224)
*Wenshuai Jiang,Junyuan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有Dini系数的散度型椭圆方程，建立了在仅连续系数下double index的几乎单调性，进而得到临界集的弱体积估计与结点集的均匀体积估计，并证明Dini条件是本质的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Lipschitz系数（NV）与Hölder系数（HJ1）的已有框架至更一般的Dini系数，并探究系数正则性对临界集与结点集体积测度估计的影响。

Method: 首先在仅连续系数下建立double index的几乎单调性；利用该性质推导临界集的弱体积估计和结点集的均匀体积估计；通过构造具有连续系数且有界double index但结点集测度无穷的例子说明Dini条件的必要性；证明次水平集的多项式增长估计；采用另一方法建立切映射的定量唯一性与锥分裂原理。

Result: 获得临界集的弱体积估计与结点集的均匀体积估计；证明次水平集的多项式增长估计；由切映射定量唯一性与锥分裂原理导出临界集的显式Minkowski型估计；结合Kenig--Zhao的结果得到边界临界集的测度界；并构造反例表明Dini假设不可去除。

Conclusion: Dini系数框架统一并推广了Lipschitz与Hölder情形的结果，Dini条件是保证结点集有限测度的本质假设；所得估计进一步给出了临界集及边界临界集的定量测度控制。

Abstract: We study divergence-form elliptic equations with Dini coefficients, extending the frameworks of \cite{NV} for Lipschitz coefficients and \cite{HJ1} for Hölder coefficients. We first establish an almost monotonicity property for the doubling index under merely continuous coefficients. This leads to weak volume estimates for critical sets and uniform volume estimates for nodal sets. The Dini assumption is essential here since we construct an example with continuous coefficients and bounded doubling index whose nodal set has infinite measure. We also prove polynomial growth estimates for sub-level sets. Using a different approach, we establish quantitative uniqueness of tangent maps and a cone-splitting principle, which yield explicit Minkowski-type estimates for critical sets. Finally, combined with the results of Kenig--Zhao \cite{KZ4}, our estimates give measure bounds for boundary critical sets.

</details>


### [137] [Soliton Resolution at Blow-Up for the Subconformal Nonlinear Wave Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17245)
*Hatem Zaag*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究亚共形与共形情形下具超线性纯幂非线性项的非线性波动方程（NLW），在初始数据满足一定条件时解有限时间爆破。作者在一维情形完全解决孤子分解猜想，并给出高维及扰动NLW的多种推广，包括二维近金字塔型爆破图的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLW在有限时间爆破后，两个核心问题尚待解决：(i) 所有可能爆破行为的分类；(ii) 爆破解例子的构造。作者旨在系统回答这些问题，并揭示双曲型与抛物型PDE在非线性层面结果与方法上的深刻统一。

Method: 综合运用PDE理论、数学物理与分析的多类技术：常微分方程技术、谱理论以及能量方法，形成协同方法框架，处理一维及高维情形、以及扰动版本NLW的爆破分析。

Result: 在一维情形完全解决著名的孤子分解猜想，彻底厘清该情形下的爆破行为；给出高维与扰动NLW的多种推广；在二维构造出具有近金字塔型爆破图的解。

Conclusion: 一维NLW的爆破分类与构造问题已完全解决，孤子分解猜想得证。尽管抛物型与双曲型方程在线性层面存在差异，非线性性质使这两类重要PDE在结果与方法上呈现出强烈统一性，并强调与其他PDE（尤其抛物型）研究的联系。

Abstract: We consider the nonlinear wave equation (NLW) with a superlinear pure power nonlinearity in the subconformal and conformal cases. Under some conditions on initial data, this equation is known to have solutions which blow up in finite time. Two questions are then relevant: (i) the classification of all possible blow-up behaviors; (ii) the construction of examples of blow-up solutions. As we will show, the situation is entirely settled in the one-dimensional case, where we fully solve the famous soliton resolution conjecture. Various extensions to higher dimensions and to perturbed versions of NLW are given, including the construction of a solution in 2-d, with a nearly pyramidal blow-up graph. Carrying out this program was made possible thanks to a synergy of techniques from the PDE theory, mathematical physics, and analysis, including ODE techniques, spectral theory, and energy methods. Throughout this presentation, we will insist on connections with the study of other types of PDEs, in particular in the parabolic case. Surprisingly enough, in spite of the difference between parabolic and hyperbolic equations at the linear level, the nonlinear nature brings in a strong unity-both in the results and in the methods-between these two important classes of PDEs.

</details>


### [138] [Logarithmic stability for recovering initial data of fractional heat equations from thin-set observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17253)
*Kai Yu,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区域上分数阶热方程由两类可能为零测度的薄集观测反演初值问题，建立定量能观测性估计、对数稳定性及带有条件收敛分析的正则化最小二乘重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典热方程的薄集能观测性已有成果，但分数阶情形尚缺乏系统研究；且实际观测集可能具有低维甚至零Hausdorff维数，需考察在此类退化观测下的反问题可解性与稳定性。

Method: 将经典热方程的薄集能观测性不等式推广到分数阶框架，采用指数为β∈(0,s)的统一谱不等式，对任意分数阶指数s>1/2给出定量能观测性估计；并在先验光滑性假设下证明对数稳定性估计，进而设计正则化最小二乘重构算法并作条件收敛分析。

Result: 对一般薄集（Hausdorff维数大于n-1）可取β=1/2；对特殊零维集（由代数无理点与快速累积序列构造）任意β∈(1/2,s)均可容许。短时能观测性成本始终为指数型，其速率由β相对于s决定。数值模拟表明仅用极少数观测点即可实现重构。

Conclusion: 分数阶热方程在低维乃至零维观测集下仍具定量能观测性与对数稳定性，所提正则化最小二乘算法收敛，重构误差由谱截断项与对数项之和控制并随噪声趋于零而对数衰减，验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: We study the inverse problem of recovering initial data for fractional heat equations on bounded domains from observations taken on two types of possibly Lebesgue-null sets: the first consists of general thin sets whose Hausdorff dimension exceeds \(n-1\); the second consists of specially constructed sets of zero Hausdorff dimension, built from algebraic irrational points and rapidly accumulating sequences.By extending thin-set observability inequalities from the classical heat equation to the fractional setting and employing a unified spectral inequality with exponent \(β\in(0,s)\), we establish a quantitative observability estimate for every fractional exponent \(s>1/2\). For general thin sets one has \(β=1/2\); for the special zero-dimensional sets any \(β\in(1/2,s)\) is admissible, yet the short-time observability cost always remains of exponential type, with the rate governed by \(β\) relative to \(s\). Under an a priori smoothness assumption, we also prove a logarithmic stability estimate.We further design a regularised least-squares reconstruction algorithm and provide a conditional convergence analysis, showing that the reconstruction error is bounded by the sum of a spectral truncation term and a logarithmic term dictated by the continuous stability, and that the error decays logarithmically as the noise level tends to zero. Numerical simulations using very few observation points confirm the feasibility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [139] [The anisotropic Calderón problem: rigidity near the Euclidean metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17261)
*Yi-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在三维及以上光滑有界连通区域上，证明了各向异性Calderón问题在欧氏度量附近的刚性：与欧氏度量足够接近（在适当Hölder范数下）且Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射相同的度量，必在固定边界的微分同胚意义下与欧氏度量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性Calderón问题一般要求解析或拟解析正则性、给定共形类或横向乘积结构等条件，且常需边界凸性。作者希望去掉这些限制，研究一般光滑各向异性扰动在欧氏度量附近的刚性。

Method: 将调和坐标与紧支校正子的Carleman估计相结合，在由扰动确定的频率范围上得到二次Fourier估计，再进行频率分解以推出刚性。

Result: 在三维及以上维数，对任何光滑各向异性扰动，只要度量在适当Hölder范数下充分接近欧氏度量且具有相同Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，就可在固定边界的微分同胚下与欧氏度量重合；无需解析/拟解析正则性、预设共形类、横向乘积结构或边界凸性。

Conclusion: 该工作在各向异性Calderón问题的边界刚性方面推广了已有结果，放宽了多个技术假设，说明欧氏度量附近的光滑各向异性扰动具有刚性。

Abstract: We prove rigidity near the Euclidean metric for the anisotropic Calderón problem on smooth bounded connected domains in dimensions three and higher. Any smooth Riemannian metric sufficiently close to the Euclidean metric in a suitable Hölder norm and having the same Dirichlet-to-Neumann map agrees with it up to a diffeomorphism fixing the boundary pointwise. The result applies to general smooth anisotropic perturbations, without analytic or quasianalytic regularity assumptions, a prescribed conformal class, or a transversal product structure, and requires no convexity of the boundary. The proof combines harmonic coordinates with a Carleman estimate for compactly supported correctors to obtain a quadratic Fourier estimate on a frequency range determined by the perturbation. A frequency decomposition then yields rigidity.

</details>


### [140] [New sharp lifespan estimates for a semilinear heat equation with zero-mass sign-changing initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17318)
*Berikbol T. Torebek*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究零总质量、小变号初值下非线性热方程 Cauchy 问题的爆破寿命，识别出 Fujita 范围内的新过渡指数 $p_m=1+\frac{1}{n+1}$，并给出各区间锐利寿命估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Lee--Ni 律针对正质量初值给出寿命估计；当初始数据总质量为零时，线性主部呈偶极子型，非线性源又生成 $O(\eps^p)$ 的正质量，二者竞争可能导致与经典情形本质不同的寿命行为，需重新刻画。

Method: 上界采用后向高斯检验函数与临界尺度 ODE 检验函数论证；下界采用统一的 $L^1$--$L^\infty$ bootstrap 方法，保持线性流的零质量对消并控制非线性源生成的质量。

Result: 在 Fujita 范围 $1<p\le p_F=1+2/n$ 内建立锐利寿命：$1<p<p_m$ 时 $T_\eps\asymp\eps^{-2(p-1)/[2-(n+1)(p-1)]}$；$p=p_m$ 时含对数修正 $\eps^{-2/(n+1)}(\log\frac1\eps)^{-2/(n+2)}$；$p_m<p<p_F$ 时 $T_\eps\asymp\eps^{-p(1/(p-1)-n/2)^{-1}}$；$p=p_F$ 时 $T_\eps\asymp\exp(\eps^{-p(p-1)})$。

Conclusion: 零初始质量在 Fujita 临界指数以下及临界处产生新的定量寿命区域，临界指数 $p=p_F$ 处寿命指数为 $p_F(p_F-1)$，不同于经典 Lee--Ni 指数 $p_F-1$，但 $p_F$ 仍为 Fujita 临界指数。

Abstract: We consider the Cauchy problem $$u_t-Δu=|u|^p,\qquad (t,x)\in(0,T)\times\R^n, \quad u(0,x)=\eps u_0(x),\qquad x\in\R^n,$$ with small sign-changing initial data having zero total mass. We assume $$u_0\in L^1(\R^n)\cap L^\infty(\R^n),\quad |x|u_0\in L^1(\R^n),\quad \int_{\R^n}u_0(x)\,dx=0,$$ and, for the sharp subcritical upper bounds, that $\int_{\R^n}x\,u_0(x)\,dx\neq0.$ Let $T_\eps$ denote the maximal lifespan. We identify a new transition exponent $p_m=1+\frac1{n+1}$ inside the Fujita range $1<p\le p_F:=1+2/n$, and establish the sharp lifespan estimates $$T_\eps\asymp \begin{cases} \eps^{-\frac{2(p-1)}{2-(n+1)(p-1)}}, &1<p<p_m,\\[2mm] \eps^{-\frac2{n+1}} \bigl(\log\frac1\eps\bigr)^{-\frac2{n+2}}, &p=p_m,\\[2mm] \eps^{-p\left(\frac{1}{p-1}-\frac{n}{2}\right)^{-1}}, &p_m<p<p_F,\\[2mm] \exp\!\left(\eps^{-p(p-1)}\right), & p=p_F. \end{cases}$$ These estimates reveal a lifespan phenomenon that is different from the classical Lee--Ni law for initial data with positive mass. In the zero-mass setting, the leading linear contribution is dipole-like, while the nonlinear source subsequently generates a positive mass of size \(O(\eps^p)\). Their competition produces the additional threshold \(p_m\), the logarithmic correction at $p=p_m$, and, at $p=p_F$, a substantially longer critical lifespan with exponent $p_F(p_F-1)$ instead of the classical Lee--Ni exponent $p_F-1$.
  The upper estimates are obtained by backward-Gaussian and critical scale-ODE test-function arguments, whereas the lower estimates follow from a unified $L^1$--$L^\infty$ bootstrap preserving the zero-mass cancellation of the linear flow and controlling the mass generated by the nonlinear source. Thus, although $p_F$ remains the Fujita critical exponent, zero initial mass creates a new quantitative lifespan regime below and at the critical exponent.

</details>


### [141] [Uniform many-particle spectral gap inequality and heat kernel bounds for strong attractive interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17333)
*S. E. Boutiah,D. Kinzebulatov,K. R. Madou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了奇异吸引对相互作用修正高斯测度的谱隙不等式，常数显式且关于粒子数和正则化参数一致；并应用于吸引对数气体的转移密度双边估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究被奇异吸引对相互作用修正的高斯测度的谱隙性质，并希望获得关于粒子数和正则化参数一致的显式常数，以处理奇异吸引相互作用带来的技术困难。

Method: 建立高斯测度（经奇异吸引对相互作用修正）的谱隙不等式，给出显式且一致于粒子数与正则化参数的谱隙常数；并借助截断相互作用权重对应的谱隙不等式。

Result: 得到关于粒子数与正则化参数一致且显式的谱隙常数；由此获得吸引对数气体转移密度的双边（上下）界。

Conclusion: 谱隙不等式是分析奇异吸引相互作用下高斯型测度及对数气体转移密度的有效工具，可导出转移密度的双边估计。

Abstract: We prove a spectral gap inequality for Gaussian measures modified by singular attractive pair interactions. The spectral gap constant is explicit and uniform in the number of particles and the regularization parameter. As an application, we use a corresponding spectral gap inequality for a cutoff interaction weight to obtain two-sided bounds on the transition density of the attractive logarithmic gas.

</details>
