### [142] [Global existence and vanishing viscosity limit of the compressible Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck system in critical spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17362)
*Hai-Liang Li,Ling-Yun Shou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了临界正则性下带Fokker-Planck扩散、通过拖曳力耦合的多维可压缩流体-粒子系统，证明了Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck与Euler-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck系统在临界Besov空间中平衡态附近强解的存在唯一性，建立了关于粘性参数一致的正则性估计并证明了收敛率为O(ε)的全局消失粘性极限，最后在额外低阶Besov假设下得到最优时间衰减估计及相对速度与分布函数微观部分增强的衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可压缩流体-粒子耦合系统在临界正则性框架下的适定性与渐近行为，特别是统一处理粘性与无粘（Euler）两种情形，并刻画消失粘性极限与长时间衰减规律。

Method: 在临界Besov空间中，针对Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck与Euler-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck系统的Cauchy问题，围绕平衡态展开分析：证明强解的存在唯一性；建立对公共粘性参数μ=λ=ε一致的正则性估计；证明全局消失粘性极限并给出收敛率；在初始数据附加低阶Besov假设下推导衰减估计。

Result: 证明了两类系统在临界Besov空间中平衡态附近强解的存在唯一性；获得对粘性参数一致的正则性估计；建立了全局消失粘性极限，收敛率为O(ε)；在额外低阶Besov假设下得到两系统的最优时间衰减估计，并给出相对速度与分布函数微观部分增强的衰减率。

Conclusion: 该工作为可压缩流体-粒子耦合系统在临界正则性下提供了统一的适定性、粘性极限与衰减分析框架，揭示了拖曳力耦合与Fokker-Planck扩散对解的长时间行为及相对速度、微观部分衰减的增强作用。

Abstract: We study multidimensional compressible fluid-particle systems at critical regularity, in which a carrier fluid and a particle phase with Fokker-Planck diffusion are coupled through a drag force. We prove the existence and uniqueness of strong solutions for the Cauchy problems of the Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck and Euler-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck systems near equilibrium in their respective critical Besov spaces. Moreover, we establish regularity estimates for the Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck system uniform with respect to the common viscosity parameter $μ=λ=\varepsilon$ and justify the global-in-time vanishing-viscosity limit with the convergence rate $\mathcal O(\varepsilon)$. Finally, under an additional lower-order Besov assumption on the initial data, we obtain optimal time-decay estimates for both systems and derive enhanced decay rates for the relative velocity and the microscopic part of the distribution function.

</details>


### [143] [Asymptotics of Laplace and Stokes solutions in the gap between a sharp tip and a smooth boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17377)
*Nicolas Roblet*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维Laplace与Stokes解在Lipschitz尖角与光滑边界间隙趋于消失时的渐近奇异行为，提出基于稳定性的方法构造间隙内解的领先阶渐近展开，并应用于确定Stokes阻力矩阵与压力场的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典润滑理论依赖较光滑边界间隙的各向异性实现降维，但尖角奇异性带来的各向同性标度要求不同方法，因此需要新的分析框架处理该极限问题。

Method: 引入基于稳定性的方法，构造二维Laplace与Stokes解在间隙内当Lipschitz尖角与光滑边界距离趋于零时的领先阶渐近展开。

Result: 得到了间隙内解的显式渐近奇异刻画，并确定了Stokes阻力矩阵与压力场的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该稳定性方法可有效处理尖角各向同性标度下的润滑极限，为相关Stokes流动阻力与压力渐近分析提供工具。

Abstract: We derive an explicit description of the asymptotic singular behavior of 2D Laplace and Stokes solutions as the distance between a Lipschitz sharp tip and a smooth boundary vanishes. While classical lubrication theory for smoother boundaries relies on the anisotropy of the gap to perform dimensional reduction, the isotropic scaling imposed by a corner singularity requires a different approach. We introduce a stability-based method that allows us to construct the leading-order asymptotics of these solutions in the gap. As an application, we determine the asymptotic behavior of the Stokes resistance matrix and the pressure field.

</details>


### [144] [Nonlocal intrinsic Harnack estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17400)
*Naian Liao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 将DiBenedetto的内蕴抛物Harnack估计推广到测量核的积分-微分方程，并建立内蕴表示型非局部估计与强极值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiBenedetto的内蕴抛物Harnack估计是非局部问题研究的核心工具，而带测量核、以分数阶p-Laplacian为模型的积分-微分方程缺乏对应的内蕴估计理论。

Method: 采用内蕴尺度（intrinsic scaling）框架，将经典抛物Harnack理论推广至具测量核的非局部积分-微分方程，建立表示型（representation type）非局部估计。

Result: 得到了该类方程的内蕴抛物Harnack估计，建立了内蕴的表示型非局部估计，并证明非负局部解满足强极值原理，即使在p>2时亦成立；类似结论对多孔介质型非局部方程同样适用。

Conclusion: 非局部问题在p>2时仍具备强极值原理，这一性质为局部问题所不具备，凸显了非局部结构与多孔介质型方程的共性。

Abstract: We extend DiBenedetto's intrinsic parabolic Harnack estimates to a class of integro-differential equations with measurable kernels modeled on the fractional $p$-Laplacian. More remarkably, we establish intrinsic, representation type nonlocal estimates and reveal that nonnegative local solutions abide by a strong maximum principle even for $p>2$, a property unique to nonlocal problems. Analogous results hold true for nonlocal equations of porous medium type.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Harmonicity and Existence of Algebraic Generalized Ricci Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16029)
*Huibin Chen,Zhiqi Chen,Fuhai Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究代数广义Ricci孤子的调和性与存在性，用Killing形式刻画调和挠率，构造新的广义nilsoliton，并给出谱与导子作用的障碍，最终确定filiform Lie代数 m₂(n) 存在广义nilsoliton当且仅当 5≤n≤8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ricci孤子在度量Lie代数上的调和性与存在性是代数Ricci流研究中的重要问题，尤其是经典nilsoliton不存在的代数上是否仍存在广义nilsoliton，以及如何判定其存在性，尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 通过涉及Killing形式的恒等式刻画任意度量Lie代数上代数广义Ricci孤子的调和挠率，证明Killing形式半正定可推出调和性而无须幺模假设；在不含经典nilsoliton的不可分解三步幂零Lie代数上构造带非零挠率的广义nilsoliton；对具有交换导代数及导子作用的幂零Lie代数给出障碍。

Result: Killing形式半正定性蕴含调和性；在维数7及维数6m+d（m>d≥1）上构造了带非零挠率且无经典nilsoliton的广义nilsoliton；得到基于谱及导子在中心商空间上作用的障碍；证明filiform Lie代数 m₂(n)（n≥5）存在广义nilsoliton当且仅当5≤n≤8。

Conclusion: 代数广义Ricci孤子的调和性可由Killing形式刻画，广义nilsoliton在更广泛的幂零Lie代数上存在，而多重障碍共同界定了filiform Lie代数 m₂(n) 的存在范围，即仅 5≤n≤8 时存在。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the harmonicity and existence of algebraic generalized Ricci solitons. Firstly, we characterize harmonic torsion of algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on arbitrary metric Lie algebras by an identity involving the Killing form. In particular, positive semidefiniteness of the Killing form implies harmonicity without a unimodularity assumption. Then, we construct generalized nilsolitons with nonzero torsion on indecomposable three-step nilpotent Lie algebras admitting no classical nilsoliton, in dimension seven and in dimensions $6m+d$ for $m>d\geq1$. Furthermore, we provide a spectral obstruction for nilpotent Lie algebras with abelian derived algebra and an obstruction based on the action of derivations on the quotient by the center. Combining these obstructions with explicit constructions, we prove that the filiform Lie algebra $\mathfrak m_2(n)$, $n\geq5$, admits a generalized nilsoliton if and only if $5\leq n\leq8$.

</details>


### [146] [Cyclic Riemannian nilmanifolds are naturally reductive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16457)
*Hui Wang,Zaili Yan,Shaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了循环黎曼幂零流形的结构，证明其为循环当且仅当李代数至多两步幂零且相关斜对称自同态族交换，并由此得出其自然约化性及等距群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究循环黎曼幂零流形这一具有左不变循环度量的幂零李群结构，刻画其度量与代数结构之间的关系，并为自然约化性及等距群的研究提供新的判据与结论。

Method: 通过李代数层面的分析，研究幂零李代数 $\mathfrak{n}$ 上的循环左不变度量，考察其与中心 $\mathfrak{z}$、斜对称自同态族 $J_{\mathfrak{z}}\subset so(\mathfrak{a})$ 的关系，进而建立刻画定理。

Result: 证明 $(N,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ 为循环的充要条件是 $\mathfrak{n}$ 至多两步幂零且 $J_{\mathfrak{z}}$ 为交换族；由此推出每个循环黎曼幂零流形均为自然约化的；并确定了无欧氏因子的单连通循环黎曼幂零流形的完整等距群。

Conclusion: 循环黎曼幂零流形与具有交换 $J_{\mathfrak{z}}$ 的至多两步幂零李代数相对应，天然具有自然约化性，且其等距群结构被完整确定。

Abstract: We study the structure of cyclic Riemannian nilmanifolds, that is, connected nilpotent Lie groups $N$ endowed with a left-invariant cyclic metric $\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle$. We prove that a Riemannian nilmanifold $(N,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ is cyclic if and only if its Lie algebra $\mathfrak{n}$ is at most two-step nilpotent and the associated family of skew-symmetric endomorphisms $J_{\mathfrak{z}}=\{J_Z : Z\in \mathfrak{z}\}\subset so(\mathfrak{a})$ is Abelian. As a direct consequence, every cyclic Riemannian nilmanifold is naturally reductive. We also determine the full isometry group of a connected and simply connected cyclic Riemannian nilmanifold without Euclidean factor.

</details>


### [147] [Decay estimates for Yang-Mills connections and Yang-Mills-Higgs pairs in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16655)
*Matheus Vieira*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维流形上Yang-Mills联络与Yang-Mills-Higgs对的无穷远衰减估计。作者建立了一个基于正调和函数的抽象衰减定理，并结合精细Kato不等式，证明自然的二次衰减假设可推出在渐近锥流形与乘积流形上更优的衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维流形上Yang-Mills型方程解的无穷远渐近行为（衰减率）是几何分析中的核心问题，但既有结果多依赖具体流形结构，缺乏统一框架。作者希望提供一个抽象工具，将衰减估计与流形的正调和函数性质解耦，从而推广到渐近锥与乘积流形等更一般的背景。

Method: 核心工具是流形上带合适正调和函数的抽象衰减定理，用以将解的衰减控制归结为调和函数的存在性与性质。随后结合精细Kato不等式（refined Kato inequalities）处理曲率张量的点态估计，将自然的二次衰减假设转化为更强的衰减率，应用于渐近锥流形与乘积流形。

Result: 证明了在渐近锥流形与乘积流形上，若Yang-Mills联络与Yang-Mills-Higgs对满足自然的二次衰减假设，则可获得改进的（更快的）衰减率。

Conclusion: 该抽象衰减定理为高维Yang-Mills型方程的渐近分析提供了统一框架，二次衰减假设足以诱导更优衰减，结果适用于渐近锥与乘积流形。

Abstract: We obtain decay estimates at infinity for Yang-Mills connections and Yang-Mills-Higgs pairs on higher-dimensional manifolds. The main tool is an abstract decay theorem for manifolds with suitable positive harmonic functions. Combining this theorem with refined Kato inequalities, we prove that a natural quadratic decay assumption implies an improved decay rate on certain asymptotically conical and product manifolds.

</details>


### [148] [A short proof of the homogeneity of an isoparametric hypersurface with $(g, m)=(6, 2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16658)
*Chao Qian,Zizhou Tang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为S^13中具有六个主曲率的等参超曲面均为齐性这一经典分类定理提供了一个简洁的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miyaoka于2013年以58页论文首次证明该定理，并于2016年附加15页勘误，证明冗长。本文旨在简化该证明。

Method: 通过结合有向向量丛Euler类的拓扑与几何两方面洞察进行论证。

Result: 给出了该分类定理的一个简洁证明。

Conclusion: 利用有向向量丛Euler类的拓扑-几何交互视角，可显著精简原本冗长的证明过程。

Abstract: A well-known hypersurface classification theorem states that any isoparametric hypersurface in $S^{13}$ with six principal curvatures is homogeneous. This landmark result was first proved in her Annals paper in 2013 by R. Miyaoka with 58 pages, and with errata in Annals in 2016 with 15 pages. The main purpose of this paper is to provide a concise proof of this theorem through an interplay between topological and geometric insights into the Euler class of an oriented vector bundle.

</details>


### [149] [Finite time singularities of the Ricci flow on compact Kähler surfaces are of Type I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16733)
*Charles Cifarelli,Ronan Conlon,Max Hallgren,Junsheng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明紧Kähler曲面上的Kähler-Ricci流若发生体积坍缩型有限时间奇点，则必满足Type I曲率界，并据此分类相应切流，从而得到紧Kähler曲面上任意有限时间奇点都是Type I型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler-Ricci流有限时间奇点的分类是几何分析核心问题之一，Type I与Type II奇点性质差异显著；此前体积坍缩情形的曲率增长速率与奇点类型尚不明确，需要补全紧Kähler曲面上奇点类型的完整刻画。

Method: 对紧Kähler曲面上Kähler-Ricci流的体积坍缩型有限时间奇点进行解析研究，建立其Type I曲率上界估计，并对抛物爆破极限所得的切流进行结构分类。

Result: 体积坍缩型有限时间奇点满足Type I曲率界，且其对应切流可被分类；结合既有结论后，紧Kähler曲面上Kähler-Ricci流的任意有限时间奇点均为Type I型。

Conclusion: 紧Kähler曲面上Kähler-Ricci流的有限时间奇点在类型上完全统一为Type I，解决了该情形下奇点分类的关键遗留问题。

Abstract: For any volume-collapsing finite-time singularity of a Kähler-Ricci flow on a compact Kähler surface, we show the flow satisfies a Type I curvature bound and classify the corresponding tangent flows. Combined with previous results, this shows that any finite-time singularity of a Kähler-Ricci flow on a compact Kähler surface is of Type I.

</details>


### [150] [On the Minkowski deficit for surfaces of revolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16792)
*Joaquim Bruna,Julià Cufí,Agustí Reventós*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过凸旋转曲面情形下的Minkowski不等式导出一般函数不等式，并建立Minkowski亏量与经线等周亏量之间的精细不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典Minkowski不等式4πA≤M²在凸旋转曲面这一特殊情形，旨在从函数不等式角度获得Minkowski不等式的新证明，并刻画曲面Minkowski亏量与经线等周亏量之间的定量关系。

Method: 以凸旋转曲面为对象，将Minkowski不等式转化为对一般函数成立的函数不等式，对该不等式进行陈述与证明，并借助它比较Minkowski亏量与经线等周亏量。

Result: 得到一个新的函数不等式；给出凸旋转曲面情形下Minkowski不等式的新证明；建立曲面Minkowski亏量与任意经线等周亏量之间的精细（sharp）不等式。

Conclusion: 经典Minkowski不等式可由更一般的函数不等式导出，且其亏量可被经线等周亏量精细控制，深化了对该不等式结构及稳定性的理解。

Abstract: By studying the classical Minkowski's inequality, $4πA\leq M^{2}$, relating area and mean curvature, in the particular case of convex surfaces of revolution, one is led to a functional inequality that holds for general functions. We state and prove this inequality obtaining a new proof of Minkowski's inequality in this particular case and we establish a sharp inequality between the Minkowski deficit of the surface and the isoperimetric deficit of any meridian curve.

</details>


### [151] [Inequalities for 4-Manifolds with Ends using Fibered boundary metric and Seiberg-Witten Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16806)
*Shyamal Kumar Hui,Chandan Kumar Adak*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用Hitchin-Thorpe不等式与Seiberg-Witten规范理论技巧，为非紧完备四维流形（无穷远处为纤维化几何）推导出一个新的不等式，并在边界纤维为单点的纤维边界度量情形下给出证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hitchin-Thorpe不等式是四维流形上联系曲率与拓扑不变量的经典结果，但其在非紧完备流形、尤其是无穷远处具有纤维化几何的流形上的推广尚不完善，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 综合运用Hitchin-Thorpe不等式与Seiberg-Witten规范理论技术；将无穷远处几何分为纤维边界度量（fibered boundary metric）与纤维尖点度量（fibered cusp metric）两类进行分析。

Result: 对无穷远处为纤维化几何的非紧完备四维流形推导出一个新的不等式；并在边界处纤维退化为单点的纤维边界度量情形下完成该不等式的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作将Hitchin-Thorpe型不等式拓展至非紧完备四维纤维化流形框架，其中单点纤维的纤维边界度量情形已得到严格证明，其余情形（如一般纤维及纤维尖点度量）尚待进一步研究。

Abstract: We deduce a new inequality using the Hitchin-Thorpe inequality for noncompact complete four-dimensional manifolds with fibered geometry at infinity and Seiberg-Witten gauge-theoretic techniques. Fibered geometry at infinity is either fibered boundary metric or fibered cusp metric. We prove it for fibered boundary metric when the fiber at boundary is a single point.

</details>


### [152] [Weighted volume monotonicity and isoperimetric comparison under a lower Ricci curvature bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17055)
*Jiewon Park,Keomkyo Seo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将 Bishop-Gromov 体积比较定理推广到带权情形：对 Ricci 曲率有下界的完备黎曼流形，在 h/f 单调不减假设下证明加权体积比关于半径单调不增，并刻画等号情形，给出若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Bishop-Gromov 定理刻画了 Ricci 曲率下界对流形体积增长的控制，但缺乏带权（加权）形式的体积比较。本文旨在建立加权版本的体积比较定理，以处理带权几何与相关分析问题。

Method: 设 p 为基点，f、h 为正径向权函数，考虑测地球的加权体积与常截面曲率 k 的单连通空间形式中的模型加权体积；在 h/f 非递减假设下，研究两者比值关于半径的单调性，并分析等号成立条件。

Result: 证明了在 h/f 非递减时加权体积比关于半径单调不增；等号成立当且仅当权比 h/f 为常数且相应测地球与模型球等距。

Conclusion: 由该单调性公式导出加权 Bishop-Gromov 型比较、环形比较不等式、加权体积倍增估计，以及加权面积-体积不等式和测地球的精确加权等周型比较。

Abstract: We establish a weighted extension of the Bishop-Gromov volume comparison theorem for complete Riemannian manifolds with Ricci curvature bounded below. Given a point $p$ and positive radial weights $f$ and $h$, we consider the weighted volume of a geodesic ball and a corresponding model weighted volume in the simply connected space form of constant sectional curvature $k$. We prove that, under the assumption that $h/f$ is nondecreasing, the ratio of these two weighted volumes is nonincreasing with respect to the radius. We also characterize the equality case, showing that equality forces the weight ratio to be constant and the corresponding geodesic ball to be isometric to the model ball. As applications, we derive weighted Bishop-Gromov-type comparisons, annular comparison inequalities, and weighted volume doubling estimates. We further apply the monotonicity formula to obtain weighted area-volume inequalities and sharp weighted isoperimetric-type comparisons for geodesic balls.

</details>


### [153] [Conformal structures and rigidity of complete stable minimal hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17078)
*Jooyeon Park,Keomkyo Seo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究多种曲率假设下完备稳定极小超曲面：非负数量曲率流形中不存在的共形刚性结果、紧致情形的Yamabe不变量非负性与等号刻画、截面曲率夹紧下的刚性及第一特征值上界，以及warped product中角度函数为正的二侧极小超曲面的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究曲率条件（数量曲率、截面曲率夹紧、warped product）如何限制完备稳定极小超曲面的共形类型、拓扑与谱性质，并统一刻画刚性现象。

Method: 综合利用共形几何与Yamabe不变量理论、稳定性不等式与Jacobi算子分析、第二基本形式作为Codazzi张量的条件、L^2范数条件，以及warped product中角度函数的符号与warping函数二阶导数条件。

Result: 在非负数量曲率(n+1)维完备定向流形中，完备定向非紧稳定极小超曲面不能共形等价于非正数量曲率n维流形中的有界区域；R^{n+1}中不存在共形等价于R^n有界区域的完备稳定极小超曲面；非负数量曲率流形中紧致稳定极小超曲面的光滑Yamabe不变量非负，并刻画等号情形；截面曲率夹紧且第二基本形式为Codazzi时得到L^2条件下的刚性及Laplacian第一特征值上界；warped product中角度函数为正且warping函数二阶导数非负时，完备非紧二侧极小超曲面稳定。

Conclusion: 曲率假设对稳定极小超曲面施加了强共形、谱与稳定性约束，得到一系列刚性与不存在性结论，并给出第一特征值上界与稳定性判据。

Abstract: We study complete stable minimal hypersurfaces in Riemannian manifolds under various curvature assumptions. In an $(n+1)$-dimensional complete oriented manifold with nonnegative scalar curvature, we prove that no complete oriented noncompact stable minimal hypersurface can be conformally equivalent to a bounded domain in an $n$-dimensional manifold with nonpositive scalar curvature. As a consequence, there exists no complete stable minimal hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ that is conformally equivalent to a bounded domain in $\mathbb{R}^n$. We also show that compact stable minimal hypersurfaces in manifolds with nonnegative scalar curvature have nonnegative smooth Yamabe invariant and we characterize the equality case. Next, we consider complete two-sided stable minimal hypersurfaces in ambient manifolds with pinched sectional curvature. Under an additional curvature condition that makes the second fundamental form a Codazzi tensor, we obtain a rigidity result under an $L^2$-condition on the second fundamental form and derive an upper bound for the first eigenvalue of the Laplacian. Finally, we establish that any complete noncompact two-sided minimal hypersurface immersed in a warped product manifold is stable, provided that the angle function is positive and the second derivative of the warping function is nonnegative.

</details>


### [154] [A proof of the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture for small volumes under a Ricci curvature lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17093)
*Marcos Agnoletto,Márcio Fabiano Da Silva,Stefano Nardulli,Reinaldo Resende*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在Ricci曲率下界与截面曲率上界条件下，于小体积区间证明了广义Cartan-Hadamard（Aubin）猜想，并将任意体积情形大幅归约到等号情形的刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard猜想刻画非正曲率流形的等周轮廓与模型空间的关系，长期是几何分析中的核心问题；作者希望在较弱曲率假设（仅Ricci下界）下取得进展，并推进一般体积情形。

Method: 在Cartan-Hadamard流形上假设Sec_g≤k̄≤0与Ric_g≥(n-1)k_，对该设定下的等周轮廓与曲率k̄的模型空间等周轮廓进行比较；通过小体积渐近分析与几何测度论工具建立不等式，并把任意体积问题归约到等号刚性。

Result: 对任意n≥2，证明了小体积范围内流形的等周轮廓不小于曲率为k̄的模型空间的等周轮廓，从而在小体积情形解决广义Cartan-Hadamard（Aubin）猜想；同时给出任意体积情形向等号情形刚性问题的实质性归约。

Conclusion: 在小体积范围内广义Cartan-Hadamard猜想成立；一般体积的完整解决被简化为验证等号情形刚性，为进一步研究提供了清晰路线。

Abstract: We prove the generalized Cartan-Hadamard conjecture, also known as the Aubin conjecture, in the small volume regime under a lower Ricci curvature bound, for any dimension $n\geq 2$. More precisely, we show that if $(M^n,g)$ is a Cartan-Hadamard manifold satisfying $\mathrm{Sec}_g\leq\bar k\leq 0$ and $\mathrm{Ric}_g\geq (n-1)\underline k$, then its isoperimetric profile is at least that of the model space of curvature $\bar k$ for small enough volumes.
  We also obtain a substantial reduction of the problem for arbitrary volumes to the rigidity of the equality case.

</details>


### [155] [Four-dimensional pseudo-umbilical biharmonic submanifolds in the six-dimensional Euclidean space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17136)
*Shun Maeta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了余维2、维数≥3的λ-双极小伪脐子流形在欧氏空间中具有常平均曲率，且当λ≥0时为极小，从而解决了四维双调和伪脐子流形的公开问题，并部分支持Chen猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dimitrić（1989）证明维数不为4的伪脐双调和子流形在欧氏空间中必为极小，但四维情形因双调和方程切向部分恒为零而长期悬而未决。本文旨在通过推广方程来解决这一近四十年的公开问题。

Method: 引入λ-双极小子流形概念，其定义方程推广了双调和方程的规范部分，并在余维2、维数≥3的伪脐子流形上分析该方程，分λ非负与一般情形讨论。

Result: 证明了欧氏空间中任何连通、余维2、维数≥3的λ-双极小伪脐子流形具有常平均曲率；当λ≥0时进一步为极小。由此推出六维欧氏空间中的四维伪脐双调和子流形必为极小。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了四维情形的空白，部分肯定地回答了Chen猜想，并为双调和子流形理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: In 1989, Dimitrić showed that any pseudo-umbilical biharmonic submanifold in a Euclidean space is minimal when the dimension of the submanifold is different from four. However, the four-dimensional case has remained open for nearly four decades. This difficulty comes from the fact that the tangential part of the biharmonic equation vanishes identically in dimension four. Therefore, we consider $λ$-biminimal submanifolds, whose defining equation generalizes the normal part of the biharmonic equation. In this paper, we show that any connected $λ$-biminimal pseudo-umbilical submanifold of codimension two and dimension at least three in a Euclidean space has constant mean curvature. Furthermore, when $λ$ is nonnegative, we prove that it is minimal. As a corollary, we show that any four-dimensional pseudo-umbilical biharmonic submanifold in the six-dimensional Euclidean space is minimal. This result also provides a partial affirmative answer to Chen's conjecture.

</details>


### [156] [Lorentzian Algebraic Ricci Solitons on the Heisenberg Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17183)
*Youssef Ayad*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在海森堡群 $H_{2n+1}$ 上存在三类不等价的左不变洛伦兹度量 $\mu,\nu,\phi$。文中证明：特定情形下 $\mu$ 是收缩的代数 Ricci 孤子；$\nu$ 仅在三维海森堡李代数 $\mathfrak{h}_3$ 上为收缩的代数 Ricci 孤子；$\phi$ 在 $\mathfrak{h}_{2n+1}$（$n>1$）上为稳态代数 Ricci 孤子，而当 $n=1$ 时 $\phi$ 是平坦的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究海森堡群/李代数上的左不变洛伦兹度量对应的代数 Ricci 孤子类型，以刻画这些度量的几何性质。

Method: 对海森堡李代数 $\mathfrak{h}_{2n+1}$ 上三类不等价洛伦兹内积 $\mu,\nu,\phi$ 分别分析其代数 Ricci 孤子方程，判定其孤子类型（收缩、稳态）及平坦性。

Result: $\mu$ 在特定情形下为收缩代数 Ricci 孤子；$\nu$ 仅在 $\mathfrak{h}_3$ 上为收缩代数 Ricci 孤子；$\phi$ 在 $\mathfrak{h}_{2n+1}$（$n>1$）上为稳态代数 Ricci 孤子，$n=1$ 时 $\phi$ 平坦。

Conclusion: 海森堡李代数上三类左不变洛伦兹度量对应不同类型的代数 Ricci 孤子，其性质依赖于维数，特别在 $n=1$ 与 $n>1$ 情形下 $\phi$ 的几何行为不同。

Abstract: There exist three nonequivalent left invariant Lorentzian metrics on the Heisenberg group $H_{2n+1}$, or equivalently, three nonequivalent Lorentzian inner products on the Heisenberg Lie algebra $\mathfrak{h}_{2n+1}$, denoted by $μ$, $ν$, and $φ$. We show that, in a specific case, $μ$ is an algebraic Ricci soliton that is shrinking. Moreover, $ν$ is an algebraic Ricci soliton only on the three-dimensional Heisenberg Lie algebra $\mathfrak{h}_3$ and it is shrinking. Finally, we show that $φ$ is a steady algebraic Ricci soliton on $\mathfrak{h}_{2n + 1}$ for $n > 1$. However, for $n = 1$, $φ$ is flat.

</details>


### [157] [Flag manifolds, spaces of frames, and connectedness properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17203)
*Augustin-Liviu Mare*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究满足 $FF^*={\rm Diag}(c_1,\ldots,c_k)$ 且各列范数平方为 $d_1,\ldots,d_n$ 的 $k\times n$ 矩阵构成的矩阵空间，在实数和复数域下分别判定其道路连通性与单连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类矩阵空间（与框架理论、相位恢复等相关的"框架"矩阵集合）的拓扑性质，如连通性与单连通性，对理解其结构与相关算法的连续形变具有基础意义，且与 Cahill–Mixon–Strawn、Needham–Shonkwiler 等一系列已有工作密切相关，需要系统刻画其拓扑连通性质。

Method: 对整数 $1\le k<n$ 及正实数 $c_i$、非负实数 $d_j$，考察由 $FF^*={\rm Diag}(c_1,\ldots,c_k)$ 与 $\|F_{\cdot j}\|^2=d_j$ 定义的 $k\times n$ 矩阵集合；分别就系数取自实数域或复数域讨论，利用旗流形（即 ${\rm O}(n)$ 与 ${\rm U}(n)$ 在对称实矩阵与 Hermitian 矩阵空间上共轭作用的轨道）作为核心工具，给出判定空间道路连通及单连通性的准则。

Result: 给出了判定该矩阵空间是否道路连通、以及相对于全体 $k\times n$ 矩阵的子空间拓扑是否单连通的准则，这些准则与 Cahill–Mixon–Strawn（2017）、Needham–Shonkwiler（2021）、Caine 与后两位作者（2026）及作者本人（2024、2026）的结果密切相关，并借助旗流形结构加以刻画。

Conclusion: 该矩阵空间的拓扑连通性质可通过旗流形统一处理，所得准则推广并联系了已有文献结果，为实/复域下框架矩阵集合的拓扑分类提供了系统框架。

Abstract: For integers $1\le k < n$ and real numbers $c_1, \ldots, c_k>0$ and $d_1, \ldots, d_n\ge 0$, we investigate the space of all $k\times n$ matrices $F$ such that the product $FF^*$ is equal to the diagonal matrix ${\rm Diag}(c_1, \ldots, c_k)$ and the squared norms of the columns of $F$ are equal to $d_1, \ldots, d_n$ respectively. Depending on the field where the coefficients of $F$ are taken from, which can be of real or of complex numbers, we are mainly interested in determining whether the resulting space is path-connected or even simply connected relative to the subspace topology in the space of all $k \times n$ matrices. The criteria presented are closely related to results previously obtained by Cahill, Mixon, and Strawn (2017), Needham and Shonkwiler (2021), the last two authors together with Caine (2026), and the author of this work (2024 and 2026). Flag manifolds, namely orbits of the canonical conjugation actions of ${\rm O}(n)$ and ${\rm U}(n)$ on the spaces of symmetric real and Hermitian $n\times n$ matrices, respectively, play a central role in our development.

</details>


### [158] [Laplacian Algebras and Subcalibrations for Minimal Hypercones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17217)
*Hongbin Cui*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于Laplacian代数发展了一套代数机制，用于构造极小超锥并判定其变分性质，将次校准构造与定量散度估计归结为代数不等式，并给出面积最小化、严格面积最小化与严格稳定性的判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造极小超锥并确定其变分性质（如面积最小化、稳定性）通常需要复杂的几何分析。作者希望建立统一的代数框架，通过闭包恒等式同时刻画极小零点集，从而简化次校准构造与散度估计，并允许处理非孤立奇点。

Method: 以Laplacian代数为基础建立代数机制，利用相同的闭包恒等式识别极小零点集，将次校准构造和定量散度估计约化为代数不等式；结合比较定理与Jacobi恒等式，从所得证书导出面积最小化、严格面积最小化和严格稳定性的判据；并借助可复现的精确符号计算验证代数恒等式与证书。

Result: 给出了面积最小化等参数超锥的多项式次校准；在FKM分裂四次族与Clifford三次族内完成了极小化分类；证明了所有阶数至少为2的复行列式实部零点集的严格面积最小性；建立了梯度平方闭包下的奇次刚性以及次校准的拉回判据。

Conclusion: 该代数机制与证书为极小超锥的变分性质提供了统一、可计算且允许非孤立奇点的判定工具，并通过精确符号计算得到支持。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic mechanism based on Laplacian algebras for constructing minimal hypercones and determining their variational properties. The same closure identities identify minimal zero sets and reduce subcalibration construction and quantitative divergence estimates to algebraic inequalities. Combined with comparison theorems and Jacobi identities, the resulting certificates give criteria for area minimization, strict area minimization, and strict stability, allowing nonisolated singularities. Applications include polynomial subcalibrations for area-minimizing isoparametric hypercones, minimizing classifications within the FKM splitting quartic and Clifford cubic families, and strict area minimization for the zero sets of the real parts of complex determinants of every order at least two. We also establish odd-degree rigidity under gradient-square closure and a pullback criterion for subcalibrations. The algebraic identities and certificates are supported by reproducible exact symbolic computations.

</details>


### [159] [Fixed Point Theorems in Quaternionic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17340)
*Niklas Henningsen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 将Atiyah-Bott不动点定理应用于四元数几何：建立四元数流形Salamon复形与twistor空间上全纯向量丛Dolbeault复形的联系，给出两类复形的不动点公式并应用于Wolf空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Atiyah-Bott不动点定理在复几何中已十分成熟，但其在四元数几何（四元数流形及twistor空间）中的应用尚待系统化。作者希望借助Salamon复形与twistor空间Dolbeault复形的对应关系，将不动点理论推广到四元数情形，并明确适用映射类型。

Method: 1) 综述一般四元数流形的Salamon复形与twistor空间上某全纯向量丛的Dolbeault复形之间的关系；2) 讨论哪类映射适合应用Atiyah-Bott不动点定理；3) 由此分别对两种复形建立不动点公式。

Result: 得到Salamon复形与twistor空间Dolbeault复形两者的不动点公式，并将该公式应用于Wolf空间。

Conclusion: Atiyah-Bott不动点定理可有效推广至四元数几何；通过Salamon复形与twistor空间Dolbeault复形的联系，可统一处理两类复形的不动点问题，并在Wolf空间上获得具体结果。

Abstract: We consider an application of the Atiyah-Bott fixed point theorem in quaternionic geometry. We review the relation of the general Salamon complex of a quaternionic manifold and the Dolbeault complex with coefficients in a certain holomorphic vector bundle of its twistor space. Furthermore, we investigate which maps are suitable for the application of the fixed point theorem. As a result, we obtain a fixed point formula for both complexes and apply it to Wolf spaces.

</details>


### [160] [Compact Proof of the Positivity of Quasi-Local Masses for a class of Initial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17361)
*Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对Wang--Yau质量建立了一个纯准局域正性定理：在Jang形变经保边界共形约化到零数量曲率后落入严格凸Euclid填充的充分小TT扰动邻域的数据类上，Wang--Yau能量化为标量平坦紧度量的Brown--York质量加非负体项，并通过在Euclid度量处沿TT方向计算Brown--York泛函的二阶变差证明严格正性。证明完全在紧填充内完成，不使用渐近平坦延拓或正质量定理，部分回答了Schoen关于准局域质量正性的真正准局域证明问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R. Schoen提出：能否给出准局域质量正性的真正准局域证明？既有证明多依赖渐近平坦延拓或正质量定理等非准局域工具，本文旨在为Wang--Yau质量提供完全局限在紧填充内的正性证明。

Method: 对一类初始数据，先作保边界的共形约化使Jang形变具有零数量曲率，并要求其位于严格凸Euclid填充的充分小横截无迹（TT）扰动邻域内；显式构造可实现该TT生成扇区的物理初始数据类；将Wang--Yau能量约化为所得标量平坦紧度量的Brown--York质量加由Jang形变决定的非负体项；再在Euclid度量处沿TT方向计算Brown--York泛函的二阶变差以证严格正性。

Result: 在所研究的数据类上证明了Wang--Yau质量的严格正性；显式构造了一类非平凡的物理初始数据，其容许Jang约化实现该TT生成扇区；证明仅使用紧填充内的计算，不依赖渐近平坦延拓与正质量定理。

Conclusion: 给出了Wang--Yau质量正性的一个纯准局域证明框架，部分回答了Schoen关于准局域质量正性真正准局域证明的问题。

Abstract: We prove a purely quasi-local positivity theorem for the Wang--Yau mass for a class of initial data whose Jang deformation, after a boundary-preserving conformal reduction to zero scalar curvature, lies in a sufficiently small transverse--traceless (TT) perturbative neighborhood of a strictly convex Euclidean fill-in.We explicitly construct a nontrivial class of physical initial data whose admissible Jang reductions realize this TT-generated sector. The argument reduces the Wang--Yau energy to the Brown--York mass of the resulting scalar-flat compact metric, together with nonnegative bulk terms determined by the Jang deformation, and establishes strict positivity by computing the second variation of the Brown--York functional at the Euclidean metric in transverse--traceless directions. The proof is entirely confined to the compact fill-in and uses neither an asymptotically flat extension nor the positive mass theorem. This gives a partial answer to a question of R. Schoen concerning a genuinely quasi-local proof of positivity for quasi-local mass.

</details>


### [161] [Milnor fibrations on rho-tubes and rho-spheres for real analytic map germs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17374)
*Maico Ribeiro,Mateus de Melo,Gabriel Santos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究用解析控制函数 ρ 替换欧氏距离平方定义 Milnor 纤维化，给出 Milnor 集与条件 (b) 及管纤维化、球面纤维化的判据，并说明纤维化对 ρ 的依赖；例子 $G_{\rho}=(xz,\,yz\rho)$ 表明在椭圆控制下正则性可发生明确变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Milnor 纤维化通常以欧氏距离平方球面为基准，本文希望探讨当用更一般的解析控制函数 ρ 定义原点时，管纤维化与球面纤维化的存在性与结构如何改变，从而揭示控制函数在局部纤维化结构中的本质作用。

Method: 对实解析映射芽 $G:(\mathbb{R}^m,0)\to(\mathbb{R}^p,0)$，用解析控制函数 ρ 替代欧氏距离平方，定义相应的 Milnor 集与条件 (b)，建立管纤维化与球面纤维化的判据，并分析纤维化对 ρ 的依赖性；通过族 $G_{\rho}=(xz,\,yz\rho)$ 进行具体计算与比较。

Result: 给出基于控制函数 ρ 的 Milnor 集、条件 (b) 及管纤维化、球面纤维化判据，并刻画纤维化对 ρ 的依赖。族 $G_{\rho}=(xz,\,yz\rho)$ 在椭圆控制下呈现正则性的明确变化。特别地，$G(x,y,z)=(xz,\,yz(10x^2+y^2+3z^2))$ 在欧氏控制下既无诱导管纤维化也无诱导球面纤维化，而对适配函数 $\rho=10x^2+y^2+3z^2$ 两种纤维化均存在。

Conclusion: 控制函数 ρ 可以决定局部纤维化结构：同一映射芽在不同控制函数下可能具备或不具备 Milnor 管纤维化与球面纤维化，因此 ρ 的选择对局部纤维化理论具有本质意义。

Abstract: For a real analytic map germ $G:(\mathbb{R}^m,0)\to(\mathbb{R}^p,0)$, we study Milnor fibrations obtained by replacing the squared Euclidean distance with an analytic control function $ρ$ defining the origin. We formulate the corresponding Milnor set and condition (b), derive criteria for tube and sphere fibrations, and examine their dependence on $ρ$. The family $G_ρ=(xz,\,yzρ)$ provides explicit changes of regularity under elliptic controls. In particular, the germ $G(x,y,z)=(xz,\,yz(10x^2+y^2+3z^2))$ admits neither the induced tube nor sphere fibration for the Euclidean control, while both fibrations exist for the adapted function $ρ=10x^2+y^2+3z^2$. Thus the control function can be essential to the local fibration structure.

</details>


### [162] [Uniqueness of free boundary minimal annuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17512)
*Davide Parise,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明单位球内光滑真嵌入自由边界极小环面与临界悬链面全等，并分类球面环域上的特定边值问题及球冠情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小曲面与自由边界问题的刚性（唯一性）结果是几何分析的核心问题之一；单位球内自由边界极小环面的分类长期未决，本文旨在给出完全分类。

Method: 利用极小曲面的刚性方法，并结合相应椭圆型方程（(Δ_{S^2}+2)u=0）正解的分类，借助一阶齐次延拓与Alt-Caffarelli锥的联系来刻画边界条件（u=0, |∇u|=1）。

Result: （1）单位球中每个光滑真嵌入的自由边界极小环面均与临界悬链面全等；（2）分类了光滑球面环域上(Δ_{S^2}+2)u=0且边界满足u=0、|∇u|=1的正解，其一阶齐次延拓恰为轴对称Alt-Caffarelli锥；（3）进一步处理了球冠中自由边界极小环面的情形。

Conclusion: 给出了单位球（及球冠）内自由边界极小环面的刚性分类，并建立了其与轴对称Alt-Caffarelli锥及球面环域上方程正解之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth properly embedded free-boundary minimal annulus in the unit ball is congruent to the critical catenoid. We also classify positive solutions of $(Δ_{\mathbb{S}^2}+2)u=0$ on smooth spherical annuli with $u=0$ and $|\nabla u|=1$ on the boundary; their one-homogeneous extensions are the axially symmetric Alt-Caffarelli cones. Lastly, we also treat the case of free-boundary minimal annuli in spherical caps.

</details>
